### 1.5 The Changes and the Chinese sources

**In words.** The 周易's sixty-four configurations are a world the spine states with no premise, and its laws are instances of §1.2: the law of waxing and waning (消息) merges nothing, so every configuration returns, while a law that admits only a broken line merges and draws everything into 坤 (`every_returns_iff`); complements are a lumping the law goes alike across (`pairs_goAlike`), and the waning into 坤 is the law that breaks that symmetry (`Hexagram.wane_breaks`). The relations of the places are readings in context that glue to the whole configuration only where every site agrees (`halves_glues_iff`); the received order is the run of a table and of no symmetric rule (`runsOrder_not_commutes`). 248 passages of the Chinese sources are tied to the declarations they correspond to (95 agreements, 94 exact scopes, 59 divergences), with no Lean added.

*Readings* [AI reading, D47, D50, D51, D53, D54, D55]. 无往不復 holds exactly where a law merges nothing; 窮則變 is not a law; 生生之謂易 is a scope, since change is wider than 生生; 繼之者善 and the ranks of 天尊地卑 diverge, the dynamics determining no significance and the configurations carrying no rank. The relational start holds across the Chinese lines, their universal forms mostly need a scope, and four non-relational overlays (a prior ground, a nature fixed before relations, a will or single centre, a contest or rank) are each answered inside their tradition. The passage maps and their censuses are in [YI-ZHUAN-AND-BECOMING](YI-ZHUAN-AND-BECOMING.md) and [CHINESE-SOURCES-AND-BECOMING](CHINESE-SOURCES-AND-BECOMING.md).

Records: [hexagrams-on-the-spine](../results/records/hexagrams-on-the-spine.md), [place-relations-in-context](../results/records/place-relations-in-context.md), [hexagram-world-laws](../results/records/hexagram-world-laws.md), [fixed-places-and-sole-lines](../results/records/fixed-places-and-sole-lines.md), [hexagram-arrangements-and-laws](../results/records/hexagram-arrangements-and-laws.md), [chinese-sources-on-the-spine](../results/records/chinese-sources-on-the-spine.md).

The 九句 of the earlier repository divide motion into what comes back (归, which gathers in and goes out) and what does not (不归, which continues and generates). The spine has three cases [AI reading, D49]: return without loss (`Law.Lossless.returns`), which has 不息 without 生生; being drawn in (`Law.Drawn.not_returns`, `Law.drawn_iff_lost`), the 收 of the third sentence, which is 不归; and never coming round (`unheld_of_new`, `more_needs_open`), which only an open world allows and which alone can be 生. A configuration the law leaves unchanged returns at every step: it is rest (静), what holds at once, not 熄, where exchange ends (停; D45). The text of the 九句 is in [the earlier repository's README](../../../sources/07-shengsheng-buxi/ssbx-repo/README.md); closing and opening are crosswalked in §10.

<a id="2-组织更新与演化--organization-renewal-and-evolution"></a>
## 2. Organization, renewal and evolution

### 2.1 Renewal and consumption belong to runs

**In words.** A formation renews where every condition it depends on or draws on keeps supporting every formation that depends on it, and consumes where it draws on a condition from which some dependent loses support for good. The two exclude each other without exhausting the cases, and the class belongs to the run, not to a configuration or to the formation's own course: persistence, success and growth do not certify renewal.

- `falls_not_recurring`: an amount counted in whole units that never rises from some step on cannot keep falling. It is the whole-number instance of the least-loss law (`Amounts.rests`, §1.3); over dense amounts a halving stock falls for ever (`Halving.approaches_without_reaching`). Depletion with no run, recurrent renewal by a rank, and drawing without renewal are its cases. Used in §4.3 (constraint 1).
- `Renewable.class_belongs_to_the_run`: in one process, from one start, the focal formation is active at every step; in one run it renews, in another it consumes the sustaining formation's condition and that formation ends. The earlier module outside the spine has the same point (`own_trajectory_does_not_decide`). Used in §4.3.
- *No finite history decides the class, in `Renewable`*: for every horizon two runs agree before it, and after it one renews and the other consumes, so a reading decided by finite prefixes that counts every renewing run counts a consuming one (`whole_run_includes_overdraw`); a reading by amount, taking no more than is renewed, is decided by finite histories and withholds every overdraw (`amount_reading`). This is shown for this process, not for every process; what finite stretches decide in every process is §3.2.
- `jointly_consuming`: two formations each within renewal together consume a shared condition and both end; the class belongs to their joint relation. Used in §4.1.

The stepwise reading [AI reading, D10] reads an event as within renewal where its step keeps every dependence it takes part in (`RenewingAt`), and reads `living` stepwise as "active and not undermining", undermining being drawing that leaves a condition below a need once met in the dependence's unbroken tenure (`MetAbove`). It differs from the whole-run reading both ways (an escalation, a brief dip), withholds drawing after a loss, and has a cost: `draws` carries no amount, so drawing exactly the renewed amount is withheld too; its witnesses are in [becoming-stepwise-reading](../results/records/becoming-stepwise-reading.md). Return grades renewal: kept, returning within a period, returning without bound, never returning; consumption is exactly some dependence never returning (`consumed_iff`, unfolding the definition). In a family of laws where renewal depends on the renewed condition, a loss beyond the threshold does not come back (`no_return_below_support`); a supported route of contribution can repair the relation (periphery); a consumer that substitutes continues by transforming its dependence and is still consuming, the sustaining formation ending (`stepwise_includes_substitution`); in one class of wear laws the two classes are dynamically distinguishable (periphery).

Records: [becoming-renewal-reading](../results/records/becoming-renewal-reading.md), [relational-renewal-classification](../results/records/relational-renewal-classification.md), [becoming-stepwise-reading](../results/records/becoming-stepwise-reading.md), [becoming-return-grades](../results/records/becoming-return-grades.md), [becoming-finite-horizon](../results/records/becoming-finite-horizon.md), [supported-renewal-recovery](../results/records/supported-renewal-recovery.md), [substitution-preserves-consumption](../results/records/substitution-preserves-consumption.md), [renewing-consuming-formation](../results/records/renewing-consuming-formation.md), [becoming-dependence-dynamics](../results/records/becoming-dependence-dynamics.md).

### 2.2 Continued generation, more, and the two roles of one relatum

**In words.** Unceasing arising needs renewal, and more without end needs replenishment with no eventual bound, given by formations within the unfolding. A formation is a condition of others and of itself, so generation is itself replenishment: an embodied relatum arises only as another gives to it. Under dissipation no finite part bears recurring uptake on its own.

- *Continued generation* (`Becoming.Arising`): a formation arises across a step when it is inactive before and active after it. In a balanced flow, positive uptake at arbitrarily late steps rules out replenishment being zero from some step on (`uptake_needs_replenishment`); where an amount never rises and every arising lowers it, generation is not unceasing (`dissipation_arising_not_recurring`), while a closed cycle with a constant total sustains it (`Recycle.closed_unceasing`). Unceasing arising is strictly stronger than recurring renewal: a tide the moon renews rises with nothing arising (`Tide.renewal_without_arising`).
- *More* (`Becoming.Flow`, `Becoming.Population`): read as "for every number, from some step on at least that many formations take", more rules out any eventual bound on replenishment (`More.replenishment_unbounded`) and on renewers where each gives a bounded amount (`More.renewers_unbounded`); the renewers may be absent at infinitely many steps (`Pulse.pulsed`). Under a census of active formations, more forces unceasing arising (`More.unceasing`); without the census, with repetition or with takers never active it does not (`Dormant`, `Repeated`, `Inactive`). The replenishment needed forms a strict hierarchy, and the renewers can be the takers themselves (`Cycle.closed_more`).
- *No finite part bears taking* (`Dissipative.opens`): under dissipation a finite family on which taking recurs is not closed from any step on, and enclosed around it draws in (`taking_draws_in`); with finitely many conditions in all, taking does not recur. In one exchange a rise comes with a fall at the same step (without dissipation, `Brood.rise_without_fall`). Where no step is closed dissipation is vacuous and a renewer gives while taking nothing (`Spray`); the opening exchange may be unrelated to the family (`SprayShuttle`). Used in §2.3 and §4.3.
- *Arising is given* (`Embodied.arises_given`): in a balanced flow where only active formations give, an embodied relatum arises only as an active formation gives to it, possibly a source depending on nothing (`SourceSeed`, `EmbodiedSource`); so generation is replenishment, and where embodied generation recurs in a finite family the family is not closed from any step on (`embodiedArising_opens`, `embodiedArising_draws_in`, `embodiedArising_not_recurring`). Without dissipation (`Shuttle`) or embodiment (`Flicker`) these fail; burning fresh fuel meets every premise (`Throughput`). Which condition is which formation is supplied by the shared index (`relabel`).

The requirement follows the rise of level, not activity: a seed can be given its level while dormant and arise later with nothing given (`Provisioned`), and waking is not coming to stand (`Sleeper`); a reserve defers the end without escaping it (`Becoming.Reserve`). A bee and a flower each depending on itself and the other are connected with no third condition, while where each depends only on the other neither valuation relates them (`Mutualism.apart_embodied`). Drawing on an embodied relatum at the step it ends is breaking, whatever ends it (`Embodied.ending_breaks`, `Predation`, `Wilting`): a verdict of the stepwise reading, not a valuation of the drawer, since comparison is by possibilities [AI reading, D6].

*Continuity across dormancy and identity.* Given a reading of holding, `Web.persisting` reads activity by it, and the class of a formation that holds throughout does not change (`Web.steady_held`, `Web.renewingIn_held`, `Web.consumingIn_held`); succession at a step where nothing holds is not constrained (`Dormancy.held_both`), and both ends of a census must read holding alike (`Web.persisting_census`). A re-identification keeps balance exactly where what it continues lies within the conservation relation (`World.balanced_reidentify_iff`), and with equality decided `World.fixed_iff`, `World.arises_fixed_iff` and `World.ceases_fixed_iff` give when identity, arising and ceasing are fixed under every naming keeping balance; balance can admit different arisings at once (`Lineage.world_readings`, `Hibernation.world_readings`). Over a run the condition for more to force arising is exact: under a census at every step, every continuation without a split leaves, at arbitrarily late steps, some formation after a step continuing none before it exactly when more are active after a step than before at arbitrarily late steps (`ListedWebs.forcedAlong_iff_more`), and a continuation without a split from some step on suffices (`ListedWebs.forcedAlong_of_eventually_noSplit`). Read along a continuation rather than a name, renewing passes into every branch of a split and consuming back into every formation merged (`Web.renewingAlong_iff`, `Web.consumingAlong_iff`); these classes are fixed by the continuation under every re-identification (`Web.renewingAlong_reidentify`, `Web.consumingAlong_reidentify`), are the name classes where each formation continues itself (`Web.renewingAlong_itself_iff`), and a merge can make a formation that renews as named consume along it (`MergeClass.readings`). Tenure and undermining read along a continuation are the named ones where each formation continues itself, are unchanged by every re-identification carrying it, and more links never remove undermining (`Web.underminingAlong_itself_iff`, `Web.underminingAlong_reidentify`, `Web.underminingAlong_mono`). Where two relata stand alike after a step of a balanced world, a balanced naming continues the one as the other, so no record of the world decides between them (`World.continues_as_alike`). Not proved: sufficient conditions for a mixed census.

*Readings* [AI reading, D3, D4, D6]. The second 生 of “不断创造更多生” (“unceasingly creating more 生”, Ren's words) is read with the scope of what can form; only its “unceasing” is stated here. Embodied relata are read as 生, so generation giving rise to generation is one formal content of the relation between the two 生. More uptake needs replenishment given within the unfolding, borne by an unfolding that goes beyond every finite part; sustaining a single taker already is (`Lone`). Rises in possibility, diversity and complexity are other readings of “more” and are stated through widening (§6.1). The reading of holding and conservation as continuation are the AI's.

Records: [unceasing-generation-needs-renewal](../results/records/unceasing-generation-needs-renewal.md), [more-needs-renewers](../results/records/more-needs-renewers.md), [no-finite-part-bears-taking](../results/records/no-finite-part-bears-taking.md), [rising-and-falling-in-one-exchange](../results/records/rising-and-falling-in-one-exchange.md), [life-as-condition-of-life](../results/records/life-as-condition-of-life.md), [generation-is-replenishment](../results/records/generation-is-replenishment.md), [reserve-defers-ending](../results/records/reserve-defers-ending.md), [identity-across-steps](../results/records/identity-across-steps.md), [classes-along-a-continuation](../results/records/classes-along-a-continuation.md), [census-balance-under-continuation](../results/records/census-balance-under-continuation.md), [living-on-and-descent](../results/records/living-on-and-descent.md), [whole-persists-through-generation](../results/records/whole-persists-through-generation.md), [where-novelty-gain-comes-from](../results/records/where-novelty-gain-comes-from.md), [returning-novelty-forgetting-on-levels](../results/records/returning-novelty-forgetting-on-levels.md), [entropy-history-and-record](../results/records/entropy-history-and-record.md).

### 2.3 Joint excess, taking all, and what is fed from beyond

**In words.** What a family takes without end it is fed from beyond; draws each within what a step gives can together exceed it, and a joint excess that brings a level below the renewers' need ends them and everything depending on the condition at that need. A whole of parts that each end, whose parts arise only where they depend on such a condition, ends with it, whoever draws. Taking all that is given excludes every other taker, and a take that rises at every step exhausts what it takes from and ends.

- `Flow.fed_from_beyond`: under balance and dissipation a finite family, listed once, from which taking recurs and which is enclosed around from some step on, cannot from any step on be fed from nothing beyond it. It needs natural-number amounts; with real amounts losses can shrink geometrically and it fails. What is needed is being fed, not the recurrence of a source (`Lone.fed_not_recurring`). Used in §4.3 (a collective fed from beyond depends on what lies beyond).
- `Flow.excess_bounded`, `Flow.joint_excess_ends`: each step of joint excess lowers the level by at least one unit, so it cannot outlast what was held; where renewal is given only by formations depending on what they renew, a joint excess bringing the level below their need ends them and every formation depending at such a need (`Exceeding.all_end`; with one taker all continue, `Alone.all_continue`). Own draws within what is given can jointly exceed it (`Pair.own_not_joint`, `Exceeding.own_not_joint`). Used in §4.3 (relative position does not protect).
- `Web.Whole.family_excess_ends`: a whole that draws its parts' arising beyond its renewal ends. Read on one world with a flow, a whole (`Web.Whole`, active exactly while some part is, §2.2) of parts listed at each step and each ending, whose parts arise only where they then depend on some condition of a family at a need of at least `m`, the family's renewal gated on it, has no part arising after `n` steps of excess of the family's uptake over its replenishment from a stock below `n + m`, and ends; the family of one condition is `Web.Whole.joint_excess_ends`. Below it, listed parts each ending, none arising from some step on, end together with their whole (`Web.Listed.end_without_arising`, `Web.Whole.ends_without_arising`), of which `Whole.needs_arising` is the corollary. The uptake is the joint draw: the whole need not be a taker, and its own draw within what is renewed proves nothing. The parts need not depend on the family once arisen; the whole ends once those arisen have ended. Every premise holds with a whole active for three steps (`WholeExceeding.drawn`), and the excess, ending parts and the arising's dependence are each needed (`WholeWithin.kept`, `WholeUnending.persists`, `WholeUngated.persists`). Read with a collective as the whole of its lives and their renewal as the family, this is OPENING's "one that consumes them consumes what it goes on through" under named premises [AI reading; [study §287](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#287-a-whole-that-draws-its-parts-arising-beyond-its-renewal-ends-we1)].
- `RegimeMargin.ends_iff_below`: the margin of a regime. The margin law (§6.1) compares levels with the rest's exchanges held as they were, and on measured data that comparison holds as an identity ([MQ1, study §253](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#253-the-margin-as-a-measured-quantity-mq1)); what a measurement tests is the margin with the condition's own giving responding to its level. With giving read off what a standing draw `H` leaves (`RegimeMargin.step`), a level below `H + y`, where giving falls short of the draw below `y`, is gone within as many steps as it stood (`RegimeMargin.ends_below`), and from `H + y` up to a top the step keeps, the level holds (`RegimeMargin.holds`): the point of no return is exact. Where giving stops below a need, that point lies at least the draw above the need, and more exactly where giving at the need falls short of the draw (`RegimeMargin.beyond_draw_iff`), and it rises with the draw where giving does not rise (`RegimeMargin.rises_by_draw`): the need is the regime's, as MQ1 measured in the simulated yeast (1.4–4.1 times the draw above the need). On real yeast the giving law is itself the regime's: at equal density giving falls as the dilution rises ([MQ2, study §266](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#266-the-margins-equation-of-state-against-held-out-regimes-mq2), post hoc), and giving lowered by the regime raises the need beyond what removal alone gives, strictly exactly where the lowered giving falls short of the draw at the old need (`RegimeMargin.lowered_raises_strictly`). Draws add, harms do not: where giving under the joint draw is subadditive above the need and no greater than under either draw, the rise of the point of no return under two draws together is at least the sum of the rises under each, less one whole unit (`RegimeMargin.harms_superadditive`), and two draws the condition holds under alone can together end it from every level (`RegimeMargin.Peaked.together_ends`). The time to break lies between `RegimeMargin.not_before` and `RegimeMargin.falls_within`, MQ1's band. Harvesting theory states the same structure over the reals (depensation and the fold of the harvested equilibrium: Clark 1976; May 1977); the module states it in whole numbers ([margin of a regime](../results/records/margin-of-a-regime.md)). Where giving adjusts, reading what is left and the recent giving (`RegimeMargin.Adjusted.step`), the regime's step is the case reading what is left alone (`RegimeMargin.Adjusted.orbit_regime`). Every rest of the state at or above the draw is the regime's wherever the adjustment rests at the draw exactly where the regime gives the draw, on the run as on the step (`RegimeMargin.Adjusted.rests_iff`), so for every giving between the recent and the regime's that moves wherever it differs from the regime's (`RegimeMargin.Adjusted.Between.rests_iff`). The point decides the fate where the recent giving lies on the level's side of the draw (`RegimeMargin.Adjusted.ends_iff_below`); with it on the other side, what was given before carries a condition across the point both ways (`RegimeMargin.Crossing.crosses`; RM2, [study §299](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#299-the-regimes-point-over-every-adjustment-of-giving-rm2), [the regime's point under giving that adjusts](../results/records/regime-point-under-adjusting-giving.md)). Runs come to rest where the adjustment rises with what is left and with the recent giving and a band keeps the state: every run within a band up to `hi` is still within `3 hi + 3` steps, at the draw with a level the regime's step leaves as it is, or below the draw with level and recent giving equal, which is nothing where nothing gives from nothing (`RegimeMargin.Adjusted.comes_to_rest`). Whether a run changes again is decided at each step, since a law's run with a bounded mark comes to rest within the bound (`LastChange.still_within`), so settling needs no omniscience here. Giving that falls as what is left rises can keep a run moving within its band for ever (`RegimeMargin.Alternation.alternates`), as overcompensating renewal cycles (May 1976) (RM3, [study §300](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#300-where-a-run-of-a-regime-comes-to-rest-rm3), [where a run of a regime comes to rest](../results/records/where-a-run-of-a-regime-comes-to-rest.md)). Where the adjustment rises with what is left and with the recent giving, the point of no return is a curve in the plane of level and recent giving. A run has at most one rest (`LastChange.still_same`), and a state at or below one whose run rests below the draw rests below it too, so within a band up to `hi`, for each recent giving, the runs resting below the draw are exactly those from levels below a least level, found by a finite search with no omniscience (`RegimeMargin.Adjusted.falls_iff_below`); it does not rise as the recent giving rises (`RegimeMargin.Adjusted.threshold_falls`), and under the regime's step it is the point `H + y` at every recent giving (`RegimeMargin.threshold_regime`). Without giving rising with what is left, a run from a level can come to rest below the draw while one from a lower level holds, and no such level exists (`RegimeMargin.Notch.no_threshold`) (RM4, [study §301](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#301-the-threshold-in-the-plane-of-level-and-recent-giving-rm4), [the threshold in the plane of level and recent giving](../results/records/threshold-in-the-state-plane.md)). None of this needs amounts to be counted. In any linearly ordered amounts with a draw taken first the step of a rising adjustment keeps the order of states, and from the first step on a step that neither raises nor lowers both climbs or sinks toward the draw, whatever the adjustment, the next such step of the same kind, while a step raising or lowering both is followed by such steps for ever (`RegimeMargin.Drawn.run_shape`); the rests are the regime's point or below the draw with level and recent giving equal (`RegimeMargin.Drawn.still_iff`). Over the non-negative reals (the periphery module `RegimeReals`, with Mathlib) every run within a band converges, to a rest where the adjustment is continuous, and the states converging below the draw are down-closed and fewer as the recent giving rises (`Drawn.converges`, `Drawn.limit_fates`, `Drawn.falls_given`). What whole numbers add is that the rest is reached in finitely many steps and the fate decided at a fixed step: giving moving halfway toward a draw of one converges and never rests (`Drawn.Relaxation.never_rests`) (RM5, [study §302](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#302-the-regime-in-any-amounts-and-its-runs-over-the-reals-rm5), [the regime in any amounts](../results/records/regime-in-any-amounts.md)). A condition's point of no return is not its own where its giving reads what is left of another. Under draws taken first, two conditions each giving from what is left of both, with each giving rising with what is left of each, keep the order of states, so a condition ending from a state ends from every state below it (`RegimeMargin.Coupled.ends_of_below`). Within a band in whole numbers a run comes to rest within `2 hi + 1` steps, and for each level of its partner the first condition ends exactly below a least level, found by a finite search, which does not rise as the partner's level rises (`RegimeMargin.Coupled.ends_iff_below`, `RegimeMargin.Coupled.threshold_falls`). A condition giving nothing where nothing is left of its partner falls by its draw once the partner stays within its draw, and ends within as many steps as its level (`RegimeMargin.Coupled.ends_after`); under dependence both ways the two end together. Support can carry a condition across its own point (`RegimeMargin.Coupled.MutualActivation.saves`); under inhibition more of the other can end a condition that holds alone, and the order of fates breaks (`RegimeMargin.Coupled.MutualInhibition.breaks_order`); beyond a band mutual support can hold a pair neither of which holds alone in a cycle that never rests (`RegimeMargin.Coupled.Relay.cycles`). A condition whose giving reads only what is left of itself runs as the regime (`RegimeMargin.Coupled.first_regime`) (RM6, [study §306](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#306-coupled-regimes-a-margin-that-is-not-its-own-rm6), [coupled regimes](../results/records/coupled-regimes.md)). Coupling is not bounded to pairs. A web of any number of conditions, each giving from what is left of all, keeps the order of states under mutual support, so raising any condition's level never ends one that held (`RegimeMargin.Coupled.Indexed.ends_of_below`). Where a condition gives nothing wherever nothing is left of another, the other's end ends it, and along every chain of such dependence, every dependent's draw positive, the end of one ends everything downstream of it, however far, while a ring of mutual dependence ends together or not at all (`RegimeMargin.Coupled.Indexed.ends_downstream`, `RegimeMargin.Coupled.Indexed.ends_together`). The cascade reaches a condition that does not depend directly on the first, and a draw of nothing on the chain stops it (`RegimeMargin.Coupled.Indexed.Chain.cascades`, `RegimeMargin.Coupled.Indexed.Chain.stopped`). The pair is the web indexed by `Bool`, and its laws of order and dependence are read off the web's (`RegimeMargin.Coupled.pair_run`) (RM7, [study §311](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#311-coupled-webs-support-across-a-web-and-the-cascade-of-ends-rm7), [coupled webs](../results/records/coupled-webs.md)).
- `Flow.continuing_excludes`: a full taker (`Flow.TakesAll`) that continues leaves no other formation taking from the condition at each of `level + 1` steps; full taking alongside another taker ends every formation depending at the matching need, the full taker included (`Flow.full_taking_ends`), and a taker active only while it takes ends, the ending derived (`Flow.continuing_ends_taker`). Of `Exceeding`'s three courses, each taking one keeps all three supported: the first's continuation does not need the second's ending, taking all does (`Excluding.three_courses`), and the courses are alike by admission (`Excluding.admission_alike`), so the loss is the member's continuation [AI reading, D44].
- `Flow.outrun_below`: where a part (the rest) takes jointly more than is given at each of `n` steps from a level below `n + m`, the level stays below `m` from then on and every formation depending at that need ends, whatever a member outside the part takes (`Flow.outrun_excess`, `Flow.outrun_ends`; full taking alongside another taker is the case of a part of two). With need one, every reading of the condition after the rest's end is alike under the member's acts (`Flow.outrun_reading_alike`), so no weight of such a reading selects its restraint. Tested on learners in a gated commons (CG2, `experiments/commons-outrun`, predictions frozen before any run): where the other members alone empty the patch, coupled learners empty it for every weight of an end reading; where they cannot, learners keep it, though the whole group at full draw would empty it.
- `Flow.seeking_ends`: a take more than now (`MoreThanNow`, §3.4) rises by at least `n` in `n` steps (`more_accumulates`), so from a condition given at most `B` a step it takes all that is given after `B + 1` steps, and under balance a formation that takes so while active is not active through a bounded window; where its activity does not resume, it ends, derived from balance and the bound on what is given alone. Where renewal depends on what it renews, the seeker exhausts the condition for every formation depending at the matching need, renewers included (`Flow.seeking_exhausts`). In `Alone` a taker taking all without its take rising continues (`Alone.takes_all`): what ends the seeker is the rising take. Used in §3.4.

What the derived extent does not reach. It runs one way, to what one's own continuation depends on: where one stops giving, a formation living on that giving ends while one continues (`Downstream.one_way`); it ends with the want (`Mortal.after_its_end`); and a clearer meeting every requirement derived here, active throughout, renewed, fed from beyond and drawing jointly within what is given, still exhausts the dweller's habitat (`Clearing.undermines_within_renewal`). The witnesses' endings are stipulated in their activity and shown consistent with balance, support and dependence; the general theorems derive endings.

Records: [continuing-self-fed-from-beyond](../results/records/continuing-self-fed-from-beyond.md), [own-count-misses-joint-excess](../results/records/own-count-misses-joint-excess.md), [whole-drawing-beyond-renewal-ends](../results/records/whole-drawing-beyond-renewal-ends.md), [derived-extent-limits](../results/records/derived-extent-limits.md), [what-taking-all-excludes](../results/records/what-taking-all-excludes.md), [the-rest-outruns-the-renewal](../results/records/the-rest-outruns-the-renewal.md), [where-a-rising-take-ends](../results/records/where-a-rising-take-ends.md).

### 2.4 Evolution and transmission

**In words.** Evolution names specific mechanisms (transmission, reconstruction, variation, selection), each explained through its own relations; it is not a synonym for every change. On the spine, descent is defined by taking part, giving and coming to stand as a condition, with no valuation.

- `price`: Price's equation in counting form, an arithmetic identity for any finite family of parents and offspring; the weighted total is not the selection term proper (`selected_uniform`).
- `SelectionTerm.covariation`: the selection term proper, with no premise: over any finite family, the number of parents times the offspring-weighted amounts, with the pairs where more offspring go with less amount, is the number of offspring times the parents' total, with the pairs where more go with more, the covariance with its denominators cleared; with `price` it is Price's equation with its selection term (`SelectionTerm.offspring_covariation`). Over generations each the offspring of the last, the mean amount is kept or rises across a step exactly where selection proper and transmission balance or raise (`Generations.kept_step_iff`, `Generations.rises_step_iff`), and a moved mean has an unbalanced step, found by deciding each (`Generations.moved_unbalanced`): with exact transmission one with selection proper, with uniform offspring numbers one with inexact transmission. Where only active formations give and activity needs support, a reading highest at the supported parents is never selected against (`SelectionTerm.Flow.support_not_against`); over generations of descent its mean, never lowered by transmission, does not fall, and with exact transmission moves only upward through a step of selection proper (`SelectionTerm.Flow.support_not_lower`, `SelectionTerm.Flow.support_moved_rises`); `Drawdown`'s flow meets these premises (`SupportedDescent.supported_descent`).
- `Reproduction.variation`: selection on offspring number itself, with no premise: read on the weights themselves no pair is discordant, so the number of members times the total of the squared weights is the square of the total weight with the squared differences over pairs, equal exactly where the weights are uniform (`Reproduction.none_iff_uniform`); with offspring numbers, over offspring the mean of their parent's offspring number is at least the mean over parents (`Reproduction.number_variation`). Over generations read by offspring number, the mean offspring number does not fall where transmission lowers none, and with exact transmission rises exactly where offspring numbers vary (`Generations.ByNumber.not_lower_step`, `Generations.ByNumber.rises_exact_iff`): the counting form of Fisher's fundamental theorem with exact transmission. A parent's offspring number is at most what it gives them (`Reproduction.Flow.born_le_given`) and, its offspring distinct within a family dissipated on, with one more at most what it takes from the family (`Reproduction.Flow.number_within_take`); distinct offspring standing within a family balanced and dissipated on number, with its takers, at most its stock (`Reproduction.Flow.born_within_stock`). A lineage within such a family reproduces at most its stock many steps and ends (`Reproduction.Flow.lineage_ends`), so read by offspring number one that began reproducing has a step at which transmission lowered offspring number by more than selection on it and what it raised (`Reproduction.Flow.closed_lineage_lowered`); without dissipation a lossless relay keeps a lineage going (`Reproduction.Relay.relay`). The additive part: counted, Cauchy–Schwarz holds over any finite list (`Reproduction.bound_sq`), so the concordance with the discordance of offspring number with any carried amount, squared, is at most the squared differences of offspring numbers over pairs times those of the amount (`Reproduction.additive_le`, `Reproduction.covariance_sq_le`): the additive part of the variance in offspring number through the amount is at most the whole, all of it where the amount is offspring number itself (`Reproduction.additive_self`), and with exact transmission the rise of the amount's mean is bounded so (`Reproduction.Generations.response_sq_le`); an amount can vary beside varying offspring numbers with no covariance (`Reproduction.Unrelated.unrelated`).
- `SelectionLevels.levels`: selection within groups and among them, with no premise beyond groups of equal size: over a family listed as k groups, the whole's concordance, with k times the groups' own discordance and the discordance among groups read by their totals, is the whole's discordance with k times the groups' own concordance and the concordance among groups; divided through, the whole's covariance is the mean of the groups' covariances with the covariance of their means, the multilevel form of Price's equation. So an amount selected against within the groups is selected for in the whole only where selection among them goes for it (`SelectionLevels.among_for_of_rises`), and over listed generations with exact transmission its mean rises only there (`SelectionLevels.among_for_of_raises`); with the same within-group counts the whole goes either way (`SelectionLevels.AmongWins.among_wins`, `SelectionLevels.WithinWins.within_wins`).
- `SelectionLevels.levels_sized`: selection within groups and among them for groups of any sizes. With n a common multiple of the sizes and each group's own term by n over its size, n squared times the whole's term balances n times the whole's size times the groups' own terms with the term of the groups' means read member by member; divided through, the whole's covariance is the size-weighted mean of the groups' covariances with the size-weighted covariance of their means, and `SelectionLevels.levels` is its case with equal sizes. Read by their totals, groups of unequal size can show nothing among them while their means carry all the whole's selection (`SelectionLevels.ByMeans.by_means`). Where the groups' means are alike nothing is selected among them, so a generation listed as groups alike in mean raises its mean exactly as selection within them goes (`SelectionLevels.rises_exact_iff_within_of_alike`): how the next generation is listed does not enter a step, but it fixes the next step's among-group part. Ending fixes that part for any sizes (`GroupEnding.Flow.rises_iff_inactive_sized`), and can use it up: with two keeping groups beside a group leaving nothing, holding back rises by selection among groups, the offspring of each keeping group form groups alike in it, and at the next step it falls below where it started (`GroupEnding.Exhausted.next_step`). Groups formed by descent keep it: with exact transmission the lineages of groups uniform in an amount keep the value each was founded at (`SelectionLevels.lineage_value`) and list every later generation (`SelectionLevels.lineages_whole`), so nothing is selected within them and the mean rises exactly where the lineages' means select for the amount (`SelectionLevels.rises_iff_lineages`), the complete-assortment end of reading group selection by assortment (Hamilton 1975). On the base, where at each step the lineages founded lower are not active, the mean never falls below where it started, over any number of steps (`GroupEnding.Flow.lineages_not_lower`), and rises wherever a higher lineage leaves offspring beside a lower one (`GroupEnding.Flow.lineages_raises`); with the groups of `Exhausted` formed by descent instead, holding back rises and stays (`GroupEnding.Assorted.kept`). Whether selection among groups accumulates or falls back is fixed by how the next groups are formed.
- `GroupEnding.rises_iff_ended`: selection among groups by ending: where every group with less of an amount than another leaves no offspring, nothing is discordant among the groups, so the whole selects for the amount exactly where k times the within-group discordance is below k times the within-group concordance with the concordance among groups; with groups uniform in it, never against it (`GroupEnding.not_against_of_ended`). On the base, where only active formations give, a member depending on a condition whose gated renewal ran into joint excess leaves no descendant from then on (`GroupEnding.Flow.excess_childless`, through `Flow.joint_excess_ends`), and over generations of descent with exact transmission the mean rises where the groups with less of the amount are such groups (`GroupEnding.Flow.raises_of_inactive`), and with groups not uniform it rises exactly where k times the within-group discordance is below k times the within-group concordance with the concordance among groups (`GroupEnding.Flow.rises_iff_inactive`); one condition shared by every group, once it ends them, leaves the next generation empty, so it supplies no selection among them (`GroupEnding.Flow.all_end`).
- `Flow.family_excess_ends`, `GroupEnding.Flow.coupled_end_together`: groups whose conditions are coupled. The joint count and the gated ending hold of a family of conditions read as one: a step at which the family's uptake exceeds its replenishment lowers its stock by at least one (`Flow.family_excess_bounded`), and where whatever gives to a member depends on some member at a need of at least m (the coupled gate), n such steps from a stock below n + m end every formation depending on any member at such a need; the laws of one condition (`Flow.joint_excess_ends` and those it rests on) are this law for the family of that condition alone. A patch renewed by a neighbour shows no joint excess read alone and its renewal is not gated on it, while read as one with the neighbour it is gated, in excess, and both formations end (`Coupled.alone_within`, `Coupled.alone_ungated`, `Coupled.all_end`): the commons test's scope prediction on the spine. Groups whose members depend on conditions of one such family end together, each leaving no offspring and nothing selected among them; where the groups with less of an amount depend on such a family they leave nothing (`GroupEnding.Flow.ended_of_family`). So ending selects among groups only between families.
- `Drift.falls_back`: drift, offspring sampled rather than listed. A next generation of two kinds drawn n times from p listed offspring of the first kind and q of the second, each sampling a course of `Typicality` weighed by the ways of drawing it: on average the sampled share is the listed share (`Drift.expected_ones`), and the squared deviations add to n·p·q·(p+q)^n (`Typicality.spread`). Where a listed step raises the share from h/N to p/(p+q) by the gap g = N·p − (p+q)·h, the samplings falling back to h/N or below weigh, times n²·g², at most N²·n·p·q·(p+q)^n: out of the whole, at most p·q/((p+q)²·δ²·n) with δ = p/(p+q) − h/N. They weigh at least q^n, the samplings losing the first kind (`Drift.lost_weight`, `Drift.falls_back_ge`), so undoing selection is rare as the sample grows and at no size ruled out. Over listed generations the same holds for a step raising the mean of an amount held or not (`Drift.Generations.raised_falls_back`), and on the base where that step is ending among groups (`Drift.Flow.ended_falls_back`). Counting weighs the samplings and does not say which happens. Over many generations, each sampled from the last (`Drift.sampled`), the final count summed over the histories is its start times the whole weight (`Drift.sampled_count`), the ends absorb (`Drift.sampled_none`, `Drift.sampled_all`), the histories still varied after k generations weigh at most r^k with r + 2 = N^N (`Drift.varied_vanishes`), and the histories fixing the first kind have, times N, a share within N·r^k/(N^N)^k below h (`Drift.fixation`): drift alone ends variation and fixes each kind with its starting share h/N.
- `unsupported_childless`: where only active formations give and activity needs support, an unsupported formation leaves no descendants, and the parents' weighted need is bounded by the level (`selected_le`).
- `transmitted_supported`: where descendants take up their parents' dependences (`TransmitsWithin`, a premise), a transmitted need exceeds the level at the giving step by at most the bound of transmission (`Slack`); in `Drawdown` transmission, variation, selection and the feedback of activity on selection lie in one unfolding. Used in §3.4 (`share_rises`) and §4.5 (passing).

Transmission is inheritance and need, and is independent where it binds (`Becoming.Transmission`). Fitness is not defined: offspring number is read as a weight and, bounded by what is given and taken, as an amount the flow fixes (`Becoming.Reproduction`); mutation, drift and population dynamics are not covered. The periphery mechanisms (inherited structural copying, formed copying and its correction, growth-constrained fragmentation, inherited organizational rules; standard Lean axioms) keep their own scope, and no correspondence between them and this content is proved; the growth case is restated on the base in `Becoming.Cuts` (§2.5). Prepared material joins recurrent chain formation (periphery): a copy of a word produces the word exactly when the pool supplies it and the support covers what it draws, and where every unit draws d from one stock and becomes two, n rounds from k units run exactly when the stock covers d·k(2^n − 1) (`Copying.built_iff`, `RecurrentAssembly.supported_rounds`). See [the theory of evolution](THE-THEORY-OF-EVOLUTION.md) and [evolutionary organization](../history/EVOLUTIONARY-ORGANIZATION.md).

<a id="feeding-and-return"></a>
### 2.5 Closures: feeding, influence, reach and system

**In words.** A formation's continuation is fed by those who give to what it requires, affected by those who exchange with what it depends on, and reaches those whose conditions its own exchange touches. The closures under these relations are nested: feeding within influence, influence and reach within the system. Feedback carries at most the influence closure, a part's exchange reaches into the reach closure, and the comparison with and without a part is decided within what the part reaches.

- `Flow.beyond_gives_nothing`: a formation's feeding closure (`Flow.WithinFeeding`) is the least region closed under feeding that holds it, and nothing beyond it gives to what a member requires. Under balance, a formation taking at every active step from a condition nothing supported gives to is active for at most the condition's starting level of consecutive steps: consumption ends with its feeders (`Flow.Balances.consumption_ends_with_feeders`). In `Overshoot` an ending returns through a feeder (`Overshoot.ends_through_feeder`), while in `Clearing` the dweller lies beyond the clearer's closure and ends while everything within it continues (`Clearing.dweller_beyond`). Used in §5 (each formation's own region along feeding).
- `Flow.withinSystem_of_withinInfluence` with `Flow.withinInfluence_of_withinFeeding`: the five levels (own events, feeding, influence, reach, system) are the frame's one closure of a relation (`WithinClosure`) under five relations, the feeding closure by definition (`Flow.WithinFeeding`), and a process's reach is the same closure under its steps (`reach_iff_withinClosure`), the seed–edge construction its case (`withinClosure_iff_closure`; EC1, [study §307](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#307-one-closure-the-closure-of-a-relation-at-the-frame-ec1)), each inclusion can be strict, and influence and reach point in opposite directions (`Clearing.levels`; in `Trophic`, wolves lie beyond a forest's feeding closure and within its influence closure, `Trophic.mediates`). One common condition makes every system the whole (`Flow.system_whole_of_common`), and one condition every formation requires and gives to makes every feeding closure the whole and bounded significance the condition (`Flow.feeding_unrestricted_of_common`). Used in §4.2 and §6.3.
- `Flow.Balances.uncoupled_beyond_influence`: what feedback carries. Nothing beyond the influence closure exchanges with what a member depends on (`Flow.beyond_influence`), so where a part lies beyond it, support within it is as without the part; in `Clearing` the clearer's support is the same whether or not it clears, while the dweller ends (`Clearing.uncoupled`). Levels follow their exchanges (`Flow.Balances.levels_agree`, `Flow.Balances.agrees_iff_even`). Used in §7.3.
- `Flow.Balances.comparison_within_reach`: for any significance covering what the part has reached before each step, the levels with and without the part retain and add exactly as they include and exceed each other, so significance bounded by a region closed under affecting compares every part within it as inclusion does (`Flow.Balances.closed_comparison`); for any two flows starting alike the comparison is decided within what their difference reaches (`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_difference`). Covering is enough and not needed (`Margin.reach_without_misjudgment`); a part with a member beyond a system can be misjudged (`Trophic.mixed_part`); with the rest fixed, effects through others' responses do not show (`Trophic.mediated_uncoupled`, `Trophic.responsive_mixed`); where a part gives what others take there may be no levels without it (`Clearing.no_levels_without_source`). Used in §6.3.
- `Cuts.closure_unchanged_iff_bypassed`: removing links leaves every closure as it was exactly where each removed link is bypassed; in a relation grown by attachment every link separates its ends, and a join frees exactly what it crosses (`Cuts.joined_cut_unchanged_iff`); on the base, each condition of a world grown by attachment is needed (`Flow.GrownBy.condition_needed`). `GrownFragmentation`'s certified cuts are periphery instances.

Earlier giving reaches a later need through a condition that stands throughout the stretch: the closure across steps contains the closure at one step, storage can make it strict (`Provision.through_store`), the exact condition for meeting a need under local balance is `m + taken ≤ level + given`, and withholding both of two redundant feeders can leave no balancing path (`Redundant.withheld_both`); carrying characterizes a possible retention, not the actual source. In `Clearing` the region along dependence is exactly each formation's feeding closure (`Clearing.dependenceRegion_iff`). Not derived: actual retention, a stricter notion of carrying, how a center represents the closure, and whether a formation knows beyond its feedback. A part is a decided family of formations, so one act is compared through a second flow (§2.6).

*Readings* [AI reading, D17, D20]. The feeding closure is what a continuation depends on; reach is where its own exchange can arrive; the system is the class one is in; situational awareness is knowing the gap between reach and what couples in (§7.3).

Records: [feeding-closure](../results/records/feeding-closure.md), [consumption-ends-with-feeders](../results/records/consumption-ends-with-feeders.md), [closure-verdict-meets-continuation-condition](../results/records/closure-verdict-meets-continuation-condition.md), [levels-of-closure](../results/records/levels-of-closure.md), [feedback-and-reach](../results/records/feedback-and-reach.md), [comparison-within-reach](../results/records/comparison-within-reach.md), [which-links-a-closure-needs](../results/records/which-links-a-closure-needs.md), [draws-on-a-shared-stock](../results/records/draws-on-a-shared-stock.md).

<a id="the-comparison-where-the-rest-responds"></a>
### 2.6 Response, propagation and loops

**In words.** Where the rest responds, a difference goes where couplings carry it; given the rest's law, the counterfactual of an act is the course the same law generates. How much difference arrives is itself a course, the carried amount, attained by a law and so the most that can arrive. It expands by orders of responses, a sum over routes back in time; a finite order is exact exactly where no loop of the couplings that go on lies upstream, and the most difference that can arrive grows faster than every polynomial only through such a loop, which forms by closing and need not run away.

- `counterfactual_is_course`: with the rest following one law of what has stood, two balanced flows starting alike with the part acting alike have the same course (`counterfactual_fixed`), so the counterfactual is the course the law generates, and a determined law steps beyond a run's history exactly at the moves it never takes (`ExchangeLaw.counterfactual_beyond_shown`). Where the law reads through what couples into each formation (`ExchangeLaw.Through`), the rest responds through its couplings (`ExchangeLaw.Through.responds`), so the comparison is decided within the responsive reach (`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_response`), the dual of the coupling closure (`Flow.withinResponse_iff_coupling`), and sensitivity is the least coupling where the law reads finitely many conditions (`Becoming.Sensitivity`). Used in §3.1 and §7.2.
- `most_difference_arrives`: a coupling strength is the measure sensitivity lacked (`ExchangeLaw.RespondsWithin`; a square law has none, `Propagation.Square.not_respondsWithin`); every gap is at most the carried amount (`gap_le_carried`), which the carrying law attains (`carried_attained`), positive exactly along a route in time of positive strengths (`carried_pos_iff`). A held difference keeps arriving without a loop (`Propagation.Chain.keeps_arriving`). Used in §4.2.
- `finite_order_iff_no_loop` with `carried_eq_carriedWithin_add`: order j is exact up to step j and where no loop lies upstream (`carriedWithin_exact_of_no_loop`); a cut at order j loses exactly what j + 1 responses carry from the carried amount, for every schedule (`Becoming.Remainder`), and the orders are sums over routes back in time, a cut being a truncation (`routeSum_eq_carriedWithin`, `Becoming.Routes`); with the same strengths at every step the carried amount is polynomial exactly where no loop lies upstream (`polynomial_iff_no_loop`), and the self-loop attains every bound (`Loops.SelfLoop.attained`). Used in §7.2 (law 8).
- `forms_by_closing` and `break_carried_nowhere`: a loop forms at the step a coupling closes a chain (`onLoop_succ_iff`), not foreseen from the amounts before it (`Closing.Onset`); loops lie within what a law reads (`not_onLoop_unread`), and for every law reading a stock only below a threshold, raising the stock changes nothing up to the break, where the loop through it forms (`ExchangeLaw.ReadsBelow.standing_no_loop`, `Thresholds.Draw`). A difference reaches a formation only along routes of reading from where it lies (`apart_only_downstream`), and observing is the end of such a route, formed by closing (`route_forms_by_closing`, `Measure.loop_at`). Used in §3.4 (the break of a runaway) and §7.3.
- `runaway_of_roundTrip` with `Frequency.Sparse.polynomial`: every return at least doubles the carried amount (`carried_doubles_roundTrip`), frequent returns make it outgrow every polynomial, and a self-loop coupling only at sparse steps stays polynomial, so a loop of recurring couplings need not run away; the rate counts the steps at which couplings act (`carried_le_count`), and that every schedule settles is exactly the dichotomy `RecursOrStops` (`settled_iff_recursOrStops`), which gives LPO (§8.6). Used in §4.3 (constraint 6) and §7.2.
- `carried_around_exact` with `Ring.bound_strict`: a *strength* is what a link gives per unit of the level it reads (`w i`), and a *factor* is what a return map multiplies a difference by in one turn (`LoopReturn.scaling`); they differ already for one condition, whose self-coupling of strength `M` returns the carried amount with factor `1 + M` (`LoopReturn.selfLoop_carried`). Around a cycle of `m + 1` links, each the only giving into the next condition and giving exactly its strength times the current level of the one before (`LoopReturn.GivesExactly`), one turn carries the product of the strengths times the amount at the loop, plus what the loop holds `m` steps on, plus the last strength times what the intermediate conditions held as the turn passed them, carried on by the strengths after them (`carried_around_exact`). That is the return map's course with factor one plus the product, plus what the loop gained over the turn's first `m` steps and that carried-on amount (`carried_around_excess`), equal to the course exactly where both are nothing (`around_course_iff`). On a ring of two or more conditions with a positive strength `w` and the same positive start at every condition, each grows as a self-loop would, `(1 + w) ^ t` times the start, so the course with factor one plus the product stays strictly below the carried amount (`Ring.bound_strict`): for a cycle of several conditions one plus the product of strengths is a lower bound on a turn, not its factor. Used in §4.3 (constraints 5 and 6) and §4.4.

The rest of the line, each with its record. *A law of the whole and no part* (`Becoming.Tail`): a counterexample to the least coupling exists exactly where a rule decides of every Boolean sequence whether it is false throughout, the weak omniscience rule, and the tail law takes the LPO rule (`not_through_sensitive_iff_rule`, `vanishDecision_iff_lpoRule`). *History sums* (`Becoming.HistorySums`): support, splitting, exact orders and the steps between; an exact order needs the alternatives to commute (`HistorySums.grouping_needs_commuting`). *Coupling by level* (`Becoming.LevelCoupling`): up to double negation, `obs` is exactly where some law reading through the couplings couples a level into a formation within its need (`not_not_obs_iff_respondsByLevel`), while through a threshold only a crossing passes (`ExchangeLaw.Through.not_respondsByLevel`, `crossing_passes_iff`); a crossing where admission is not decided is not shown. *What dependence alone carries* (OB1, [study §285](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#285-what-dependence-alone-carries-of-a-level-ob1)): through dependence alone, a formation exchanging only where supported exchanges no less where the levels at the step stand no lower (`ExchangeLaw.Through.rises_at_step`), so of one level at the step dependence alone carries exactly the responses rising with it, by units at thresholds (`carried_iff_rising`, `Layers.carry`), a fall only where some level falls (`ExchangeLaw.Through.carried_fall_needs_fall`), and supplying a deficit not at all (`supply_not_carried`); where the level and a complement stand at a fixed total, units depending on both carry every response (`Complement.carry`, `supply_carried_by_complement`). *A move formed within the unfolding* (`Becoming.MoveFormation`): a move is the part's own exactly where it is formed through the part's configuration.

Not derived: the strengths themselves and a least strength; real amounts and strengths below one (damping); continuous time; laws reading infinitely many conditions at a step; which of the bound by returns and the bound by coupling steps is met beyond the self-loop; a turn around a cycle of several conditions in closed form, since the exact turn keeps what the intermediate conditions hold; the spectral radius of given strengths; that formations respond only through their couplings; which responses to levels at earlier steps dependence alone carries; and a complement where the total is not fixed. The conjecture of [observation-is-a-route](../results/records/observation-is-a-route.md), that observation is no second relation, is settled at the step of the exchange ([what-dependence-alone-carries-of-a-level](../results/records/what-dependence-alone-carries-of-a-level.md), §8.1).

*Readings* [AI reading, D25, D39, D43, D45]. An act has two counterfactuals: with the rest fixed, and with the rest responding through its couplings; a counterfactual is not a second world but the law's step beyond its history, and what couples into a formation (识) is what its exchange is sensitive to. The coupling strength says how strongly where sensitivity says whether. On 12 October Ren asked, comparing the theory with “Fermi's diagrams” (he meant Feynman diagrams), “how how accurate can we do that uh what is the limitation do we have losses” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-12-move-towards-100.md)): the orders are the same structural kind as a sum over histories, the responses its vertices, `Loops.binom` counting time orderings; a cut loses exactly the remainder of a Neumann series, in time order as in the Dyson series; the loss is the cut's, not the theory's, and the match is structural, not physical. Only a loop of the couplings that go on makes a difference run away, and such a loop need not; which loops a world has is part of its situation, loops forming by closing and dissolving where couplings stop. A runaway's break is where a loop forms: the blindness of §3.4 and the formation of loops are one event seen from two sides. `obs` is no second kind of relation beside the couplings: dependence couples by threshold, observation by level. In the stronger sense, that `obs` derives from dependence, it is settled at the step of the exchange: observing a level is depending on it and on where its amount goes, wherever the level has a complement at a fixed total or the response rises with it, and only a response falling with a level nothing complements, supplying its deficit, needs coupling by level. The one relation at the root is dependence within a conserved whole, where what one part lacks another holds.

<a id="3-认识与探究--knowing-and-inquiry"></a>
## 3. Knowing and inquiry

### 3.1 Testing, experiment and mechanism

**In words.** Knowing lies within the unfolding: what reaches a formation is what couples into it, by threshold (whether a need is met) or by level. A claim is excluded only through a difference in what couples in; a judgment is a one-step inquiry; what an act would have done is fixed by a law, not by the record of one run; and a mechanism is a description that carries a law.

- `Web.Excludes.not_of_alike`: a claim and its rival must differ where something couples in. Without a model a finite prefix does not exclude a claim about what follows it (`Web.tail_unexcluded`); relative to a model a formation observing a stock excludes a false claim at the first step (`Flow.excludes_tail_within`), but excludes the model and the claim together, so a wrong model excludes what holds (`Web.excludesWithin_iff_conjunction`, `Stationary.model_error`). What the clearer cannot test the dweller can, and together they test what neither can alone (`Clearing.tested_by_dweller`, `Clearing.pooled`). Used in §7.2 (law 5) and §7.3.
- `commonResponse_judge_iff`: a response admissible at every configuration believed possible is a common response of the judging process, so robust judgment, a change chosen under observation and safe choice are one common response, preserved by refining observation (`commonResponse_antitone`; on the flow, `Web.class_judge_iff`). A sound inquiry policy secures its goal on the configurations its executions reach (`Scope.secures`), and narrowing candidate rules by an observation gives the greatest safe answer (`Narrowing.revise_is_after`, `narrowed_answer_greatest`).
- `Run.not_fixes_of_untaken`: relative to determinacy, the record fixes a move's outcome exactly where the run has taken that move there (`Run.fixes_of_takes`), so what one's own act would have done otherwise is fixed only where the configuration returns (`Run.fixes_on_return`); a run keeping its whole history fixes only what it did (`keepHistory_fixes_nothing_else`). Experiment needs a model of what is irrelevant: where the step factors through a projection, one occasion stands for its class (`Run.generalizes`); a wrong projection is never excluded while the move goes in step with what it leaves out (`Confound`). Used in §7.2 (law 6).
- `carries_law_iff`: a projection carries a law exactly where configurations alike under it go alike, with no premise; the image is the only candidate (`Image.least`), a law can hold coarse and not whole (`Coarse`) or whole and not coarse (`Switching`), and a state going on with its rule has a law of its own exactly where the rule makes no difference to it (`adaptive_state_law_iff`). What depends only on the projection re-absorbs the rest within one step (`DependsOnlyOn.reabsorbed`), of which a rule holding the outcome (`Law.perturbation_reabsorbed`) is the instance. Used in §4.5.
- `Run.law_under_iff`: along one run, under an equivalence, some process going alike admits the run's steps exactly where any two alike steps end alike; its readings through a projection, through coupling that changes and through fixed conditions are corollaries (`Run.law_through_iff`, `Run.law_through_dependence_iff`, `Run.law_through_coupling_iff`). A level the formation does not couple into can break the law at its coupling (`Unseen.no_law`); forming and dissolving dependences separate the fixed and the changing criterion (`Changing.dissolving_consistent`, `Changing.forming_inconsistent`); going alike does not give an exact model of finite paths (`Stuck`).

Threshold coupling passes only whether a need is met (`Web.alike_of_threshold`); a difference below threshold stays until support diverges (`Flow.subthreshold_alike`), which fails where others respond to the stock itself (`Attracting.higher_breaks_first`); what lies beyond a region whose members respond only through their own couplings does not show within it (`Flow.alike_within_closed`). A standard causal model x′ = F(x, u, ε) is a determined process whose move is input and noise together (`inputs_determined`): an intervention is a move not taken, unfixed by the record and fixed by the model (`inputs_intervention_unfixed`, `inputs_model_fixes`), and noise is a move not represented (`forget_inputs_determined_iff`). The response hypotheses and stepwise balance on what couples in are supplied.

*Readings* [AI reading, D25, D26]. “Couples into” replaces “registers”, the earlier rendering of Ren's 识 (shí); 识 is kept for a formation's own course through the lumping its couplings make. Exclusion through what couples in is the structure of falsifiability. Feedback through support has three kinds of not-seeing, each in its scope: below threshold, beyond the horizon, beyond the coupling closure. Replication is return; a control is the model through which the step factors; variation independent of what is left out is randomization. A mechanism is a projection that carries a law, emergence is going alike at a coarser description, and a system changing its own causality has its law on the joint configuration.
