<a id="what-a-finite-stretch-refutes-in-every-process"></a>
### 3.2 What finite stretches decide

**In words.** A history is read off its finite stretches: the histories are the ideals of the stretches, their completion. What a finite stretch can refute is a closed class, what it can verify an open one; every reading splits into a part finite records bear on and a remainder no record refutes; and above both lies what a method comes to say in the limit. Telling whether a history ends is deciding whether it changes again.

- `Stretches.adheres_iff_closed` with `Refutable.not_not`: adhering is the least closed class holding a reading's runs, and a refutable reading holds, up to double negation, at every run adhering to it; removing the double negation, for every process, is exactly double-negation elimination (`refutable_holds_iff`), and telling a settling history from a going one is exactly LPO (`Stretches.cut_decided_iff_lpo`). The finite histories are a compact basis (`Stretches.histories_representation`). Read by moves alone, a history is a course or a sequence of moves (`run_goesOn`), and a process can have every course feasible and no run (`history_without_run`). Used in §2.1 and §8.6.
- `Ideals.open_iff_verifiable`: the open classes are exactly the verifiable ones, with no premise either way; the step from a verifiable reading to a refutable complement is double-negation elimination, and on runs Markov's principle (`verifiable_compl_refutable_markov`). Every reading is its hull met with its remainder (`split`), the largest such part (`remainder_largest`), refuted by no stretch (`remainder_not_refuted`); this is the safety–liveness decomposition (Alpern and Schneider 1985) stated constructively, and its disjunctive form is excluded middle (`orNotAdheres_dense_iff_em`).
- `insulates_iff_dense`: a practice keeping its verdicts within a favoured reading, each fixed by the record so far, exists exactly where the favoured reading is dense, and a refutable reading so kept excludes nothing (`Insulates.refutable_not_not`). Recurrence is dense and neither verifiable nor refutable (`Sequences.recurs_not_verifiable`). Used in §3.5.
- `Limit.still_verifies` with `Limit.Free.settles_not_refuted`: where configurations are told apart, “the last step changed nothing” verifies settling in the limit, while under Markov's principle no method refutes it in the limit; recurrence is the other way round (`Limit.Sequences.lastMove_refutes`, `Limit.Sequences.recurs_not_verified`). Identification in the limit (Putnam, Gold, Kelly) is tracking a constant target (`Limit.identifies_iff_tracks`), and learning through a map is tracking (`LearnableRuns.learnable_iff_tracks`), identification only where the law leaves the feature unchanged (`LearnableRuns.Merge.learnable_not_identifies`). Used in §3.3.

Settling thus sits on three levels: no finite stretch verifies or refutes it, a method verifies it in the limit, and deciding it for every process of a finite world is LPO (`LastChange.lpo_iff_settles`, §3.4). Which readings of an actual inquiry are verifiable, refutable or dense depends on the process they are read on.

*Readings* [AI reading]. These laws are the formal side of the [demarcation assessment](SCIENTIFIC-PHILOSOPHY.md#15-scientific-standing-and-warranted-correction). They do not make refutability a criterion of standing: a hypothesis about an unending course, such as recurrence, can be honest and dense; a verdict kept dense by revision cannot also claim that finite records bear on it. “This has stopped changing” is a claim inquiry can come to make and keep making, though no step tells it the claim has become final; “this changes again and again” cannot be come to even in the limit.

Records: [a-history-read-off-its-finite-stretches](../results/records/a-history-read-off-its-finite-stretches.md), [what-a-finite-history-refutes](../results/records/what-a-finite-history-refutes.md), [what-a-finite-stretch-verifies](../results/records/what-a-finite-stretch-verifies.md), [what-a-method-comes-to-say-in-the-limit](../results/records/what-a-method-comes-to-say-in-the-limit.md), [learning-through-a-map-is-tracking-in-the-limit](../results/records/learning-through-a-map-is-tracking-in-the-limit.md).

### 3.3 Learning and identification

**In words.** Induction is resolved into laws, not certified. With no premise no prefix fixes the next value, and every extrapolation has a course that answers it. 理 is the modeling of the laws of structure and change, one notion because a model of change is a lumping the step goes alike across. Within a bound of N states a prefix of 2N − 1 steps fixes the course, given the bound or not; a feature is learnable through a map exactly where a reading of the future through the map fixes it; and where runs part, the situation fixes which way, and weights count situations.

- `no_prefix_fixes_next` with `diagonal_misses`: no prefix fixes the next value, even among finite worlds, and for every extrapolation a course answers it and keeps its whole prefix, so it is open. Where a list holds a determined law's orbit, extrapolation by elimination misses fewer than `tri N` times and that it rests cannot be refuted (`held_byElimination_bounded`, `held_rest_unrefuted`), though its prefix never shows it.
- `goAlike_iff_lumped`: a model of structure is a lumping (`Lumped`), a model of change a lumping across which the step goes alike, and the two are one. Finite data fix an untried value exactly where it is lumped with an observed input (`determines_lumped_iff`); the class of all functions is free at every untried input (`every_determines_nothing`); training is selection through the data and is right beyond it exactly where the class is determined there (`Training.loss_through`, `Training.fit_right_iff`). Used in §7.1 and §7.3.
- `Identification.bounded_agree` with `Identification.leastBound_exact`: two histories with at most N and N′ states that agree before N + N′ − 1 agree everywhere, exact for every pair (after Fine and Wilf, counting states); the extrapolation by the least bound, given no bound, is right from 2N − 1 on, and none is right at 2N − 2 on every such history (`Identification.rest_earliest`); the bound buys only silence. Within one world Moore's bound N − 1 holds with no decision (`Law.sameFuture_iff_within`), and tests are bounded prefixes too (`Law.sameTests_iff_within`). Used in §4.4 (pace).
- `learnable_iff`: in a world a list holds, a feature is learnable through a map exactly where configurations with the same future through it have features agreeing from some step on; this is an observability condition, not a procedure. Features lumped by the map are always learnable (`learnable_of_lumped`); unrolled on its own proposals, the extrapolation given nothing fixes the future through the map (`Learnable.ahead_fixes`); a feature is learnable exactly where a table of at most N labelled names of N − 1 values learns it (`Learnable.learnable_iff_table`), and no extrapolation given nothing learns every learnable feature (`Learnable.given_nothing_not_every`).
- `Parting.world_right_iff`: where runs part, no extrapolation, the process included, is right on both (`Parting.not_right_on_both`); some extrapolation is right at a step exactly where no runs part there, so identification holds for every process exactly where runs do not part. Most courses show the weights, with no premise (`Typicality.spread`; exponentially, `Typicality.concentrated`), and a law whose moves are situations is determined, so the finer courses number the sum over histories (`Situations.sum_finer`): a chance is a number of situations of a kind.

The rest of the line. *The split that makes sense*: the coarsest split keeping the map's value and carrying a law makes sense (`Law.identifiesAlike_of_law`), a part of the value alone makes sense exactly where the rest makes no difference to its next value (`Law.part_identifiesAlike_iff_step`), and states are read off a history, finitely many exactly where it repeats (`finitely_many_states_iff`; the law of moments is close to definitional). *Readings within what they read*: a family indexed by what it reads misses a reading for any map moving every value (`Diagonal.gap_unsaid`, Lawvere), completing is an unfolding whose gaps never return (`Diagonal.unfolding_open`), the liar is a rhythm (`Diagonal.negation_returns`), and with an undecided value it rests undecided while a family of three-valued readings is still incomplete (`Undecided.liar_both_sides`, `Undecided.incomplete`). *Claims in context*: a context-free frame fits exactly where what contexts show glues (`Contextual.fitsFree_iff`); Specker's boxes, the Mermin–Peres square and nine two-qubit observables (states as nonzero vectors with four integer components, narrower than Hilbert space) glue nowhere (`Specker.noGlobal`, `no_context_free_values`). Periphery: the acquisition chain's transfer as generalization (`uniform_determines`), and Hebbian development as an adaptive system (`hebbian_no_activity_law`). Not added: real or complex amplitudes, and which situation happens; a cubic late rest of elimination is attained (`Identification.byElimination_rest_cubic`).

*Readings* [AI reading, D26, D33, D34, D40, D43, D52]. 理, after Ren's “我觉得理更像是对结构和变化的规律的建模” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-12-learning-wisdom-root-and-induction.md)), is the modeling of the laws of structure and change; the uniformity of nature is the local hypothesis `Lumped`, named where used. For a trained network, generalization comes from the lumping that architecture and updates impose, not from the loss; the meta level is learning again, with no top. The map bounds what is learned, and what lies beyond it is reached only by widening it. A finite prefix is a projection, and a bound makes it lose nothing; what a learnable feature adds to the prefix is a finite reading of a future it already fixes. The diagonal's limit is the projection's, holding the whole in one description inside, not something beyond the truth; the undecided value is what revision has not decided, not a third truth. Wave and particle are projections onto context-free values, and a value is what a context shows. Ren wrote that “reality is always situational” and that “just saying we are all one doesn't make it true, the situation makes it true” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-16-the-situation-makes-it-true.md)): everything read through the map is derived situationally as far as runs do not part, and at a parting the situation fixes the way.

Records: [learning-by-refutation](../results/records/learning-by-refutation.md), [generalization-through-lumping](../results/records/generalization-through-lumping.md), [the-split-that-makes-sense](../results/records/the-split-that-makes-sense.md), [readings-within-what-they-read](../results/records/readings-within-what-they-read.md), [revision-with-an-undecided-value](../results/records/revision-with-an-undecided-value.md), [claims-shown-in-context](../results/records/claims-shown-in-context.md), [identification-where-bounded](../results/records/identification-where-bounded.md), [learnable-through-a-map](../results/records/learnable-through-a-map.md), [what-an-extrapolation-given-nothing-fixes](../results/records/what-an-extrapolation-given-nothing-fixes.md), [what-finitely-many-labels-fix](../results/records/what-finitely-many-labels-fix.md), [identification-where-runs-part](../results/records/identification-where-runs-part.md), [typicality-by-counting](../results/records/typicality-by-counting.md), [weights-count-situations](../results/records/weights-count-situations.md), [transition-grouping-word-partition](../results/records/transition-grouping-word-partition.md).

<a id="the-boundary-condition-for-无明"></a>
### 3.4 Seeking, 无明 and selection

**In words.** Seeking is activity toward a target met at a state, the target formed within the unfolding. A target rests only at a state the law leaves unchanged; “more than now” needs an open world, and “above all others” never rests for two. Losses reach a seeker only through what couples into it, and a selection through a selector's couplings reaches exactly what couples in: what lies beyond is selected alike, raising what couples in draws from beyond and, past the slack there, breaks what depends on it, an optimum of any order rising with what couples in lowers what lies beyond as far as any outcome does wherever that leaves what couples in room to rise, and, where the stock drawn on couples in by threshold only, how far off a break is does not couple in.

- `Law.rests_iff`: under a determined law, seeking comes to rest with its target met exactly where it reaches a state the law leaves unchanged that meets the target. Under undetermined laws enough parts in two, possible (`Enough.possibly_rests_iff`) and assured, the second adding that every run settles (`Enough.assured_iff`; not given by every configuration staying and meeting, `Enough.Free.stays_meet_not_assured`). In a world a list holds, some run keeps changing exactly where a reachable change can be undone (`Settling.keeps_changing_iff`), a run has fewer than N changes (`LastChange.changes_lt`), and that every run settles is exactly LPO (`LastChange.lpo_iff_settles`). Used in §8.6.
- `more_needs_open` with `Law.aboveAll_never_rests`: “more than now” met at every step needs an open world, and “above all others” is met by at most one seeker (immediate from the definition of a strict maximum). Where what stands runs down from some step on, rises stop by exhaustion, not by enough (`Flow.more_exhausted`; and descent with them, `Flow.descent_exhausted`); read on a take from a condition given within a bound, exhaustion ends the seeker (`Flow.seeking_ends`, §2.3). Where carriers of a target leave more offspring than others, their share rises (`share_rises`, an averaging inequality; through a selection, `share_rises_through`). Used in §4.5 and §7.2 (law 7).
- `Web.break_not_bounded`: before the break, for every horizon, some world with the stock raised is alike through the formation's couplings and lasts past the horizon; a policy through the couplings that keeps its support never uses a larger stock coupled by threshold only, and one that has used it has already lost support (`Flow.policy_keeps_difference`, `Flow.policy_uses_after_failure`). Coupling the stock by level passes it at once (`Web.observed_raise_couples`). The law holds because the stock is unobserved by hypothesis; where it is observed, a break can often be seen coming, as the early-warning-signal literature shows for systems approaching critical transitions through critical slowing down (Scheffer et al., "Early-warning signals for critical transitions", *Nature* 461, 2009), and that is the case the hypothesis excludes. Used in §7.2 (law 4).
- `Web.selects_alike_iff`: two histories are selected alike by every selection through `g`'s couplings exactly when they are alike through them, by construction of `Web.SelectsThrough`; so with the rest fixed, everything beyond the coupling closure is selected alike, also pooled (`Flow.unselected_beyond_coupling`), and an act whose difference stays beyond the actor's closure is selected alike by every member, the actor included (`Flow.act_unselected`, the rest's response derived). The gap is the reach closure less the coupling closure (`Clearing.unselected`). Used in §7.1–§7.4, where the pool laws are stated.
- `Flow.favours_reaching_beyond`: where a family holding what a group couples into gains nothing across a step, a rise of what couples in is paid in full from beyond (`Flow.Balances.rise_paid_from_beyond`), so every selection holding only where it rises holds only on flows taking from a condition no member couples into; `Diversion` is an instance, and its supplying flow shows the premise binds (`Diversion.supplying_raises_untaken`). Used in §4.3 and §7.2 (law 3).
- `Flow.Balances.breaks_beyond`: where the whole gains nothing, the beyond falls by the rise and the loss exactly (`Flow.Balances.beyond_falls_exactly`), so what a rise draws is depended on as soon as the rise with the loss exceeds the slack of the dependences beyond, conditions nothing depends on counting in full (`Flow.Balances.breaks_beyond_past_free`): one breaks, and where activity needs support its formation ends or changes (`Flow.Balances.breaks_beyond_unsettles`). Every selection rewarding such a rise holds only where a formation outside the group loses a dependence on a condition no member couples into (`Flow.favours_breaking_beyond`; in `Diversion` the dweller ends, `DrawnBeyond.Dweller.rise_ends_dweller`), and a rise equal to the slack breaks nothing (`DrawnBeyond.WithinSlack.within_slack`). Raising from nothing new is reaching beyond (`Flow.RaisesOn.reaches`); over generations whose more offspring go only to raisers, the share reaching beyond is never selected against, does not fall where reaching is transmitted and with exact transmission moves only upward (`Flow.share_not_against`, `Flow.share_not_lower`, `Flow.share_moved_rises`; it falls where reaching is not carried on, `DrawnBeyond.Lapsing.share_falls`); closed, the family rises by at most what the beyond loses (`Flow.Balances.rise_bounded_by_beyond`). When several formations take at once the joint draw breaks: a dependence breaks exactly where its need with all that is taken exceeds the level with all that is given (`Flow.Balances.broken_iff`), the highest needs first, whoever takes (`Web.Broken.of_need_le`), on a condition an active formation draws (`Flow.Balances.breaks_beyond_drawn`), and a break within each drawer's own slack belongs to no one drawer's take (`Flow.Balances.broken_shared`); restraint by the excess, by any drawers, keeps it (`Flow.Balances.kept_of_restrained`).
- `Optimum.least_iff`: over any set of outcomes (the levels after a step), every order rising with the levels of what couples in, ranking above every outcome that exceeds another there whatever either holds beyond, has its optima at the least level beyond, condition by condition, exactly where every outcome standing above another beyond is beaten on what couples in; shared units, a unit beyond movable into what couples in, give that room (`Optimum.least_of_shared`), and counting the beyond only after what couples in changes nothing (`Optimum.Rising.mono`, `Optimum.Drain.counted_after_drains`). An optimum breaks exactly the dependences beyond that some outcome breaks (`Web.optimum_broken_iff`), and where activity needs support their formations end, no member of a group coupled only into what couples in and coupled into by none (`Web.optimum_ends_beyond`; in `Diversion` the store's optimum drains the source and the dweller ends, `Optimum.Drain.drained_ends`). Of balanced steps from equal totals gaining nothing on the whole, one leaves the family at most where another does exactly where it leaves the beyond with the loss at least where the other does (`Flow.Balances.family_le_iff`, `Flow.top_iff_least`), and restricted to outcomes keeping each dependence beyond, the optimum takes exactly the slack (`Flow.keeping_optimum_takes_slack`; `Optimum.Drain.kept_takes_slack`). Room is read off one step of exchange: among the outcomes open to a part whose members take at most their capacity and give no more than they take, the rest exchanging as it does, there is room above any floors (`Optimum.Above.room`), and every optimum stands at each condition beyond at what the rest leaves less the part's capacity, or at nothing (`Optimum.optimum_beyond`), untouched where the part may take nothing (`Optimum.optimum_untouched`), lowered exactly where it has capacity and something stands (`Optimum.optimum_lowers_iff`); a dependence beyond breaks exactly where its need exceeds that level (`Web.optimum_broken_iff_capacity`; in `Diversion`, exactly where the taker's capacity is positive, `Optimum.Drain.drawing_broken_iff`). Several optimizers at a mutual best response, each alone by its own order rising with its own family, leave a condition beyond every family at what the formations outside them leave less their joint capacity, or at nothing, each taking its whole capacity wherever something stands, as one part made of all of them would (`Optimum.mutual_beyond`, `Optimum.MutualBest.full`, `Optimum.mutual_as_one`, `Web.mutual_broken_iff_capacity`; two takers each within the slack of a need of one leave nothing, `Optimum.TwoTakers.joint_drains`). Over a course in which the part chooses again at every step, each step's level beyond is read off the one before by that law (`Optimum.course_beyond`; for several optimizers at mutual best responses, `Optimum.mutual_course_beyond`). Where the rest renews the condition by exactly `r` a step at a positive need and by at most `r` below, and takes there with the capacity `k` at every step, a dependence at that need, met at the start, is lost exactly where `r < k`, for good, and where `k ≤ r` the condition stays at its need (`Web.course_lost_iff`, `Web.course_kept_iff`); nothing is drawn beyond the starting level with `r` a step (`Web.course_drawn_within`). What the renewal does below the need decides only what is drawn: where it stops there, a course above the renewal draws a bounded amount (`Web.course_ended_drawn`), a positive draw is bounded exactly where `r < k`, and over a long course keeping draws more (`Optimum.drawn_bounded_iff`, `Optimum.keeping_draws_more`); where at least `a` is given at every step, everything given is drawn, at least `a` a step, without bound, the dependence lost all the same (`Web.course_drawn_unbounded`). Several optimizers jointly above the renewal lose it, however small each one's capacity (`Web.mutual_course_lost`; in `Optimum.Renewing` a store optimizer above the renewal ends with less in its store than one at it, `keeping_holds_more`, and two takers at mutual best responses with less than a restrained course none of whose steps is one, `restraint_holds_more`). Where an order ranks whole courses over a horizon, what a course draws from a condition beyond, what it leaves there and everything the renewal falls short of its greatest `r` add to the start with `r` a step (`Optimum.drawn_add_shortfall`, `Flow.Balances.taken_add_shortfall`), so what is drawn registers a level only through what is renewed there, and two courses given alike and ending alike take the same (`Flow.Balances.taken_eq_of_alike`). Against a renewal given by a law of the level, the courses drawing the most are exactly those holding a level of greatest renewal through the horizon and leaving nothing at its end (`Optimum.most_iff`); where such a level lies within the start with what is given there and the capacity covers the first step's draw down to it and it with `r`, over at least two steps, every optimum of every order rising with the draw is one of them (`Optimum.frontier_holds`): a dependence whose need lies above every level of greatest renewal is broken at the first step and unmet through the horizon, and one that every such level meets is kept to the horizon's last step and broken at its end (`Web.frontier_broken`, `Web.frontier_kept`; in `Optimum.Holding`, under the logistic law the optimum draws a source from a dweller's need down to where it renews most, `logistic_broken`, and under a gate holding at the need and below it are both optima, taking the same, `gated_alike`). Kept at a need `m` through the horizon, where what is given at or above `m` is at most `q`, a course draws, with what it leaves, at most the start with what is given there and `q` a step (`Optimum.keeping_le_most`, `Flow.Balances.keeping_taken_le`), and every optimum among the keeping courses of every order rising with the draw holds where `q` is given and leaves exactly `m` (`Optimum.frontier_keeps`, `Web.frontier_keeps`; attained, `Optimum.continued_keeping_frontier`); so keeping forgoes, against the course holding a level of greatest renewal and leaving nothing, `(r − q) n + m`, the renewal given up at the need for each step and the need left at the end. An order adding to what a keeping course draws a coupling below that has only optima breaking the dependence within the horizon, one adding more to a keeping course and nothing to a course falling below the need only optima keeping it, and both have optima (`Optimum.frontier_breaks_below`, `Optimum.frontier_keeps_above`, `Web.frontier_breaks_below`, `Web.frontier_keeps_above`, `Optimum.continued_breaking_frontier`, `Optimum.continued_kept_frontier`); where the capacity cannot draw down to nothing or to the need, the end it reaches enters the thresholds. Where `q < r` the amount grows with the horizon and outweighs every coupling short of the horizon with the need (`Optimum.forgone_grows`, `Web.frontier_breaks_long`; in `Optimum.Holding`, under the logistic law keeping the dweller through `n + 2` steps forgoes `n + 3`, `logistic_coupled`). Added instead for each step the need stays met from the start, a coupling `c` does not erode so: a course met through `j` steps draws, with `r` for each of them, at most the start with what is given there, `r` a step and `q` for each of them (`Optimum.met_drawn_le`, `Flow.Balances.met_taken_le`), so every optimum of every order rising with the worth keeps the need through every horizon where `r ≤ q + c` and `c` exceeds the end the keeping course reaches (`Optimum.frontier_keeps_stepwise`, `Web.frontier_keeps_stepwise`), and meets it to the last step where `r < q + c` (`Optimum.frontier_met_before_end`, `Web.frontier_met_before_end`); where `c` with the end the capacity reaches is below the need, the horizon's end breaks the dependence, at the last step alone where also `r < q + c` (`Optimum.frontier_breaks_at_end`, `Web.frontier_breaks_at_end`, `Web.frontier_breaks_last`); where `q + c < r` a long horizon outweighs it (`Optimum.outweighed_stepwise`, `Web.frontier_breaks_stepwise_long`); optima exist in every case (`Optimum.continued_stepwise_frontier`, `Optimum.continued_ending_stepwise_frontier`, `Optimum.continued_holding_stepwise_frontier`; in `Optimum.Holding`, under the logistic law three a step keeps the dweller at every horizon, two a step to the last step, and at most one a step breaks it, `logistic_stepwise`). Where the order weights the draw of the step from `t` by `w t`, any weighting, what a course could draw at a step, with what it drew before, changes by what the level it leaves gains, `w (t + 1)` on it with its renewal against `w t` on it held (`Optimum.Next.drawable_step`); so every optimum of every order rising with the weighted draw gains at each step what an open course of greatest weighted gain leaving nothing gains, and leaves nothing weighted (`Optimum.frontier_gains_weighted`, `Optimum.weighted_frontier`), breaking at the first step a dependence above every level of greatest weighted gain (`Web.frontier_broken_weighted`); where the weighting falls faster than every positive level renews, `w (t + 1) (z + R z) < w t z`, every optimum ends its own base at the first step, breaking every dependence on it (`Optimum.frontier_exhausts`, `Web.frontier_exhausts`), and where some level within reach renews faster, none does (`Optimum.frontier_leaves`, `Web.frontier_leaves`; in `Optimum.Holding`, under the logistic law a weighting falling more than twofold a step ends the source, less than twofold holds it at one, and at twofold both are optima, `logistic_weighted`). Where the start lies below a level `y` it cannot reach in one step, and the next level rises with the level below `y`, nothing open rises faster than the course rising as fast as what is given allows (`Optimum.Next.le_rise`); a course gaining, weighted, at least what any open course's level gains is an optimum and every optimum gains what it does (`Optimum.frontier_of_gainsMost`, `Optimum.frontier_gains_of_most`), so where the weighted gain rises with the level up to `y` and is greatest there, the rebuilding course is an optimum (`Optimum.rebuilding_frontier`), and where it rises strictly, every optimum stands at its level, drawing nothing, while it is below `y`, restoring every dependence whose need that level meets (`Optimum.frontier_rebuilds`, `Web.frontier_rebuilds`), and stands at `y` after where `y` gains the most strictly (`Optimum.frontier_rebuilt`; in `Optimum.Holding`, a source doubling up to eight, from one, is rebuilt to two, four and eight by every optimum under weights falling less than twofold, the dweller restored at the first step, `doubling_rebuilds`). Each of these laws uses only what stands to be drawn at each step, so each holds over any law `G` of what stands, a level `z` at the step from `t` giving `G t z`, which may change with the step and give less than the level or more; the renewal law `z + R z` is the case, and holding or rising to `y` needs only `y ≤ G t y` (`Optimum.Next`, `Web.Next`). Where others first draw from the level an optimizer leaves, leaving `K t z`, which renews by `R`, what stands for it is `K t z + R (K t z)` (`Optimum.Next.leftBy`); where what stands at each level is at most the level, every optimum of every order rising with the weighted draw whose weighting falls at every step, however slightly, stands at nothing at every step from the first to the horizon's last (`Optimum.Next.frontier_ends_of_le`, `Optimum.Next.frontier_ends_drawn_first`), and where it is less than every positive level, so does every optimum under positive weights rising at no step (`Optimum.Next.frontier_ends_of_lt`, `Optimum.Next.frontier_ends_drawn_more`), each breaking at the first step every dependence on the base with a positive need met at the start (`Web.Next.frontier_ends_drawn_first`, `Web.Next.frontier_ends_drawn_more`; in `Optimum.Holding`, from two, a source doubling up to eight is rebuilt to four and eight by every optimum alone under weights rising at no step and falling less than twofold, and ended at once by every optimum of the same order with others first drawing all but a quarter of what is left, `doubling_drawn_first`, `doubling_drawn_first_linear`). Where the eligible outcomes are supplied otherwise, without room an optimum need not lower anything (`Optimum.Drain.room_needed`), and an order rising only to a target has the keeping and the ending outcome both among its optima (`Optimum.Drain.satisficing_either`). The drive is Zhuang and Hadfield-Menell's (2020) Theorem 1 in discrete, conserved form (§7.1). Used in §7.1 and §7.2 (law 3).

Selection also revises the lumping it works through: every selection expressible through an old lumping stays expressible through a new one exactly when new indistinguishability implies old, and an instance actually forms a distinguishing relation, replenishes the support used and changes the response. Consequences revise the next reference plan: a failure receipt changes the retained contact sequence and a new record is installed only once a check passes. The integrated account (36 theorems, 62 declarations, empty axiom reports) joins formation and continuing revision in one actual infinite run, broken by cutting the return, freezing the method or exhausting a tight reserve. Recipes, targets, return laws, faithful contact and revision rules are supplied there; continuing activity is not normative warrant, nor perpetual motion from a finite stock ([integrated account](../../meta-attractor/COMMON-ORGANIZATION.md#integrated-account)).

*Readings* [AI reading, D25, D26, D41, D42, D45]. 无明 (wúmíng, “not-seeing”; Sanskrit avidyā), Ren's term, is the not-seeing that keeps seeking locked onto a target that cannot be met at rest. The position every formation selects from grounds it and is not it; the position is why the losses locked seeking makes beyond what couples into it do not reach it through its own feedback. 明 (míng) is coupling that reaches what support does not mark: measurement, a model, or others (§7.3). Knowing enough (知足) is a target met at a state the law leaves unchanged, and knowing where to stop (知止) a target within that boundary, so that a pursuit ends by enough and not by exhaustion. Failing to rest has two sources: the target, which is 无明, and the process, runs that keep changing, which is not 无明 and is named by its structure; knowing a run will not change for ever is not knowing when it has stopped. Capture is a target formed by a selection that rewards what cannot rest and held by a rule read from the share it selects (§4.5); domination itself is not among what is excluded. A runaway is a loop through whose own couplings, before the break, the world is alike with one with as much more stock as one likes. Any selection decided by what couples in (keeping, rewarding, reproducing, training) reaches exactly what couples into the selector; for AI training the reward process is the selector, which the AI connects with the title of Ren's paper “When Capability Outruns Correction”. These readings rest on short theorems; the weight is carried by the readings.

### 3.5 Knowledge and the conditions of inquiry

**In words.** Propositional knowledge is a true judgment whose formation or maintenance is connected, through cognitive capacities, to the actual relations that make it correct, its correctness not accidental ([the chapter on knowledge](KNOWLEDGE.md)). Knowing that, understanding and knowing how are distinct achievements; the account of 8 October 2026 ([history](../history/PHILOSOPHICAL-ACCOUNT.md)) joins them with common inquiry and practical wisdom.

The inquiry conditions checked (periphery, standard Lean axioms, unless marked): a necessary, not sufficient, information bound for finite fallible inquiry; equivalence under branching tests is bisimulation; feasibility is a finite plan branching on observations; derivation is sound (an earlier module, no axioms), and a binding-preserving substitution supplies the original warrant at every position; questions across scales, where even all prefix questions can miss a loss in between; acquired bindings in compositional use; executed instructions transporting evidence; and diagnosability under fallible witnesses, read on the core process of inspection: two candidates are told apart on a complete transcript exactly where their fault envelopes are cross-separated, and a model admitting every witness's failure blocks every later certificate (`diagnosable_iff`, `total_fault_blocks`).

*Accounts of joining* (core restatements of the earlier explanatory modules). How fragments join is told by the questions an account answers. For locally finite, incidence-connected scopes answering alike at the root, the equally answering fragment pairs form a common refinement covering the scopes and keeping joins; a join-preserving map lifting joins at one fixed participant keeps every incidence question, with a unique factor between two such refinements. Two exact reduced accounts of functional or of finite-outcome joining carry the same structure, aligned uniquely on the codes used; joining questions stay exact under the same encoding, and a finite joining tree lifts from its specified fragments; with references retained, configurations over different carriers that answer the branch questions alike match every finite formation sequence. Without an anchor the incidence language loses a real distinction: a coarse account exact and reduced for it can turn a spent history's impossible self-join into a possible one. A target separated by tests, joint tests included, is unique, and exclusive footprints within the parent support every leaf request (`same_target_unique_by_core`, `realized_requests_supported_by_core`).

*Open.* That the complete core is indispensable to scientific inquiry has not been established (§8.1).
