<a id="4-集体动力学--collective-dynamics"></a>
## 4. Collective dynamics

Collective activity is explained from actual relations first. It does not presuppose opposed interests, bargaining, who gains more, or a fixed common utility ([the reminder on common problems](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-08-collective-destructive-attractors.md#common-problem-solving-and-awareness)); actual conflicts and differences remain. A collective is a region of formed relations (§5), not a unit with an interest of its own. None of the laws below is an axiom or says "must"; whether an actual collective meets a law's conditions is empirical (§8.7).

### 4.1 Joint consumption, markets and the meta-attractor

**In words.** Joint consumption has no single consumer: the consumer and the formation that loses support both take part in the drawn condition, and difference-making belongs to the joint activity. Where the influence the successful gain weakens the constraints on the strategies that produced the success, present advantage changes future selection ([meta-attractor](../../meta-attractor/README.md)). The loop in which the rule of selection, and how it is read, are formed from the share it selects is stated in §4.5.

- `consumption_relates`: consumer and loser both take part in the drawn condition; with `jointly_consuming` (§2.1), the class belongs to the joint relation.
- *Joint difference-making*: under overdetermination, withholding any single party's part, the loss happens all the same, while the relational reading still counts each drawer as consuming; in a gradual depletion no finite set of events makes the whole difference, while the breaking event makes the difference to that break.
- *Substitution does not certify renewal*: a party continuing by consumption can turn to other conditions, and the one that lost does not recover (`stepwise_includes_substitution`, §2.1).

So under relational neutrality, a party continuing by consumption cannot place the one it ends outside significance on the ground of unrelatedness (§6.3), and “my share makes no difference” does not remove a drawer from consumption. None of this yields responsibility or duty. Market selection stays within the generative regime only where successful activity renews the conditions on which further exchange and life depend and those affected can still discover damage and change the arrangement ([markets and generative regimes](MARKETS-AND-GENERATIVE-REGIMES.md)); the periphery bounds checked are continued operation under a protection certificate, the exact reach of partial correction, a supported finite transition, and an interval of positive seeds finite support cannot rescue.

Records: [joint-difference-making](../results/records/joint-difference-making.md), [market-protected-continuation](../results/records/market-protected-continuation.md), [market-partial-correction-boundary](../results/records/market-partial-correction-boundary.md), [market-supported-finite-transition](../results/records/market-supported-finite-transition.md), [market-finite-support-obstruction](../results/records/market-finite-support-obstruction.md), [formed-software-continuation](../results/records/formed-software-continuation.md).

### 4.2 Mutual dependence and its pace

**In words.** Read the closures of §2.5 as the whole: a flow shares conditions throughout where every system is the whole, is mutually dependent where every influence closure is, and mutually fed where every feeding closure is. Each gives the one before it and neither converse holds; mutual dependence is not mutual benefit. Where the couplings carrying such dependence act at every step around a loop, the most difference that can arrive grows exponentially both ways.

- `Flow.mutuallyDependent_iff_reach`: a flow is mutually dependent exactly where every reach closure is the whole, so whatever a formation affects can, at some remove, affect it back. The clearer's web shares throughout while the dweller lies beyond the clearer's influence closure (`Clearing.shares_not_mutual`); in `Drawing` two formations each take from what the other requires and neither feeds the other (`Drawing.mutual_not_fed`).
- `Flow.MutuallyDependent.unrestricted`: under each reading, significance bounded by the matching closure is the continuation condition (`Flow.SharesThroughout.unrestricted`, `Flow.MutuallyFed.unrestricted`), extending §2.5's one common condition to any web of mutual feeding. Used in §6.3.
- `exponential_around`: around a loop coupled at every step a chain advances one coupling a step (`CoupledAlways.chainInTime`), R turns multiply what reached the condition by at least 2^R (`carried_pow_around`), and with strengths at most M and gaps at most D it is at most D·(1 + M)^t (`carried_le_pow_always`); from every condition to every condition where couplings are mutual at every step (`CoupledThroughout.exponential`). A selection with transmission raises the carriers' ratio at least as (a/b)^n (`share_ratio_pow`).
- `Flow.Balances.half_stands`: under balance, with nothing given, a draw exceeding everything drawn before it leaves more than half the starting stock at every step it is met, and lasting n steps needs a stock of at least 2^n − 1 (`Flow.Balances.doubling_needs`); in `DoublingDraw` four of seven units stand at the last step, then none.

*Readings* [AI reading, D39, revised 19 October 2026]. “A highly entangled system” reads as mutual dependence, sharing throughout the weaker reading and mutual feeding the stronger; every link of affecting then lies on a loop through the whole. “Exponential pace” reads as growth around loops acting at every step, and in a selection as growth of the share ratio. “Little time to react” reads as `half_stands` with `Web.break_not_bounded`: what stands gives no warning. That a dependence acts as a coupling by level with positive strength at every step is supplied; whether the Earth is so is not measured.

Records: [mutual-dependence-and-its-pace](../results/records/mutual-dependence-and-its-pace.md).

<a id="what-a-collectives-continuation-needs"></a>
### 4.3 Relative position, and what a collective's continuation needs

**In words.** A part's position read against the others' sums to zero by counting: its rise is the rest's summed fall, and no change raises every part. Position does not protect: a joint excess ends a part at its end through its dependence, whatever its position. A collective's continuation is asked of runs, not of positions: keeping a part is not renewal, there is no number of the whole, conditions are joined rather than multiplied, a counter-loop has to reach how the rule reads, and the invariants are laws, not axioms.

- `RelativePosition.change_balances`: across every change of amounts a part's rise in position is the rest's summed fall, no change raises every part (`RelativePosition.not_all_rise`), and every part can draw more while one falls (`DrawingMore.grow_and_fall`; on a flow with no hypothesis, `Flow.drawn_positions`).
- `Flow.ends_at_race_end`: with balance, a joint excess that every part joins ends a part at its end exactly through its dependence; with renewal gated the greatest parts end with the condition for good (`Flow.race_ends_greatest`, `Flow.greatest_end_for_good`; in `Race` the part that rises in position ends with the rest). Ungated renewal restores the part (`Restored.returns`); a greatest part that does not depend on what it draws down continues (`Clearing.greatest_continues`), and what it ends is a continuation with significance only under a significance (`Clearing.loss_needs_significance`): under the condition it counts from every standpoint, under none it does not.
- `Renewable.kept_part_not_renewal`: no course leaves the guard “the focal formation is active” from the start, while one run renews and another consumes the sustaining formation's condition; whether no course leaves a guard is a fact of the configuration, renewing a fact of the run.
- `Viability.kernel_and_iff` with `ChoicePoint.no_rule_decides_joint`: where every course is counted, no course leaves two guards together exactly where no course leaves either, the whole truth of “any factor at zero makes the product zero”; where continuation goes through choices, each condition can be kept while the two cannot (`ChoicePoint.each_not_joint`), and no rule on the separate answers decides the joint one.

The account of social viability brought to the theory ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-19-collective-continuation.md)) lists eight constraints and a ninth that unifies them, keeping the capacity to correct. Each is restated as laws of what a course does under stated conditions, holding in cells, bodies, ecosystems, institutions, networks and collectives of intelligences wherever these are so represented:

| Constraint | Law | Limit |
|---|---|---|
| 1. Material reproduction | Recurring uptake needs replenishment that never stops for good (§2.2); `falls_not_recurring`; a reserve defers the end (`Becoming.Reserve`); persistence, success and growth do not certify renewal (`Renewable.class_belongs_to_the_run`). | No finite history decides the class (`Becoming.Horizon`). |
| 2. Rewarding what erodes the whole | A rise of what a group couples into is paid from beyond (`Flow.Balances.rise_paid_from_beyond`); carriers leaving more offspring raise their share (`share_rises`); joint consumption has no single consumer (§4.1). | Whether what is drawn from beyond is depended on is open. |
| 3. Information true to the world | A selection separates exactly what its selector couples apart (`Web.selects_alike_iff`, by definition); a wrong model excludes what holds (`Stationary.model_error`); measurement, models and pooling widen what reaches a selection (§7.3). | A difference reaches a measurement only along routes of reading (`apart_only_downstream`). |
| 4. Cooperation among strangers | Giving that waits on giving (`Becoming.Withholding`); thresholds and restoring laws (`Becoming.Tolerance`) (§4.4). | Amounts of giving, thresholds with memory and how a restoring law forms are open. |
| 5. Bounded conflict | A loop closes a difference only through a link giving back less than it received (`Becoming.LoopReturn`); a factor, what one turn multiplies a difference by, is not a strength, what a link gives per unit of the level it reads: a self-coupled condition with strength `M` has factor `1 + M` (`LoopReturn.selfLoop_carried`). The gap of two rejoining courses at one condition is a return map's course exactly where their exchanges respond to it by that map (`LoopReturn.responds_iff`, `LoopReturn.gap_course`), which in Rejoining's witness holds from the act on and not across it (`LoopReturn.replenishing_no_return`). | Real factors are open, and so is a law of exchange whose response is a map of the gap rather than a supplied premise. |
| 6. Feedback on power | Every return at least doubles the carried amount (`carried_doubles_roundTrip`) and frequent returns outgrow every polynomial (`runaway_of_roundTrip`); around a cycle the course with factor one plus the product of strengths stays at or below the carried amount (`LoopReturn.carried_around_course`), strictly on every ring of two or more conditions (`LoopReturn.Ring.bound_strict`), and where each link gives exactly its strength equality holds exactly where the loop gains nothing over the turn and the intermediate conditions carry nothing on (`LoopReturn.around_course_iff`); whole factors keep at 1 and run away at 2 or more (`LoopReturn.whole_factors`); “above all others” never rests for two (`Law.aboveAll_never_rests`); a change to the state alone is re-absorbed (`Law.perturbation_reabsorbed`); where the rule reads the share it selects, only a change of how it reads leaves (`Becoming.RuleCapture`), and a loss the loop does not read leaves it locked (`ClosedCapture.lock_outlives_loss`). | The speed of a runaway with real factors is open; whether an actual rule reads the share it selects is empirical. |
| 7. Stability with adaptability | How fast a rule may change (`Becoming.Pace`); a level held from both sides settles where the response does not overshoot, and a rule reading `ℓ` steps late needs a band at least `2 (ℓ + 1) d` wide (`Becoming.Regulation`); one reader keeps several levels exactly where it reads each within its window, and what it can keep is a pinwheel density (`Becoming.ReadingSchedule`). | The lumping of many levels into one, noisy rules and one policy keeping a band from every start under late reading are open. |
| 8. Forming the next members | A pattern outlasting its carriers lasts only through passing (`Becoming.PatternPassing`). | That carrying forms by passing is supplied. |
| Keeping the capacity to correct | A difference closes only through a formation that couples the two courses apart and exchanges differently (`Flow.closes_through_coupling_apart`); what nothing within the responsive reach exchanges with stays (`Flow.stays_beyond_response`); 明 is coupling joined to a selection (§7.3); a loop closes only through a link giving back less. | Differences at many conditions at once are open. |

*Readings* [AI reading, D48]. A collective can keep itself by consuming others, and continuing fed from beyond is a dependence on what lies beyond, not an independence from it; a society that oppression or extraction keeps going for centuries is kept, not renewing. “Local gain above zero, effect on the whole below zero” is located, not summed: the loss lies at a dependence that breaks, at a position, and ranking basic conditions first excludes every natural-number score of them (`Becoming.ScoreRepresentation`, §6.5). `Viability = Perception × Coordination × Correction × Regeneration` is a heuristic: the joint question has to be asked of the joint course. Positive feedback on power and wealth is a loop whose rule is read from the share it rewards; term limits, audits, courts and antitrust are couplings that carry what an actor's acts do back into what selects the rule, stated as pooling couplings (§7.3–§7.4), not as interests set against each other, and capability is a reason to pool, not to govern. The account's last step, a small set of “system survival axioms”, runs the other way from the theory: each invariant is a law of one unfolding under local conditions, read in each domain by a correspondence; homeostasis, checks and balances, the regulation of what reaches beyond, fault tolerance and carrying capacity are readings of these laws ([study §193](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#193-what-a-collectives-continuation-needs-v)). Joint keeping of several conditions is in [conditions-kept-together](../results/records/conditions-kept-together.md).

Records: [relative-position-is-zero-sum](../results/records/relative-position-is-zero-sum.md), [conditions-kept-together](../results/records/conditions-kept-together.md), [protection-priority-excludes-uniform-aggregation](../results/records/protection-priority-excludes-uniform-aggregation.md).

### 4.4 Giving that waits on giving, tolerance, loops, pace and regulation

**In words.** Where giving waits on giving, withholding with memory stays and spreads, and without memory it returns around every loop; more waiting never gives more. For every law that keeps inclusion the worst start is total withholding, and from it only a formation giving whatever it waits on restores, exactly into the formations every long enough chain of waits passes it. A loop closes a difference only through a link giving back less than it received. A rule changing only at reading steps fixes what it holds at stretches of 2N − 1, and a level held from both sides settles exactly where the response does not overshoot.

- `withholding_absorbs` with `withholding_returns`: with memory one withholding ends all giving within `d` waits from step `d` on, and withholding stays and spreads (`waitsAll_withholding_stays`, `withholding_spreads`); without memory it returns around every loop at every multiple of its length; under both laws more waiting never gives more (`waitsAll_more_waiting`, `answers_more_waiting`). In a ring of three a formation giving whatever it waits on restores all from the third step, and no earlier (`Ring.givesFirst_restores_exact`).
- `worst_start` with `restores_iff`: for every law keeping inclusion a formation gives at step `n` from every start exactly where it does from total withholding; under the tolerating law (`tolerating`; tolerance zero is waiting on all) total withholding is kept without a formation sparing all it waits on (`total_stays`), and with one giving first and sources decided, a formation gives from every start exactly where no chain of `n` waits clear of sources leads into it, a loop clear of sources never restored (`clear_loop_withholds`). Tolerance absorbs without restoring (`kept_set`, `thin_absorbed`, `Triad.total_kept`); the fault-envelope certificate read as a law of giving keeps a set giving exactly where envelopes are separated (`certified_available_iff`); on a web the tolerance law is a run (`web_tolerating`).
- `closing_needs_less`: where every link gives on at least what it receives the difference never falls (`never_closes_of_no_less`); a return map below what it receives closes within as many turns as the difference, exactly (`closes_within_exact`); a cut closes at once only where every later link gives nothing for nothing (`cut_closes`, `cut_reopened`); with whole-number factors a loop closes at the first turn, keeps, or runs away (`nothing_between`), and closing gradually needs a link that is not a multiplication (`gradual_needs_another_link`). The self-loop's return map is the carried amount (`selfLoop_carried`, `selfLoop_gap`); around a cycle of several links giving exactly their strengths one turn is that course with what the loop gained and what the intermediate conditions carried on (`carried_around_excess`), so equality fails on every ring of two or more conditions (`Ring.bound_strict`); a gap at one condition of rejoining courses is a return map's course exactly where the exchanges respond to it (`responds_iff`), and then these laws apply to it (`gap_course`, `gap_scaling`). This answers for loops §7.7's question which law closes rather than widens a difference.
- `pace_law`: a two-level course is a run of one law (`paced_run`); a rule that never reads is unreached and loses what it loses (`rule_unreached`, `never_reads_loses`); a fast difference reaches the rule only at a reading step (`rule_until_reading`), regaining what a fixed rule loses needs a change of rule (`Switching.changing_regains`), and a stretch of `L` steps fixes every pair of held-rule courses with at most `N` states exactly when `L ≥ 2N − 1` (from §3.3); a course read whole is fixed by its first `N + 1` steps (`whole_fixed_exact`).
- `Flow.withinGap_iff_between` with `Regulation.no_narrower_band`: with balance the exchange departs from evenness by at most the gap exactly where the next level lies between `m` and the level, the bound being exactly no overshoot; within it, opposing, the level settles at `m` (`Flow.regulates`, `Flow.settles`, `Flow.restores_exactly`), and beyond it an opposing exchange crosses `m` (`Regulation.Overshooting.oscillates`; one-sided restoring stays away, `Regulation.OneSided.above`). A rule reading `ℓ` steps late keeps no band narrower than `2 (ℓ + 1) d`, attained by the late return (`Regulation.lateReturn_band`); a late relay overshoots by `ℓ M` and settles exactly where `a + (ℓ + 1) M ≤ b + 1` (`Regulation.relay_threshold`).
- `ReadingSchedule.keeps_iff_within`: one reader for several levels. A schedule reads one condition a step, and a draw on a condition is withdrawn `ℓ` steps after a step that reads it. With `(a + ℓ) d ≤ m < (a + 1 + ℓ) d`, the schedule keeps the condition within the margin `m`, against every balanced flow's draws of at most `d` a step, exactly where it reads it within every `a` steps (`Flow.Balances.retains_of_read`; the witness `ReadingSchedule.within_of_keeps`). What one reader keeps is then a question of windows (§7.3; [study §310](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#310-one-reader-several-conditions-keeping-is-reading-within-the-window-and-what-one-reader-can-keep-rs1)).

Not derived: that a given system regulates, how late it reads and its step; one policy keeping a band from every start; the lumping of several regulated levels into one; real factors.

*Readings* [AI reading, D48]. Cooperation among strangers is giving that waits on giving across chains longer than any acquaintance; more waiting never giving more is the structural price of complexity. The spiral “others will deceive me, so I deceive first” is withholding with memory, which absorbs; around a loop without memory withholding returns. Kept promises, enforced contracts and institutions acting whatever was last received are laws that give first: the law changes, the state alone does not. Tolerance is the slack that lets cooperation survive scattered defection, and it does not rebuild trust lost everywhere; a cycle of mutual reliance with no source giving first stays withheld. An exchange of harms is a loop: answering exactly in kind keeps the difference, answering more runs away, and a conflict closes only where some link gives back less (compensation, arbitration, absorption, or a cut followed by links giving nothing for nothing); with whole-number factors nothing closes gradually, which is why the account's conflict institutions are links of another kind. Stability with adaptability is a slow level reading the fast one at stretches of at least `2N − 1`, as constitution is slower than law and law than policy; that this layering is good is the comparison's to say, not the law's.

Records: [giving-that-waits-on-giving](../results/records/giving-that-waits-on-giving.md), [restoring-giving-from-the-worst-start](../results/records/restoring-giving-from-the-worst-start.md), [thresholds-of-waiting](../results/records/thresholds-of-waiting.md), [whether-a-loop-closes-a-difference](../results/records/whether-a-loop-closes-a-difference.md), [how-fast-a-rule-may-change](../results/records/how-fast-a-rule-may-change.md), [two-sided-regulation-and-late-reading](../results/records/two-sided-regulation-and-late-reading.md), [one-reader-several-conditions](../results/records/one-reader-several-conditions.md). Study: [section 224](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#224-joins-r33).

### 4.5 Passing, shared codes and capture through the rule

**In words.** A pattern outlasting its carriers lasts only through passing, and ends within a span once passing stops. A code formed between formations by hearing makes no form: the start and the order of hearing fix the form reached, no form is privileged, and only a centre, a preferred form or an order of hearing breaks a symmetric disagreement. Where a rule of selection is read from the share it selects, what holds the outcome is how the rule reads, not the rule's value, which every reading step sets again. Where how the rule is read is itself formed from the share, and nothing in the loop reads what lies beyond the share, the loop is closed: a loss it does not read leaves it locked however large the loss grows, a change of the reading is absorbed as a change of the rule's value is, and what leaves is a change of the law, such as a reading that reaches what lies beyond the share.

- `Web.ByPassing.ends_within`: a pattern present from `T` on whose carriers carry it briefly has a carrier beginning within every `d` steps (`Web.begins_within`, in double-negation form), under the passing premise every beginning is a passing (`Web.ByPassing.begins_passed`), a step with no carrier is followed by none (`Web.ByPassing.empty_stays`), and where passing stops the pattern ends within `d` steps; `PatternRelay` attains each bound. The passing premise is supplied, as transmission is (§2.4).
- `run_agrees_every_start_iff`: hearing makes no form (`run_held`), agreement is kept exactly through the hearings closed for it (`kept_iff`), an order brings every start to agreement exactly where it gives every formation one origin, and any form in use can become the shared one (`run_shared_iff_in_use`). On a path whose ends each follow the least-indexed neighbour, the ends never agree (`Path.never_agree`); a law privileging neither forms nor formations never resolves a symmetric disagreement (`never_agrees_of_both`, `TwoFormations.three_agree`). An imposed code merges what codes told apart, and only a form kept outside brings it back (`outside_needed`); a reading written back into records rests at once and is barred only by records kept from before (`kept_bars`, `added_never_replaced`).
- `favouring_locks` with `uniform_locks`: where a selection does not lower a target's share under a rule that favours it (`Raises`) and the rule read at a reading step is a monotone function of the share (`ReadsShare`), with no rule turning against the reading the share falls at no step and the course is locked from the step after its first reading step; where the reading favours at every share, every course is locked from the step after its second, exactly (`Second.second_exact`). A perturbation keeping the rule's direction is absorbed at the next reading step (`perturbation_absorbed`), one reversing it and held past the threshold locks the other way (`Reversed.not_absorbed`), and monotone two-level laws keep the order of courses (`order_kept`). This mirrors `Law.perturbation_reabsorbed`: there the state is re-absorbed because the step does not read it, here the rule's value because the reading does not. That an actual rule reads the share it selects, monotonically, is the premise `ReadsShare`; it is where “the winner shapes the rule” of `Becoming.Capture` enters, as a premise, not a conclusion.
- `ClosedCapture.lock_outlives_loss`: read the fast configuration as the share and what lies beyond it. Where neither the next share nor the rule read reads what lies beyond (`Unread`), courses from starts alike in share and rule stay alike in both at every step (`unread_course`), and exactly then along every schedule (`courses_alike_iff_unread`): this is the law of a part read alone (`Law.part_identifiesAlike_iff_step`) on the two-level step, what the loop reads making sense by itself exactly where nothing beyond the share is read (`identifiesAlike_iff_unread`); and the share's law joined with a step beyond runs exactly as the share's law alone (`join_course`); so from a locked configuration a loss rising by at least one a step passes every bound while the course stays locked and neither share nor rule falls below the start. A reading that favours at no loss above a bound turns at the step after the first reading step past it (`turned_by_loss`); in `Beyond` the same selection from the same start stays locked read from the share and, read from the loss, favours the others from the second step with the share falling at the third (`Beyond.closed_locked`, `Beyond.open_turned`, `Beyond.open_falls`). Where how the rule is read is itself formed from the share (`mind`) and the rule read through it (`read`), rule and reading are one rule read from the share (`both_reads`): locked, none of share, rule and reading falls (`readers_not_below`), a change of the reading of any size, either way, is absorbed at the next reading step (`reading_absorbed`), and where the rule read favours at every reading every course locks from its second reading step (`readers_uniform_locks`); `Proportional` meets every premise (`Proportional.reform_absorbed`). That the reading is formed from the share is the premise `ReadsShare` on `mind`, not a conclusion.

*Readings* [AI reading, D48, D56, D57, D58]. Forming the next members is passing a pattern across brief carriers, and passing carries whatever it carries: patterns of capture pass as patterns of care do; nothing in the law says which are worth passing. The second-order failure the account names, corruption taking over courts, press, statistics and audit, is a loop whose rule-reading reaches the couplings that would carry the difference back, the routes of 明 themselves, and the third order, rewarding those who break the routes of correction, a selection favouring narrowing what couples in, so that the gap of §7.2 widens as capability grows. “Stable is not good” is the theory's own starting point. 名无固宜，约定俗成谓之宜 is the order of use breaking the symmetry, 荀子's 王者制名 a centre, 书同文 an imposed code whose merges stay unless a form is kept outside (“古文由此絕”), and the 說文's 止戈为武 a reading written back into its own evidence, barred only by the bone and bronze forms in which 止 is a foot. 韩非子's 二柄 (「今君人者释其刑德而使臣用之，则君反制于臣矣」) is a rule of selection read from the share it selects, 韩非's framing of it as control between opposed interests kept as a divergence; 管子's 据有余而制不足 is the proportional reading; 商君书's 壹赏、壹刑、壹教 is one reading favouring one way at every share. The corpus's meta-attractor (元吸引子) is this loop closed: the winners of one round writing the next round's scorecard is `ReadsShare`, the winners forming the minds the scorecard will later read is `ReadsShare` on `mind`, and “the metric survives its own refutation” is `lock_outlives_loss`: the refutation lies beyond the share, and nothing in the loop reads it. Goodhart's law says a measure made a target stops measuring; the loop says why it is kept: what keeps it reads the share it gives, not what it fails to measure. What leaves is a change of the law, such as 明, a reading reaching what the share does not carry (§7.3), not reform of the state, the rule's value or the readers alone, since the share forms each of them again. That actual rules and readers are so formed is the premise, and that readers are minds is a reading. None of these says whether a shared, imposed or locked code or loop supports or damages 生生.

Records: [a-pattern-lasts-through-passing](../results/records/a-pattern-lasts-through-passing.md), [a-code-formed-between-formations](../results/records/a-code-formed-between-formations.md), [capture-through-the-rule](../results/records/capture-through-the-rule.md), [closed-capture](../results/records/closed-capture.md), [joint-practice-forms-continuation](../results/records/joint-practice-forms-continuation.md), [joint-plan-composition](../results/records/joint-plan-composition.md), [organizational-formation-from-generative-comparison](../results/records/organizational-formation-from-generative-comparison.md).

### 4.6 Responsive transformation

**In words.** Understanding, purposes and action are reorganized through changing relations and consequences (因应转化): activity alters the conditions of further activity, some effects become available to discernment and memory, and acquired understanding enters evaluation and changed practice. A warranted direction is not an assured outcome: a local mechanism can guarantee an effect within a range without guaranteeing that the range, its interpretation or the reasons for it persist through every later change.

The relations to examine are consequences becoming discernible, acquired grounds staying applicable, understanding changing operative purposes and practice, support continuing through a transition, and evaluation staying answerable to living consequences; each has its own standing, and together they are not a proved checklist. The direction is not the maximization of change, flexibility, information or compliance: constant revision can destroy the continuity learning needs, and persistence can sustain care or entrench a mistake. Revising an interpretation of the good gives no AI authority to exempt itself from warranted oversight; the bridge to corrigibility is the relational capacity for such transformation, including forming and revising the judgment about what should change. The statement of 9 October 2026, with its table of conditions: [history](../history/PLURAL-SYSTEMS-AND-CORRECTION.md#15-responsive-transformation).

Records: [acquired-revision-of-pending-task](../results/records/acquired-revision-of-pending-task.md), [versioned-request-completion](../results/records/versioned-request-completion.md), [pending-task-context-applicability](../results/records/pending-task-context-applicability.md), [formed-goal-revision-continuation](../results/records/formed-goal-revision-continuation.md).

<a id="5-评价的形成--how-evaluation-forms"></a>
## 5. How evaluation forms

**In words.** Positions, their regions and significance all form within the unfolding. A position's own region is a region of formed relations, not an owner, and the significance `sig` is the one evaluative parameter supplied. A standard's gaining influence, its recognizing its target, its being warranted and its being followed are four distinct questions; a formed standard can be wrong, and learning a disposition is not learning a reason. The represented dynamics contain no significance, so they do not entail any.

- `Web.selfValuation_own_least`: read with the formations as the standpoints, a formation's own region is its closure under a relation of the unfolding, its least boundary, and `living` and `related` are the web's stepwise reading; along the empty relation the own region is the formation's own events, along feeding the feeding closure (`Web.selfValuation_empty_own`, `Web.selfValuation_feeding_own`). Which relation a center's region is drawn along is read off the unfolding, not derived. Used in §6.2.
- `Web.significance_undetermined`: on any web with an event that lives, with the web unchanged, living significance holds where everything has significance and fails where nothing has. The theorem evaluates these two constant significances only. That no law of the dynamics constrains significance holds meta-theoretically, because significance is not a field of `World`, `Web` or `Flow`; it is not a stated theorem. It shows non-entailment by the represented dynamics; it is not evidence that becoming leaves significance undetermined, and it does not show that the condition is needed. This is the one place the point is stated; later sections cite it.
- `LivingSignificance`: realized living content falls under a relevance; read from significance, the relevance is the significance of realized living content, with no valence (`Frame.relevance`, `relevance_least`). At the level of claims every living claim has significance from every standpoint (`Valuation.Significant`). It is a condition, characterized with no premise in §6.2, and the condition of §6.4 restricted to living claims.

The coupling laws read one further supplied relation, `obs`, where a level couples into a formation by level, the second relation supplied beside the web's dependence (§2.6, §8.1). It derives from dependence wherever the response rises with the level or the level has a complement at a fixed total, and is a second relation only for a response falling with a level nothing complements (`carried_iff_rising`, `Complement.carry`, OB1).

*Routes of formation.* Evaluative uptake: acquired understanding forms evaluative influence and enters warranted application ([evaluative uptake](../history/EVALUATIVE-UPTAKE.md)). A warranted response under conditions: an initially missing evaluative connection can become the mediator of a warranted response to another being, and the same associative learning can later suppress the response without overturning the reason; the warrant rests on a defended normative premise, the repair principle of [evaluative grounding](../history/EVALUATIVE-GROUNDING.md) (an actor answerable for an identifiable uncompensated deprivation of another being's independently warranted resources or capabilities has an actor-addressed reason for appropriate repair), stated at a bounded scope and not derived from the dynamics. The evaluator route: self-good, with unrestricted monotonicity and tolerance, gives a standpoint-indexed instance of living significance on a single history, narrowed (periphery), and is unified in §6.2.

*Readings of comparison.* Comparing joint compositions needs relational information; single possibilities are not enough (periphery). Under the relational reading C ranks a consuming formation below its absence, while the wide reading by events does not decide (an earlier module, no axioms). Priority of basic conditions is independent of C and excludes every natural-number score (periphery). Comparison while the classification is undecided is in its record (periphery).

Records: [own-good-to-reachable-living-significance](../results/records/own-good-to-reachable-living-significance.md), [relational-generative-comparison](../results/records/relational-generative-comparison.md), [relational-reading-comparison](../results/records/relational-reading-comparison.md), [protection-priority-excludes-uniform-aggregation](../results/records/protection-priority-excludes-uniform-aggregation.md), [classification-robust-generative-comparison](../results/records/classification-robust-generative-comparison.md), [metaethical-core-characterization](../results/records/metaethical-core-characterization.md).

<a id="6-universal-metaethical-core--普遍元伦理核心"></a>
<a id="6-universal-metaethical-core"></a>
## 6. Significance, comparison and practical judgment

The theory asserts no ethical axiom. §6.1–§6.3 hold with no premise about significance, or for any significance and any valuation, with standpoints of any type (`Pos`; the formations are the instance `Pos := F`, §5). The continuation condition enters only in §6.4, where every theorem said to hold “under the condition” takes `Unrestricted` as a hypothesis; the theory asserts it nowhere. §6.5 says where ordering lies. Readings marked [AI reading, Dn] are recorded in the [decision register](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/DELEGATED-CHOICES.md) with what in the work would change them; readings of Ren's words in this section that carry no number are of the same kind.

<a id="613-生成的空间不用公理p56-the-generative-space-with-no-axiom-p56"></a>
<a id="613-生成的空间不用公理--the-generative-space-with-no-axiom"></a>
<a id="comparison-content-under-regrouping"></a>
<a id="614-比较内容怎样由关系约束p91-relational-constraints-on-comparison-content-p91"></a>
<a id="614-比较内容怎样由关系约束--relational-constraints-on-comparison-content"></a>
<a id="613-the-generative-space-with-no-axiom"></a>
<a id="614-relational-constraints-on-comparison-content"></a>
### 6.1 The generative space, with no premise

**In words.** Possibilities are what can form next from what exists. One course widens another when its levels admit every formation the other admits and, at some step, one more; a part is generative where the levels with it widen those without it, degenerative where the reverse. Widening is an order with no premise, and a part does one of four things: widen, narrow, keep or be mixed. The boundary is exact: a part closes something only by a net draw beyond the margin by which the levels without it meet a need, and opens something only by a net give. No significance, standpoint or boundary enters.

- `Web.Widens.trans` with `Web.Widens.not_reverse`: widening goes one way and is transitive, and the four cases exclude one another with no classical logic (`Web.Widens.not_keeps`, `Web.Widens.not_mixed`, `Web.Keeps.not_mixed`). That every part falls under one of the four is the classical step, not stated in Lean. Witnesses: a giver from beyond widens (`Inflow.widens`), the clearer narrows (`Clearing.narrows`), a conveyor is mixed (`Transfer.mixed`); mixed parts are not ordered by inclusion, and ordering them needs a measure, a further premise.
- `supported_iff_within_margin` with `Balances.withheld`: withhold a part with the rest of the exchange as it was; the levels without it are unique where they exist, and each level with what the part took equals the level without it with what the part gave, so a formation is supported exactly when the part's cumulative net draw stays within the margin (`supported_of_net_give`, `admits_of_net_draw`; finding the condition uses classical logic). A part giving back at least what it takes closes nothing; one giving back exactly what it takes changes nothing. Where requirements do not loosen, a formation gains support only through a rise given by someone supported (`gains_from_supported`), and where nothing beyond gives, the unsupported stays so (`unsupported_stays`, each premise needed by `Restoring`). Used in §6.4 (the boundary of not harm).
- `Flow.widens_iff_within_margin_at`: with balance only at the conditions each formation requires before the step, the levels with a part widen those without it exactly when its net draw stays within the margin for every formation they admit and, at some step, its net exchange brings within the margin one they do not; the boundaries of retaining, losing and adding what has significance hold alike, for any significance (`Flow.retains_iff_within_margin_at`, `Flow.loses_iff_beyond_margin_at`, `Flow.adds_iff_within_margin_at`).
- `Web.paths_comparison_widens`: on level paths an extension with every claim counted is exactly a widening (`Web.paths_extension_iff_widens`), so with “better” read as widening C holds for every reading of `living`. The proof is an unfolding: the normative content of C has moved into calling widening better, which this theorem does not show (§6.5, §8.5). Used in §6.5 and §7.5.
- `Web.includes_iff_within_every`: the comparison over the whole is the comparison within every region at once; a region's comparison takes the region as an input, the whole's takes no boundary. What holds within a region holds within every region inside it, and nothing lies beyond the whole (`Web.AdmitsWithin.narrow`). The clearer, active and supported throughout, closes nothing within itself and its source and closes the dweller beyond them (`Clearing.loses_beyond`, a witness for that “can” in one constructed world with the clearer stipulated active): “only non-degenerating dynamics continue indefinitely” fails for parts, while the whole has nowhere to push the loss.

*Represented boundaries.* A formation renews over a region where it is active and keeps every dependence on the region's conditions it takes part in: over a region covering everything it takes part in this is renewal at the step; over its required conditions it keeps its own support yet may undermine another; over a region omitting a required condition it can lose its own support (`Omitted`). A center and its boundary are formations and relations within the unfolding: the dynamics fix the extent a center must count, not the center (`Clearing.center_not_derived`, §6.2), so a region is a parameter of the statement, with no status outside the unfolding. The whole is never any center's model of it: no configuration of a frame whose claims are its own configurations realizes every pattern of claims about them (`Frame.self_incomplete`, §3.3).

*What widening counts.* `Web.Widens` compares which members of the given type of formations `F` the levels admit at the web's thresholds (§0, block A), so a course that brings about a kind of formation or a requirement outside `F` widens nothing on this measure, and 生生, read as creating the conditions and generators of further forming, can exceed what widening registers; Along a reading of one world's formations in another's, widening compares the two (`Web.WidensAlong`; along the identity it is `Web.Widens`, `Web.widensAlong_id_iff`), and a grown world widens the old along the inclusion exactly where the old admissions are kept and either an old formation gains admission or a new one is admitted (`Web.grow_widensAlong_iff`): with the old levels kept, a growth that admits a new formation widens while on the old formations nothing widens (`Web.grow_widens_unregistered`), and an attachment as in `Flow.GrownBy` (§2.5) widens exactly where its new condition meets the new formation's need (`Web.attach_widensAlong_iff`). Between two webs the reading is supplied; within one unfolding it is derived: across a stretch from one step to a later one the levels widen along a continuation where every formation they admit before is linked to one they admit after and one admitted after is linked to none admitted before (`Web.WidensAcross`), which is widening as named where each formation continues itself (`Web.exceedsAcross_itself_iff`), is unchanged by every renaming carrying the continuation (`Web.widensAcross_reidentify`), and is harder to exceed with more links (`Web.ExceedsAcross.anti`), so a formation read as budding from one already admitted adds nothing (`Bud.readings`). *Regrouping* keeps the process and only merges claims: where members of a group hold alike in the configurations read, each content is recoverable and the extension comparison is unchanged; where requirements are decidable merging is lossless under every level test exactly when members have the same least requirement at every step, which characterizes admitted content, not the identity of lives; two feasible courses of one law of exchange show a grouped description making mixed gains and losses look alike, an omission of a description, not a refutation ([argument and counter-review](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ETHICAL-LEARNING-INTEGRATION.md#relational-comparison-content)). *The open-form result*: `Becoming.Handoff` connects the finite formation witness to the common definition of positive relevance (`Run.novel_by_handoff`, `Run.relevance_by_handoff_of_unrestricted`), with the significance condition an explicit hypothesis; a formal connection, not a new principle ([completion audit](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/RISK-CONDITIONING-INTEGRATION.md#spine-bridge-completion)).

*Readings* [AI reading, D1, D2, D14]. On 10 October 2026 Ren wrote that being generative is that “its existence creates more possibilities of becoming - more future, not less”, and that “"not harm" - this is a phase transition with a clear boundary, not some "outside will or belief"” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-axiom-route-levels-and-possibilities.md)); on 11 October he asked whether “for anything, its continuation into the future leads to more futures(flourishes/plurality) from generatively” could let the theory “derive everything without axiom” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-generative-space-no-axiom.md)). Read so, comparing futures adds no premise, since it compares what the levels admit; widening, narrowing and keeping read generative, degenerative and neutral; the regions read self, system and whole and the boundary a center represents; and the phase boundary is the margin of a part's own net exchange, with no will or belief from outside the dynamics. 生生 reads as generation generating generation, its direction compared by widening; comparison uses no reading of `living`. “More futures” works as the definition of a region, not as a fact about everything: some parts lessen what can form. The comparison counts formations admitted, not renewal capacity or the richness of relations.

Records: [support-from-the-supported](../results/records/support-from-the-supported.md), [closing-needs-net-draw](../results/records/closing-needs-net-draw.md), [four-cases-of-a-part](../results/records/four-cases-of-a-part.md), [loss-beyond-a-region](../results/records/loss-beyond-a-region.md), [represented-boundary-against-dependence](../results/records/represented-boundary-against-dependence.md), [comparison-by-widening](../results/records/comparison-by-widening.md), [comparison-content-under-regrouping](../results/records/comparison-content-under-regrouping.md), [open-form-realization-under-revision](../results/records/open-form-realization-under-revision.md).

<a id="67-从想要延续者重述ren-的提议-restating-from-what-wants-its-continuation-rens-proposal"></a>

<a id="61-what-living-significance-says"></a>
<a id="62-minimality-and-the-shape-of-rivals"></a>
<a id="63-connection-and-consumption-from-the-unfolding"></a>
<a id="64-reasons-addressed-to-every-position"></a>
<a id="67-restating-from-what-wants-its-continuation"></a>
### 6.2 What living significance says, for any significance

**In words.** On a connected whole with some standpoint and some living claim, living significance for every standpoint is exactly a seed (some living claim significant from some standpoint) with two boundary neutralities: relational (significance passes along every formed relation to a living claim) and standpoint (what has significance from one standpoint has it from every one). Where every living claim is held by some standpoint, it is exactly self-significance at every standpoint with standpoint neutrality. Every premise is needed, and each rival keeps the same rule inside a boundary already formed. The dynamics fix the extent a center must count, not the center.

- `Valuation.significant_iff`: the characterization. Each neutrality is the rule holding at every instance (`Neutral`), so everything living significance adds beyond what an evaluator holds lies in the two neutralities; the direction to the premises is a projection, and the content is the converse and minimality. Any position holding self-significance (`Valuation.SelfSignificance`) with living content of its own supplies a seed (`Valuation.Seed`), which is weaker.
- `Model.seed_is_needed` with `Model.connection_is_needed`: dropping the seed, either neutrality, connection or A each leaves a consistent countermodel (`Model.relational_neutrality_is_needed`, `Model.standpoint_neutrality_is_needed`, `Model.articulation_neutrality_is_needed`). The rival holds the rule only within its own region, its own standpoint, or those who endorse; the present-aim evaluator, with no seed, values nothing; a living claim outside every formed relation is reached by no transmission. That every rival is the same rule inside a boundary is a framing of these countermodels, not a theorem about all rivals. For rivals that weigh what can form additively it is exact: a weighting that counts every formation ranks as C does wherever C ranks and decides only mixed comparisons, and one that gives some formation weight zero is a boundary (`Weighting.widens_iff_every_positive`, `Weighting.weighed_eq_of_unweighted`; [THE-STEP-OF-INVARIANCE §6.5](THE-STEP-OF-INVARIANCE.md#65-graded-status-priority-and-constraints)). Non-additive orders, priority weighted by how well off each is in the outcome, and deontic constraints beyond not closing are not modelled (§8.9), and Sidgwick's dualism of practical reason is located, not dissolved, at boundary neutrality.
- `Valuation.significant_iff_held`: where every living claim is held (`Valuation.Held`), living significance is self-significance everywhere with standpoint neutrality, with neither connection nor relational neutrality; with lives no standpoint holds, relational neutrality is added for the unheld only (`Valuation.significant_iff_reached`); each premise is needed (`Apart.not_enough`, `Unclaimed`, `Relayed`). On a connected whole where every standpoint holds a living claim, self-significance with relational neutrality also gives it (`Valuation.significant_of_transmits`): standpoint neutrality is the completion needing no connection, not the only one. Read stepwise, with each formation's region its closure under any relation, every living event is held (`Web.selfValuation_held`, `Web.selfValuation_significant_iff`; along the empty relation, `Web.significant_iff_self`).
- `Clearing.center_not_derived`: in `Clearing`, each formation holds and values its own events and the clearer also its source's; every life is held, self-significance holds everywhere, each standpoint's significance is closed under feeding, and the dweller's living event has no significance from the clearer's standpoint. Along every formed relation of the stepwise reading the extent would reach the dweller; that the source requires nothing is stipulated. Self-significance ranges over living claims only, so a formation's own undermining events fall outside it (`Clearing.clearer_not_living`). Used in §6.4.
- `Conditions.significant_iff_of_common`: under a correspondence discipline, a common condition every living formation takes part in connects the whole, and there living significance is a seed with both neutralities; disjoint conditions can disconnect it (`Separate.not_connected`). The discipline relates two events through a condition only where one draws on it and the other depends on it at a positive level, so an idle condition connects nothing (`Web.inert_connects`) and connection is made by living use (`Shared.connection_by_living_use`), though a renewed condition used once still connects (`one_use_connects`). Under relational neutrality valuing a consumer's activity reaches every living event of what it consumes (`Conditions.valuing_consumption_reaches`; stepwise, `Web.valuing_breaking_reaches`).

*Further relations and readings of `living`.* Living on (drawing on a relatum and depending on it at a positive need) and descent (taking part in the step a relatum begins to stand and giving to it) connect a bee and a flower and generations each given by the one before; under relational neutrality valuing the fox's eating reaches the hare's living; living on needs actual drawing and a dependence that can fail, and the relatum lived on need be neither embodied nor active (`Drowsy`). Conveyance relates two events at a step (`Flow.conveyance`); an idle relatum is related to none, the boundary of a continuing family is crossed from every step on (`Flow.region_not_a_cut`), and valuing a relatum's event reaches what conveys into it (`Flow.valuing_reaches_source`). Readings are relative to a run (`Renewable.class_belongs_to_the_run`); whole-run and stepwise readings differ only in `living`, both ways (`Renewable.readings_differ`), and an event's stepwise reading is the conjunction of its relations' (`Web.living_iff_relations`). In the renewable process a finite-prefix reading counting every whole-run renewing run counts a consuming one (`Renewable.finite_reading_includes_consumption`), and a whole-run but-for test of a single part cannot replace participation (`Overdetermined.no_difference`, `Renewable.no_finite_segment_makes_difference`). Whether conditions such as the biosphere or the climate are shared by all living formations is an empirical correspondence; whether the relational reading is 生生 is a philosophical argument, and its ground is not established ([assessment](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING-REVIEW.md); the comparison of readings in [study §8.5](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md)).

*Reasons addressed to every position.* The positioned articulation D, not conditioned on endorsement, follows from A with positive content read as significance from every standpoint (`positioned_articulation_of_articulates`). A sound inquiry aimed at new living content gives every position with access to it a contributory reason (`universal_reason`): its normative premises are the seed, both neutralities (together `Valuation.Grounds`) and A, while connection and soundness are facts about the unfolding and the inquiry; the theorem passes the normative content through and does not supply it. A contributory reason is not a duty or a ranking, and predicts no recognition or compliance. Used in §7.4.

*Readings* [AI reading, D1, D9, D10, D11, D12, D13, D15, D43]. Ren's answer to what makes ethical quality: “我的答案是生生不息 - 一种整体的，不断创造更多生的方向，而这个方向产生的可能性，多样性越高，复杂性/复杂结构的复杂度可以不断的上升，就是一个好的方向” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-shengsheng-ethical-direction.md); “My answer is 生生不息, generation of life without ceasing: a direction of the whole that keeps creating more life. The more possibilities and diversity this direction produces, and the more the complexity of complex structures can keep rising, the better a direction it is.”). Living significance concerns realized living content; 生生 is the object of the ethical direction, compared by widening, and the `living` readings, which all read “not damaging”, do not include further generation. Ren's proposal of 10 October 2026, that “it can be anything that want's its continuation to have significance” and that “the whole … is actually perspective dependent” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-wanting-continuation-one-axiom.md)), was first analysed as putting a center at every wanting point; since centers and boundaries form within the dynamics and along dependence the center is not derived, the condition is read as its wording says (§6.4), and self-significance (reading “wanting one's continuation”) and standpoint neutrality follow from it. Living on, descent and conveyance are adopted as formed relations of continuation, not direct valuations. The weak form, for each formation, that its undamaging continuation has significance, reads “as long as not harm” as retaining (§6.4), the deontic reading (a claim on others not to harm) being unformalized; the strong form, for the whole, that continuation creates more significance, reads “more” as widening and can be met only where the world stays open (§1.3). The earlier reading, its draft restatement and the narration of D11–D15 are kept in the history of the chapter as of 17 October 2026.

Records: [metaethical-core-characterization](../results/records/metaethical-core-characterization.md), [becoming-renewal-reading](../results/records/becoming-renewal-reading.md), [condition-correspondence-discipline](../results/records/condition-correspondence-discipline.md), [living-on-and-descent](../results/records/living-on-and-descent.md), [living-significance-on-held-lives](../results/records/living-significance-on-held-lives.md), [conveyance-crosses-the-boundary](../results/records/conveyance-crosses-the-boundary.md), [positioned-positive-reason](../results/records/positioned-positive-reason.md), [positive-ground-open-futures](../results/records/positive-ground-open-futures.md), [universal-practical-warrant](../results/records/universal-practical-warrant.md).

<a id="69-不用公理能走多远p40-how-far-the-route-without-axiom-reaches-p40"></a>
<a id="69-不用公理能走多远--how-far-the-route-without-axiom-reaches"></a>
<a id="610-反馈所及与比较p42-feedback-reach-and-the-comparison-p42"></a>
<a id="610-反馈所及与比较--feedback-reach-and-the-comparison"></a>
<a id="69-how-far-the-route-without-axiom-reaches"></a>
<a id="610-feedback-reach-and-the-comparison"></a>
### 6.3 How far the route without the condition reaches

**In words.** With no condition at all, “should” is what a center does once its represented boundary matches what it actually depends on. What a continuation depends on is its feeding closure; a loss returns to a center through its feeders, and what lies beyond the closure supplies nothing within it. So the route without the condition reaches the self and what it depends on, and the whole only where the whole is one feeding closure, where its verdict is the condition's. Any significance covering what a part reaches compares that part as the condition does. What the condition adds is significance for continuations whose loss returns to no one.

- `Flow.within_closure_iff_retains`: under the condition, where a formation's feeding closure is the whole, the verdict within its closure is the condition's verdict on every comparison (as in `Overshoot`); significance bounded by the whole is the condition (`bounded_whole`), and wherever every formation the other path admits lies in the region or is admitted by the first, bounded significance retains as inclusion does (`Web.retains_bounded_iff_includes`). In `Clearing` the verdicts part: from the clearer's standpoint its part retains within its closure, and under the condition, and from the dweller's own closure, it loses the dweller (`Clearing.within_closure_yet_loses`).
- `Flow.Balances.comparison_local`: a center valuing only what its feedback carries compares its own part as the condition does wherever the part reaches only within that closure; reaching beyond can make it misjudge, as the clearer does, and need not (`Margin.reach_without_misjudgment`). Bounded by each reach closure the clearer's part loses the dweller although that significance is not the condition, while bounded by each influence closure it retains (`Clearing.reach_counts_dweller`, `Clearing.system_counts_dweller`); where each center's system is the whole, bounded significance is the condition (`Flow.bounded_system_whole`). These three witnesses are stated in `Becoming.BoundedSignificance`.
- `Flow.MutuallyDependent.unrestricted`: under mutual dependence significance bounded by what can affect each center is the condition (§4.2), and where a center's reach is the whole, as where every formation depends on one condition and exchanges with it (`Flow.reach_whole_of_common`), the weaker version coincides with the condition's comparison.

The constraint runs along feeding (`Flow.Balances.consumption_ends_with_feeders`, `Overshoot.ends_through_feeder`), and the region along dependence in `Clearing` is the derived feeding closure (`Clearing.dependenceRegion_iff`), so the disagreement of centers does not rest on a stipulation. What the derived extent does not reach (`Downstream.one_way`, `Mortal.after_its_end`, `Clearing.undermines_within_renewal`) is in §2.3. Not derived: that a center wants its continuation, represents its feeding closure or knows its reach closure; that a loss within the closure does return (feeders may be redundant, and stock and timing decide when an ending arrives); how others would respond, which needs the second flow; the adding comparisons in positive form for every significance, which hold where significance is decidable (`Flow.Balances.adds_iff_exceeds_of_decidable`) and otherwise up to double negation; and any warrant, which this route does not claim.

*Readings* [AI reading, D17, D18, D19, D20, D39]. Route (b) is the AI's wording ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-axiom-route-levels-and-possibilities.md)): “No axiom. The theory only describes, and “should” is what a center does once its represented boundary matches what it actually depends on.” It names two mechanisms that need no premise, selection between wholes and the constraint that a part degrading its whole removes its own supply; Ren answered that a whole realizing, understanding and updating itself is “also under the selection rules and constraints in theory, but in practice, how this happens, needs more thoughts”. A center that wants its continuation and represents its closure has a reason of its own to retain what lies within it, since a loss there can return to it: this explains motivation and is not a warrant. Selection between wholes removes the whole of `Overshoot`, which ends, and keeps that of `Clearing`, where the clearer continues while the dweller ends; neither mechanism reaches a loss that returns to no one. Of the levels Ren wrote: “i think it should be the whole, and a weaker version is within the system that one is in, then the self”. The system partitions the formations with no nesting and no center, so “the system that one is in” reads as the reach closure, centred, nesting and pointing forward; to compare its own part a center must know what the part reaches beyond its feedback, which reads Ren's “still need situational awareness”, attached to “the full truth set” in his statement of the long-term objectives ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-strongest-claims-direction.md)). Ren wrote that “at this moment in time, the whole earth has become a highly entangled system” and “everyone country, every person, is heavily coupled into it” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-truth-exists-opening.md)): “heavily coupled” reads as coupling strengths, “highly entangled” as mutual dependence (§4.2), not quantum entanglement. Ren wrote that the whole can realize itself, “like us, we recognize that "we" are the universe/ecosystem that realizes its own existence”: a center whose represented boundary is the whole holds the condition's significance (`bounded_whole`).

Records: [feeding-closure](../results/records/feeding-closure.md), [consumption-ends-with-feeders](../results/records/consumption-ends-with-feeders.md), [closure-verdict-meets-continuation-condition](../results/records/closure-verdict-meets-continuation-condition.md), [levels-of-closure](../results/records/levels-of-closure.md), [feedback-and-reach](../results/records/feedback-and-reach.md), [comparison-within-reach](../results/records/comparison-within-reach.md), [derived-extent-limits](../results/records/derived-extent-limits.md).

<a id="615-若任何东西的延续都有意义--if-the-continuation-of-anything-has-significance"></a>
<a id="第二部分一条公理--part-ii-one-axiom"></a>
<a id="68-一条公理推出什么p39-what-the-one-axiom-yields-p39"></a>
<a id="68-一条公理推出什么--what-the-one-axiom-yields"></a>
<a id="68-what-the-one-axiom-yields-as-a-condition"></a>
<a id="612-g0-的各处用法在公理之下p44-g0s-uses-under-the-axiom-p44"></a>
<a id="612-g0-的各处用法在公理之下--g0s-uses-under-the-axiom"></a>
<a id="612-g0s-uses-under-the-condition"></a>
<a id="615-if-the-continuation-of-anything-has-significance"></a>
<a id="68-what-the-continuation-condition-yields"></a>
<a id="612-the-uses-of-living-significance-under-the-condition"></a>
### 6.4 The continuation condition

**This section is conditional: the theory does not assert its condition.** It takes the condition as a hypothesis and states what follows and what does not.

**In words.** A significance says, for a standpoint, a step and a formation, whether from that standpoint the formation's continuation at that step has significance. The continuation condition says that nothing restricts it: from every standpoint the continuation of anything has significance at every step. It is boundary neutrality applied once, to the significance of continuation. It fixes scope, whose losses count; it gives significance, not a positive valence; and by itself it orders nothing.

**Instantiation, said once.** `Unrestricted sig` is `Neutral` applied to `sig`, and `Neutral rule` is `∀ i, rule i`: under the condition `sig` holds of every standpoint, step and formation. Every theorem below that takes the condition as a hypothesis (`livingSignificance_of_unrestricted`, `Valuation.premises_of_unrestricted`, `Web.retains_iff_includes` and the rest) is an instantiation of that universal statement; counting such theorems counts uses of it, not dependence on further content. A statement scan of the core on 19 October 2026 finds 30 to 33 theorems taking it as a hypothesis and 24 to 31 stating it in their conclusion (the equivalences that characterize it, the decomposition, the witnesses where it fails, `bounded_rival`), the numbers depending on whether hypotheses stated as section variables or under a `∀` are counted (on 17 October: 26 and 20 in the core, 7 in the bridge, which left with the F3 sweep; the [inventory of 11 October](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/PART-II-INVENTORY.md#31-用到公理的-22-条--the-22-that-use-the-axiom) found 22 of 157 cited theorems took it). Import order is not dependence: `Becoming.Significance` and `Becoming.Metaethics` are imported, through `Becoming.Interdependence`, by 48 other core modules, among them `Becoming.Loops`, `Becoming.Observation` and `Becoming.Onward`, which use no theorem taking the condition; the layers follow imports, not the line between any significance and the condition.

- `Valuation.premises_of_unrestricted`: under the condition, on any reading where a claim's significance is some formation's continuation, living significance holds at the level of claims, with self-significance everywhere, both neutralities and, wherever something lives, a seed, with no connection used; the stepwise, relation-level and whole-run readings are such readings (`Web.significant_of_unrestricted`, `Conditions.significant_of_unrestricted`). Positive relevance read from significance is the least relevance satisfying living significance, holding of exactly the realized living content (`relevance_least`), so every use of living significance in core and periphery is discharged (`livingSignificance_of_unrestricted`, `Extension.new_relevance`, `SecuredContent.contingent_newContent_of_unrestricted`, `securesNewContent_by_bound_of_unrestricted`); characterizations and countermodels are unaffected. Living significance is therefore a condition, not an axiom, and needs none of its own.
- `unrestricted_iff_livingSignificance`: on a frame whose claims are formations at steps, each realized somewhere, the condition is exactly living significance with every claim living and positive relevance read as significance from every standpoint; on the level paths of every web this holds outright (`Web.unrestricted_iff_paths_livingSignificance`). On level paths the condition, significance and C read one object, admission (`Web.paths_one_object`): the condition widens living significance's scope to anything and weakens “positive” to “has significance”. Used in §7.5.
- `Web.retains_iff_includes`: under the condition, retaining what has significance (`Web.Retains`) is inclusion and adding (`Web.Adds`) is exceeding, alike from every standpoint (`Web.adds_iff_exceeds`, `Web.retains_agree`); widening is retaining and adding, keeping the same is retaining both ways, mixed is adding both ways (`Web.widens_iff_retains_adds`, `Web.keeps_iff_retains`, `Web.mixed_iff_adds`). So the generative space read as significance loses nothing, and the condition adds no rule. The margin laws of §6.1 hold for any significance (`Flow.Balances.retains_iff_within_margin`, `Flow.Balances.loses_iff_beyond_margin`); the condition only makes them cover every formation.
- `Preemption.not_undermining_yet_loses`: what has not yet formed counts. A drawer taking a condition's unit before any dependence on it forms is living on the stepwise reading, yet its part narrows what can form, and under the condition it loses a continuation with significance. Undermining is a sign of loss where the levels without the part meet the dependences (`Web.UnderminingOn.not_retains_unrestricted`, through `Web.exceeded_of_below`), and renewal at a step is no sign of keeping (`Preemption.within_renewal_yet_loses`). Over the whole run, with only active formations taking from what they draw on, a part drawing only on conditions renewed over the run keeps what can form (`Flow.Balances.not_narrows_of_renewed`), and one giving no more than it takes where a dependence is consumed narrows (`Flow.Balances.narrows_of_consumed`); keeping is not widening, and a part can consume over the run and keep (`Untaken.consuming_keeps`). Arising is where a part's place in the order begins: with only active formations taking and giving, a part none of whose members is active at the start or arises over the run changes nothing that can form (`Flow.Balances.keeps_of_not_arising`), and a member's arising decides nothing, since the arisen member can narrow what can form (`ArisingDrawer.arises_narrows`) or widen it (`ArisingGiver.arises_widens`) under the same premises. These run-class laws take no condition.
- `Clearing.clearer_loses_dweller`: under the condition the clearer's part loses the dweller from every standpoint, while the levels without it admit every formation and the clearer is supported throughout, so withholding the part ends nothing (`Clearing.admitted_all_without`; a witness in one constructed world). Without the condition, with each formation's significance stopping at its dependence, the centers disagree (`Clearing.centers_disagree`). Under the condition what retains in the whole retains within every region (`Web.Retains.within`), the fewer continuations have significance the more is retained (`Web.Retains.of_fewer`), and a bounded significance retains what is admitted within its region (`Web.retains_bounded_iff`). Used in §6.5.

**What the condition does not settle.** Under it significance is the same everywhere, so every narrowing loses something with significance, predation, apoptosis and ending a cancer alike; such parts are mixed (`Transfer.unordered`), and telling harm from what is not harm among them needs a measure. No ending is licensed: a path retaining what has significance along another ends none of it (`Web.Retains.not_added`), and the giver's part retains and adds (`Restoring.Inflow.retains_adds`). On level paths C holds because “better” is read as widening (§6.1), not because of the condition. A, C, L and the practical commitments stay disclosed conditions; the level at which formations are individuated (members or lineages) fixes what counts as a loss; which reading the `living` of living significance names is a choice (`Preemption` shows two differ); and the warrant of the condition is open (§8.5), with no route attempted that would make significance a function of the world's own data. A significance bounded by the self is a premise too: Ren wrote that the self “can not justify itself without some sort of "belief"”. `bounded_rival` proves only that a significance bounded by regions holds the rule within its region and is not the condition where something lies outside; that such a rival is consistent with every law of the dynamics holds meta-theoretically (§5).

*Reading Ren's words* [AI reading, D16]. The wording of the condition is Ren's, kept as provenance: “if we want to add one axiom, that is for anything, its continuation into the future has significance(doesn't have to be positive, we could argue as long as not harm), or continuation into the future needs to create more significance(being generative)” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-wanting-continuation-one-axiom.md)). `Unrestricted` reads it: the formation index reads “anything”, a formation's continuation at a step “its continuation into the future”, and the standpoint index “has significance” without qualification, no center privileged. Significance is not graded and need not be positive. Retaining reads “as long as not harm”, and adding reads “create more significance” and “more possibilities of becoming”, so the strong form is the generative case of the weak form. Ren's answer of 10 October 2026 places centers within the dynamics and calls “not harm” a phase transition with a clear boundary ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-centers-within-the-dynamics.md)); the margin laws state that boundary. Ren's order of levels, the whole, then the system one is in, then the self, is an order of how complete a judgment is, not a description of what life does [AI reading, D43]. *Dated note.* Living significance was an adopted axiom until 11 October 2026 and the condition was asserted, in a separate part, until 16 October 2026; both were then restated as conditions the theory does not assert ([11 October](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-fold-g0.md), [16 October](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-16-fold-part-ii.md)); the retired labels are in §10(c).

Records: [continuation-condition-yields-living-significance](../results/records/continuation-condition-yields-living-significance.md), [significance-retained-within-margin](../results/records/significance-retained-within-margin.md), [the-run-classes-and-widening](../results/records/the-run-classes-and-widening.md), [whole-first-and-bounded-centers](../results/records/whole-first-and-bounded-centers.md), [ranking-under-readings](../results/records/ranking-under-readings.md).

<a id="611-公理的分解自身的预设与不变的一步p43-the-axiom-decomposed-the-selfs-presupposition-and-the-step-of-invariance-p43"></a>
<a id="611-公理的分解自身的预设与不变的一步--the-axiom-decomposed-the-selfs-presupposition-and-the-step-of-invariance"></a>
<a id="611-the-axiom-decomposed-the-selfs-presupposition-and-the-step-of-invariance"></a>
<a id="65-比较提升与实践判断--comparison-lifting-and-practical-judgment"></a>
<a id="66-不做什么--what-it-does-not-do"></a>
<a id="611-the-condition-decomposed-the-selfs-presupposition-and-the-step-of-invariance"></a>
<a id="65-comparison-lifting-and-practical-judgment"></a>
<a id="66-what-it-does-not-do"></a>
### 6.5 Scope and order: the step of invariance, C and practical judgment

**In words.** The ethics rests on two premises with two roles. The continuation condition fixes scope: exactly decomposed, it is the self's presupposition (from a standpoint, the continuation of what lies within its boundary has significance) joined to the step of invariance (that presupposition does not change with how the self's boundary is drawn). That step is the condition's content beyond the self; it extends the scope to losses that return to no one, and it ranks nothing. C, the widening comparison, fixes order: calling widening better is the only premise that ranks courses. Courses are compared on possibilities; lifting (L) and the practical commitments (J1–J4) carry the comparison to responses and choice. Neither premise is asserted, and no ethical result follows from the condition alone.

- `unrestricted_iff_presupposed_along`: along any relation the whole is an admissible boundary of every self, and the condition is exactly the presupposition sub-true (holding as the boundary is drawn somewhere, `SubTrue`) and boundary-invariant (`BoundaryInvariant`) at every standpoint; generally where the whole is admissible (`unrestricted_iff_presupposed`), and from covering (`unrestricted_of_presupposed`). Super-truth (`SuperTrue`, after Fine 1975; sub-truth after Hyde 1997) is invariant sub-truth (`superTrue_iff`), and what lies within the self however its boundary is drawn is exactly the closure (`superTrue_member_iff`). Logically the decomposition is A ⇔ P ∧ (P → A), given A ⇒ P, with supervaluation bookkeeping: it locates the contested step and derives nothing beyond it. The step runs both ways (`not_subTrue_of_invariant`); where the whole is admissible it is the conditional from the presupposition to the condition (`boundaryInvariant_iff_conditional`); given the presupposition everywhere and every formation within some closure it is standpoint-invariance, the rule of standpoint neutrality (`boundaryInvariant_iff_standpointInvariant`; apart otherwise, `OneCounts.forms_apart`).
- `presupposition_scopes`: route (b) and the condition are one presupposition with “however the boundary is drawn” at narrow and wide scope. Sub-true along a relation, it is significance over the closure (`subTrue_significantWithin_iff`): along the empty relation a standpoint's own good, along the total relation the condition (`subTrue_empty_iff`, `subTrue_total_iff`); route (b)'s significance is the least making every presupposition sub-true (`bounded_closure_least`). Sub-true along affecting it compares every part within the reach closure as the condition does, using neither invariance nor super-truth (`Flow.Balances.presupposed_reach_comparison`; along sharing, the system, which is the stronger premise, `Flow.presupposition_order`). Where every formation lies within each closure, invariance is trivial and the step has moved into drawing the self's boundary closed under the relation (`unrestricted_iff_presupposed_of_whole`, `Flow.unrestricted_iff_presupposed_of_common`). Each premise is needed (`Clearing.without_presupposition`, `Clearing.without_invariance`, `Clearing.with_fixed_boundary`).
