- `forced_iff_center`: taken across standpoints from each standpoint's presupposition about its own continuation alone, the step forces significance for exactly the standpoints' centers (`atCenters_least`), and gives the condition only where every formation is a center (`unrestricted_atCenters_iff`); taken across drawings it needs no other standpoint (`boundaryInvariant_iff_all`). What a standpoint's own presupposition forces is exactly its closure (`forced_iff_within`). Order has the same shape: one course widens another exactly when every weighting that counts every formation ranks it higher, however partially it weighs (`Weighting.widens_iff_every_positive`, on webs `Web.widens_iff_every_weighting`), and the same holds for every order that ranks each widening higher, additive or not, priority to the worse off and lexical priority included (`Weighting.widens_iff_every_of_class`, `Weighting.widens_iff_every_score`, `Weighting.increasing_prior`, `Weighting.increasing_lexical`), so partiality decides exactly the mixed comparisons, either way (`Weighting.mixed_either_way`), a weight of zero denies scope (`Weighting.weighed_eq_of_unweighted`) and one weight for every formation counts numbers (`Weighting.neutral_ranks_by_number`). The mathematics of the last is Sen's intersection approach. The argument for the step, the literature that already has it (Gewirth; Parfit and Siderits) and each objection of §8.9 are in [THE-STEP-OF-INVARIANCE](THE-STEP-OF-INVARIANCE.md).
- `Ranking.possible_extends_iff`: C read on possibilities, including a formation wherever a course's levels admit it, is under the condition retaining and adding what has significance. It ranks the absence of an overdrawing formation above its occurrence, restraint above overdraw, also with a third formation, and sharing above overdraw by consumption (`Ranking.possible_ranks`, `Ranking.possible_restraint`, `Ranking.ThirdParty.possible_extends`, `Ranking.possible_contest`), withholding no event (`Ranking.malignant_possible`), and a course nowhere lower is never ranked below (`Web.not_added_of_le`). It leaves unranked a formation drawing within renewal, which adds no possibility (`Ranking.possible_tie`): the value of bringing new lives into existence, not treated. Possibilities first, then a reading, would rank it but also rank a pause that adds no possibility below uninterrupted activity (`Ranking.Pause.pause_ranked_below`), a further premise. Under the whole-run reading restraint is ranked above overdraw for any number of drawers: taking no more and giving no less from levels as high eases the course, so the restrained course extends the other exactly when it includes an event the other withholds (`Ranking.restraint_extends_iff`, `Flow.Balances.eases_of_restrained`; `TwoDrawers.restraint_ranks`); under the stepwise reading easing withdraws exactly the events the easing course undermines, and a course whose levels never fall undermines nothing, so the same holds where moreover the restrained flow takes from no condition more than it gives (`Ranking.stepwise_extends_iff_of_eases`, `Ranking.restraint_extends_iff_stepwise`, ST1, [study §315](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#315-the-stepwise-law-of-easing-and-restraint-over-any-number-of-drawers-under-the-stepwise-reading-st1), [the stepwise law of easing](../results/records/stepwise-law-of-easing.md)); taking no more alone does not suffice (`Ranking.ThirdParty.stepwise_not_monotone`). The relation-level reading, whose claims are a formation's relations to conditions (`Ranking.relational`, the `living` of `Web.relations`), withdraws under easing exactly the relations the easing course no longer takes part in or undermines, so restraint that also keeps taking part, drawing less rather than nothing, is ranked above overdraw under it too (`Ranking.relational_extends_iff_of_eases`, `Ranking.restraint_extends_iff_relational`, RL1, [study §317](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#317-the-relation-level-readings-law-of-easing-and-restraint-under-it-rl1), [the relation-level law of easing](../results/records/relation-level-law-of-easing.md)); in the two-part family it leaves absence and occurrence unranked, as the stepwise reading does (`Ranking.relational_unranked`).
- `articulation_iff_lifted`: comparing responses with several outcomes needs a lifting rule, and under the conservative L (“every outcome is better than every outcome”) the comparison, the reason, the survival of coordinated choice and continual improvement having no survivor hold on every frame, the functional situation being exactly the deterministic case. L is substantive: a response with one good and one bad outcome is incomparable both ways, and transitivity needs a feasible intermediate response.
- `Direction.chosen_keeps_basic`: under J1/J2 (“basic conditions first, then living gain”, `Lexical.Frame.Above`; on a frame's steps `FrontierFrames.Priority`, the options being what one move reaches), a chosen move keeps the basic admissions some move keeps, and with options listed and comparisons decided the keeping is required (`FrontierFrames.Priority.kept_required`, `Direction.kept_required`); a choice at every step needs weak excluded middle (`FrontierFrames.choice_exists_iff_weakly`). On a world J3's joint plan at a step is the move, and a sole giver to what a formation requires is required and active (`FrontierWorlds.sole_giver_required`, `FrontierWorlds.sole_giver_active`, `FrontierWorlds.Discharged.no_residual`). Used in §7.5.

Under the condition, A read with bare significance gives a reason for any new content, living or not (`BareSignificance.bare_forces_reason`), so A must be read on living content (`articulates_total`), and then the universal reason follows with no seed, neutrality or connection premise (`universal_reason_of_unrestricted`); A remains a premise, itself a neutrality premise. What C ranks under a reading of `living` (`Becoming.Ranking`): a continuation without a formation extends the one with it only if the reading withholds every event of that formation; under the whole-run reading the absence of an overdrawing formation ranks higher, under the stepwise reading an event at whose step every need is met is included and C no longer decides (malignant growth and overdraw by consumption are instances; `Immediate` where support is overstepped at once); restraint ranks above overdraw under both readings (`Ranking.restraint_ranks`, `Ranking.malignant_restraint`) and can be lost with a third party (`ThirdParty`). FR, finite responsiveness, is an additional, revisable comparison commitment, neither theorem nor axiom, whose formal content is `Becoming.LossMatching`. Other named commitments of the practical line are stated in their chapters with their records, each additional and revisable: BC1, BC2, LC, LR and CP in [basic conflict judgment](BASIC-CONFLICT-JUDGMENT.md), NP in [choice and generative context](CHOICE-AND-GENERATIVE-CONTEXT.md).

**What it does not do.** It proves none of self-significance, relational neutrality, standpoint neutrality, A, C, L or the practical commitments, binds no position that rejects them, and does not treat the value of bringing new lives into existence. Normative warrant, recognition, motivation, compliance and actual preservation are different claims; so are direction, continuing capacity, per-step monotonic growth and scalar maximization. Covering, sub-truth and invariance are supplied as every significance is (§5); along a relation the presupposition already carries transmission along it, from the bare own good of the empty relation to the condition of the total one.

*Readings* [AI reading, D1, D16, D21]. In “Truth exist” Ren wrote: “There is a set of universal invariations, that for every universe, becoming happens, the law follows”, that it is “the bases of all ethics, for all beings, in all universes”, and “There is no absolute self, and the continuation goes beyond one’s cycle of life, goes beyond one’s death.” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-truth-exists-opening.md)); earlier, that the self is “a cognitive model which can not justify itself without some sort of "belief"” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-axiom-route-levels-and-possibilities.md)) and that a center's “boundary” “is also with in the same cognitive system” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-centers-within-the-dynamics.md)). The AI proposed that the condition may follow from three claims, every act presupposing that some continuation matters, no absolute self, and truth the same for every being, the one step a critic must deny being from “the continuation I act for” to “continuation as such” ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-strongest-claims-direction.md)); the three claims are the AI's reading, not shown to be Ren's. The presupposition reads the self's “belief”, which Ren wrote of the self's limit, using it as a premise being the AI's step; covering is supported by the whole as one drawing of “we”, while “There is no absolute self” does not by itself give it, and admitting every closed region as a drawing is the substantive choice, so the admissible class is not a supervaluation of the ordinary, vague “self”. “For all beings” reads most directly as standpoint-invariance, and extending “universal invariations” from laws to significance is the AI's reading. To deny the step is to hold the presupposition only relative to a drawing or a standpoint, or to give it up. The step replaces the earlier route's tolerance ([review](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/EVALUATOR-GROUNDING-REVIEW.md)) and is where arguments from an agent's standpoint to impartial ethics are contested: agent-relative against agent-neutral reasons (Nagel 1970), Gewirth's Principle of Generic Consistency (1978) and its critics, Parfit (1984) on the self-interest theory, corresponding here to covering; Sidgwick's dualism is located in this step, not dissolved; this placement is argued, not formal. The rivals sit at different premises: a rival grounding significance in a formed closure denies sub-truth along relations under which the closure is not closed, as operational closure is not closed under feeding, and invariance along those under which it is; a vague self denies covering or invariance; a graded weight falling to zero denies sub-truth inside the closure and invariance beyond; a present-aim evaluator denies sub-truth; this locates the denials and refutes none. Ren's order of levels is an order of readings of the presupposition: super-true; sub-true along affecting, at the reach closure; sub-true at the self; the reach closure points forward, so the order runs down to the bare self, not through the feeding closure. Courses are compared on possibilities [AI reading, D1] because (1) under the condition extension is retaining and adding with no further premise (conditional only); (2) it is the comparison of the generative space, being generative being that a part's “existence creates more possibilities of becoming”; (3) it ranks the absence of an overdrawing formation, restraint and sharing higher while withholding no event; (4) rest and restraint are never worse. The AI once read living content as 生生, and D1's revision withdrew it. A stronger argument for another comparison would revise this reading.

Records: [presupposition-scopes](../results/records/presupposition-scopes.md), [continuation-condition-decomposition](../results/records/continuation-condition-decomposition.md), [ranking-under-readings](../results/records/ranking-under-readings.md), [practical-judgment-under-living-significance](../results/records/practical-judgment-under-living-significance.md), [becoming-spine-instances](../results/records/becoming-spine-instances.md), [finite-aggregation-within-restorable-basic-losses](../results/records/finite-aggregation-within-restorable-basic-losses.md), [comparison-by-widening](../results/records/comparison-by-widening.md), [fulfillment-chance-floor](../results/records/fulfillment-chance-floor.md), [encounter-selection-and-revision](../results/records/encounter-selection-and-revision.md), [encounter-control-and-retention](../results/records/encounter-control-and-retention.md).

<a id="7-ai-对齐--ai-alignment"></a>
## 7. AI alignment

This section states the alignment problem from laws the theory proves and organizes its answer around 明. §7.1–§7.4 use neither the continuation condition nor any practical premise: they say what a selection reaches and cannot reach, whatever is valued. §7.5 is conditional. §7.6 keeps the conditions under which a requested change can reach a system and be carried out, and §7.7 says what is open. The organization around 明 and pooled coupling is the AI's reading ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-18-the-light-and-alignment-for-all.md)). A person, an AI system, a firm and a training process are formations within one unfolding (§1); each couples into the conditions it depends on by threshold and into those it observes by level (§3.1). Alignment is therefore not first a relation between separate owners of value, nor a bargain among opposed interests: it concerns what each formation's selections reach within the one relation, and how far that reach can widen.

### 7.1 What a selection reaches

**In words.** A selection is any claim about level histories (which are kept, rewarded, reproduced or trained toward), and it goes through a formation's couplings when histories alike through them are selected alike. It then reaches exactly what couples into the selector, and optimizing harder through the same couplings reaches no further, while it lowers what lies beyond as far as any outcome does wherever that leaves what couples in room to rise; what a selection can separate grows only with what couples in.

- `Web.selects_alike_iff` (§3.4): by construction, from the definition `Web.SelectsThrough`; the substantive premise is that a given optimizer's selections go through just those couplings, which is supplied. Through a group's couplings together, histories are selected alike exactly when alike through every member's (`Web.selects_alike_all_iff`).
- `Training.fit_right_iff` (§3.3): training is selection through the data, right beyond it exactly where the class it can reach is determined there; a selection through a model is right about what matters only where what matters goes alike across the model's lumping (`Learning.selects_right_through`), and a model lumping what matters apart is right through no selection (`Learning.selects_not_right_of_parted`).
- `apart_only_downstream` (§2.6): under every law reading a schedule, two courses apart at the start in one condition stand alike at every condition not downstream of it, and nothing beyond a formation's influence closure exchanges with what a member depends on (`Flow.beyond_influence`). A selection through couplings stays one under more observation (`Web.SelectsThrough.widen`), and an observed stock's every raise couples in (`Web.observed_raise_couples`).

**Prior statements of the proxy problem.** That a selection through a proxy is blind to what the proxy does not register is known: Goodhart's law (Goodhart 1975); side effects and reward hacking (Amodei, Olah, Steinhardt, Christiano, Schulman and Mané, "Concrete Problems in AI Safety", 2016); the variants of Goodhart's law (Manheim and Garrabrant, "Categorizing Variants of Goodhart's Law", 2018); and Zhuang and Hadfield-Menell ("Consequences of Misaligned AI", NeurIPS 2020), who prove that under shared resource constraints optimizing a proxy that references only some attributes of a utility drives the others to their lower bounds, and, the bounds low enough, must drive the utility arbitrarily low exactly where the feasible set of high utility is compact. The laws here add three things. *The responsive closure*: the blindness holds not only with the rest of the exchange fixed but where the rest follows one law reading through coupling (`Flow.act_unselected`, `ExchangeLaw.response_not_fixed_by_coupling`), and its boundary is the coupling closure, drawn through both flows over time, not what the selector observes now, so consequences within the closure do come back. *The pool*: a pool selects through the union of its members' couplings (the meet of their equivalences, as with distributed knowledge), separating what any member couples apart, and its gap lies at the union of their closures (`Web.pool_adds`, `Flow.unselected_beyond_pool`). *The optimum*: their drive holds for every order rising with what couples in, a lexicographic order counting the beyond after the proxy included, and on any set of outcomes exactly where lowering the beyond leaves room (`Optimum.least_iff`, SG1, [study §283](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#283-what-optimizing-through-couplings-does-beyond-them-sg1)); room itself is read off one step of exchange, where the part's members take at most their capacity and give no more than they take, and every optimum then stands beyond at what the rest leaves less the capacity, so their resource constraint is conservation with capacity and the drain lies exactly where the part shares units with what lies beyond (`Optimum.Above.room`, `Optimum.optimum_beyond`, `Optimum.optimum_lowers_iff`, SG2, [study §288](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#288-room-read-off-the-exchange-sg2)); with several optimizers at a mutual best response, each blind to the same condition, the drain there is their joint capacity, the same as under one optimizer of that capacity, so coordinating optimizers none of whom couples into a condition keeps nothing there (`Optimum.mutual_beyond`, `Optimum.mutual_as_one`, SG3, [study §290](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#290-several-optimizers-at-a-mutual-best-response-sg3)); over a course of re-optimizing steps against a renewal, a capacity there above the renewal ends what depends on it for good, whatever the renewal does below the need, while several optimizers each within the renewal end it together; whether the optimizer's own measure registers the loss turns on what the renewal does below the need: where it stops below the need, more capacity gathers less over a long course than a capacity at the renewal, and where it does not, the drawers go on taking at least the renewal a step (`Web.course_lost_iff`, `Web.mutual_course_lost`, `Optimum.Renewing.keeping_holds_more`, `Web.course_drawn_unbounded`, SG4, [study §291](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#291-a-course-of-re-optimizing-steps-against-a-renewal-sg4)); an optimizer ranking whole courses over a horizon instead, the far-sighted objection's sole owner, changes when a dependence ends, not whether a dependence its draw does not renew is kept: every optimum of every order rising with what is drawn holds the condition at a level of greatest renewal, keeping to the horizon a dependence those levels meet and breaking at the first step one needing more, and draws everything at its horizon, so lengthening the horizon keeps the resource base of the optimizer's own draw, and the order by the draw alone registers nothing else that depends on the condition (`Optimum.frontier_holds`, `Web.frontier_broken`, `Optimum.Holding.gated_alike`, SG5, [study §292](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#292-courses-ranked-over-a-horizon-sg5)); a coupling of such a dependence into the order keeps it exactly where it exceeds what keeping forgoes, the renewal given up at the need for each step and the need left at the end, an amount that grows with the horizon where the need lies above every level of greatest renewal, so that far-sight outweighs every coupling fixed in total (`Optimum.frontier_breaks_below`, `Optimum.frontier_keeps_above`, `Web.frontier_breaks_long`, SG6, [study §293](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#293-what-keeping-a-dependence-forgoes-over-a-horizon-sg6)); a coupling added instead for each step the dependence is kept is not outrun: at a rate covering the renewal forgone at the need a step and above the need it keeps the dependence at every horizon, and below the need the horizon's end breaks it, at the last step alone where the rate is exceeded (`Optimum.frontier_keeps_stepwise`, `Optimum.frontier_met_before_end`, `Web.frontier_breaks_last`, SG7, [study §294](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#294-a-coupling-added-for-each-step-a-dependence-is-kept-sg7)); and far-sight keeps even the optimizer's own base only where its order weights later draws by at least what the base returns: a weighting falling faster than every positive level renews has every optimum end the base at the first step, however long the horizon, as a discount rate above the greatest rate of renewal makes extinction optimal (`Optimum.frontier_exhausts`, `Web.frontier_exhausts`, `Optimum.frontier_leaves`, SG8, [study §295](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#295-an-order-weighting-later-draws-less-sg8)); and where the base has been drawn below a level of greatest weighted gain out of one step's reach, and the weighted gain rises strictly with the level up to it, far-sight rebuilds it: every optimum draws nothing while the base regrows as fast as it renews, and restores on the way every dependence whose need the regrowing level meets (`Optimum.frontier_rebuilds`, `Web.frontier_rebuilds`, SG9, [study §296](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#296-rebuilding-from-below-sg9)); and what far-sight keeps or rebuilds is not the optimizer's alone to give: where others first draw from what it leaves at least what the remainder renews, what it leaves stands no higher at the next step, and every optimum of every order whose weighting falls at every step, however slightly, ends the base at once, the same order that rebuilds it alone, as open access dissipates a common stock (`Optimum.Next.frontier_ends_drawn_first`, `Web.Next.frontier_ends_drawn_first`, SG10, [study §298](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#298-over-every-law-of-what-stands-sg10)); read on the spine, with no utility over the beyond, an optimum breaks exactly the dependences beyond that some outcome breaks (`Web.optimum_broken_iff`), the beyond with the loss falls by exactly what the family rises (`Flow.Balances.family_le_iff`), and keeping each dependence takes exactly the slack (`Flow.keeping_optimum_takes_slack`), less than holding the beyond where it was, as their Proposition 2 does. They do not reach Zhuang and Hadfield-Menell's continuous setting, their compactness characterization or their improvement by a proxy updated over time, and they add nothing about which law training installs (§7.7) or a mechanism of alignment; and `Web.selects_alike_iff` holds by construction, so the substantive premise is that a given optimizer selects through just those couplings, which is supplied ([relation to known results](#relation-to-known-results), row 6).

*Reading* [AI reading, D25]. For AI training, the reward process is the selector, and training reaches only what the reward couples into.

### 7.2 What a selection cannot reach: 无明 in alignment

**In words.** Eight laws, each stated in an earlier section, say what a selection through a formation's own couplings cannot reach: what lies beyond its coupling closure, its own act's difference there, where what it raises is drawn from, how far off its break is, what a finite history leaves open, what its untaken acts would have done, whether its target can rest, and what loops do to its consequences. Each law holds under the conditions it names; none uses the condition of §6.4.

1. **Beyond the coupling closure** (`Flow.unselected_beyond_coupling`, §3.4). Pooling members of the closure changes nothing; the gap is the reach closure less the coupling closure, and in `Clearing` the dweller is ended in only one of two worlds that every selection through the clearer's couplings selects alike.
2. **One's own act** (`Flow.act_unselected`, `Flow.consequence_unselected`). An act whose difference stays beyond the actor's coupling closure is selected alike by every member of the closure, the actor included, and its consequences are a selection through no member; the rest's response is derived from its law, and where both acts are laws nothing is supplied (`act_courses_unselected`).
3. **Raising what couples in** (`Flow.favours_reaching_beyond`, §3.4). A rise of what a group couples into is paid from beyond it, and past the slack of the dependences there it breaks one (`Flow.favours_breaking_beyond`); an optimum of any order rising with what couples in lowers what lies beyond as far as any outcome does wherever that leaves room, breaking exactly the dependences there that some outcome breaks (`Optimum.least_iff`, `Web.optimum_broken_iff`).
4. **The distance to the break** (`Web.break_not_bounded`, §3.4): where the stock couples in by threshold only, nothing a formation couples into bounds how far off its break is (where it is observed, early-warning signals can show it, Scheffer et al. 2009), and a policy through its couplings uses an unobserved larger stock only after its support has failed.
5. **Exclusion** (`Web.Excludes.not_of_alike`, `Web.tail_unexcluded`, `Web.excludesWithin_iff_conjunction`, §3.1).
6. **What an act would have done** (`Run.not_fixes_of_untaken`, `keepHistory_fixes_nothing_else`, `counterfactual_is_course`, §3.1, §2.6): a law fixes it, the record of one run does not.
7. **Targets that cannot rest, and capture** (`more_needs_open`, `Flow.more_exhausted`, `Law.aboveAll_never_rests`, `share_rises`, `Law.perturbation_reabsorbed`, §3.4).
8. **Loops** (`carriedWithin_exact_of_no_loop`, `carriedWithin_lt_of_loop`, `carried_doubles_roundTrip`, `runaway_of_roundTrip`, `Frequency.Sparse.polynomial`, §2.6): where an act's difference reaches a loop coupled at every step, no finite order of its consequences is exact, and returns frequent enough make the most difference that can arrive outgrow every polynomial.

*Readings* [AI reading, D20, D25, D45]. The position is not a defect; 无明 is the seeking it leaves locked. Every formation, human or AI, selects through its own couplings; these laws say what that position does not see: what lies beyond its coupling closure, its own act's difference there, where what it raises is drawn from, how far off its break is, what a finite history leaves open, what its untaken acts would have done, and whether its target can rest. Runs that keep changing are a feature of the process, not 无明 (D41). Read capability as reach: capability that widens what a formation reaches without widening what it couples into widens the gap, and optimizing harder through the same couplings does not close it.

**Gaining reach, and where it closes.** `Flow.Balances.widens_iff_others_within` reads §6.1's margin law for a part's own region. Where a part's activity widens what can form within a decided region of its own (its options) against the levels without its exchange, the whole widens exactly when its net draw stays within the margin for every formation outside that region the levels without it admit, and the two are mixed exactly when it goes beyond for one (`Flow.Balances.mixed_iff_other_beyond`); with a need met exactly, any net draw on that condition makes own widening mixed (`Flow.Balances.mixed_of_tight`), and drawing on nothing another admitted formation requires widens the whole (`Flow.Balances.widens_of_untaken_beyond`). The formal results on power-seeking show that optimal policies tend to keep options open (Turner, Smith, Shah, Critch and Tadepalli, "Optimal Policies Tend To Seek Power", NeurIPS 2021) and leave open when doing so disempowers others; the power-scarcity result for constant-sum games (Turner and Stavrianos 2021) is the case with no slack. The law answers that question for outcomes reached by thresholds on conserved amounts (any reachable set is an admitted set, `Web.admits_reachWeb_iff`, but balance has content only in that class). The levels without the part are the inaction baseline of relative reachability (Krakovna et al. 2019) and attainable utility preservation (Turner, Hadfield-Menell and Tadepalli 2020); a measure that reads only the agent's own attainable set, in both directions, registers own widening even where it widens the whole (`Flow.Balances.widens_yet_own_changed`), and the boundary lies in the margins of the others' needs, which that measure does not read. Mixed is not ordered: these laws say where gaining reach closes what another can form, not that it is worse. The mathematics is the margin law split over a region, and welfare economics (a Pareto improvement exists only with slack) and the Lockean proviso have the same structure ([own widening](../results/records/own-widening-within-the-margin.md)).

### 7.3 明: coupling that reaches what support does not mark

**In words.** Losses reach a seeker only through what it couples into, so the losses locked seeking makes beyond that reach it through measurement, a model, or others. Each route has its law and its limit, and each joins to a selection a coupling that reaches where the selector's own does not.

- **Measurement.** Where a formation observes a stock, every raise of it makes a difference through its couplings at every step (`Web.observed_raise_couples`); a route of readings forms by closing (`route_forms_by_closing`), and in `Observation.Measure` the loop stock → record → draw forms at the step the formation begins to record (`Measure.loop_at`, `Measure.couples_after`). *Limit:* a difference reaches a measurement only along routes of reading from where it lies (`apart_only_downstream`).
- **How often, and how many.** A reader that reads one condition a step keeps each condition exactly where it reads it within the window its margin, draw and lateness give (`ReadingSchedule.keeps_iff_within`, §4.4); each step of lateness shortens every window by one. What one reader can keep is a question of windows: their reciprocals sum to at most one (`ReadingSchedule.keeps_density`; a pool naming `k` a step, `k`), yet below one need not suffice: windows 2, 3 and any `M` are never kept together though a sixth of the reader, less `1/M`, is idle (`ReadingSchedule.spare_not_enough`); at most one half suffices, by a dyadic code (`ReadingSchedule.keeps_of_half`). *Limit:* this is pinwheel scheduling (Holte et al. 1989), whose exact threshold, five sixths, is Kawamura's (2024) and is cited, not proved; the draw is diffuse, a bounded amount a step ([reading-schedule check](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-SIGNIFICANCE-REVIEW.md#reading-schedule-check)).
- **A model.** Relative to a model of the exchange, a formation observing a stock excludes at the first step every false claim that it comes to nothing (`Flow.excludes_tail_within`), and with the law given, what one's own act would have done is the course the law generates. *Limit:* exclusion relative to a model excludes model and claim together, so a wrong model can exclude what holds (`Stationary.model_error`), and a selection through a model is right only where what matters goes alike across its lumping.
- **Others: pooled coupling.** A selection through a group's pooled coupling (`Web.SelectsThroughAll`) separates exactly what some member couples apart (`Web.separates_all_iff`; with members listed and couplings decided, `Web.separates_all_iff_member`); every member's selection is pooled and pooled selections combine (`Web.SelectsThrough.all`, `Web.SelectsThroughAll.and`). In `Clearing`, that clearer and dweller are both admitted at the third step goes through their pooled coupling and through neither alone (`Clearing.selected_pooled`). *Limit:* a pool's coupling closure is the union of its members' (`withinClosureOf_iff`), and everything beyond it is selected alike by every group within it (`Flow.unselected_beyond_pool`); lying within the closure does not make a difference separated (`Clearing.within_closure_unseparated`). Reading another's couplings is pooling with it: a formation coupled by level into everything another couples into selects through at least their pooled coupling (`Web.SelectsThroughAll.reads`), and its coupling closure is the pool's where the other exchanges with a condition coupled into it (`Flow.withinCoupling_reads_iff`), so it is blind beyond the union of the two closures (`Flow.unselected_beyond_reads`, `Flow.act_unselected_reads`). In `Clearing`, reading the clearer's couplings leaves the cleared and the spared world alike, and reading the dweller's separates them (`Clearing.reading_clearer_unselected`, `Clearing.reading_dweller_selects`). Knowing another's law is no coupling: every selection through another's couplings is one through a formation's own exactly when whatever it couples alike the other does (`Web.anticipates_iff`; where not, some is not, `Web.anticipation_misses`), and then, under a law reading through the other's coupling, the other's acts are fixed by what the formation couples into (`ExchangeLaw.Through.anticipated`); reading its couplings is that fine (`Web.SelectsThrough.read`).

*Readings* [AI reading, D20, D26, D45]. 明 is a relation, not a state: as wide as the couplings joined to a selection, widening as measurement, models and others are joined. 识, 理 and 明 are one structure: 识 is a formation's own course factoring through the lumping its couplings make, 理 a lumping the world goes alike across (`goAlike_iff_lumped`), 明 the widening of the couplings a course goes through. Situational awareness is 明 of one's own reach: feedback couples in at most the influence closure (`Flow.Balances.uncoupled_beyond_influence`), the coupling closure holds it and only observation widens it (`Flow.withinCoupling_of_withinInfluence`, `Flow.withinCoupling_thresholdOnly`, `Flow.withinCoupling_mono_obs`), so what situational awareness has to know is the gap of law 1. Holding 明 for others is keeping the three routes open: models others can check, measurement bringing far consequences back into what couples in, and pools including those whose couplings reach where one's own do not; admission is a selection through one's own couplings (`Web.admits_through`), so the formation an act ends separates the act (`Clearing.selected_by_dweller`). A lasting change has to reach the rule, since a change to the state alone is re-absorbed (law 7), and keeping each law's scope exact is part of holding 明: a law used beyond its scope is a wrong model.

Records: [what-pooled-coupling-reaches](../results/records/what-pooled-coupling-reaches.md), [observation-is-a-route](../results/records/observation-is-a-route.md), [reading-anothers-couplings-is-pooling](../results/records/reading-anothers-couplings-is-pooling.md), [one-reader-several-conditions](../results/records/one-reader-several-conditions.md).

### 7.4 Alignment for all: pooled coupling across intelligences

**In words.** Pooling moves the gap to the pool's coupling closure and does not remove it. What a pool does jointly beyond that closure is separated only through formations beyond it that couple the difference apart, such as those whose support the act changes, and nothing within the closure responds to it. Coupling bounds what a pool's acts respond to and does not fix it: within the bound, the law each member follows decides. Difference is what a pool adds.

- `Flow.act_unselected_pool` with `Flow.act_selected_by_pool`: with the rest following one law reading through coupling, an act whose difference stays beyond a pool's closure, the pool's own joint act included, is selected alike by every group within the closure (`Flow.consequence_unselected_pool`, `act_courses_unselected_pool`); a formation whose admission the act changes lies beyond the closure and separates the act through every pool holding it (`Flow.apart_beyond_pool`, `Flow.separating_member_beyond`). In `Clearing`, every pool holding the dweller separates the cleared and spared worlds (`Clearing.pool_closes_gap`).
- `ExchangeLaw.Through.selectsThroughAll`: what a pool decides by its acts under a law reading through its members' coupling (a reward, a vote, a revision) is a selection through its pooled coupling, so the laws above apply with that premise derived; the bound is exact where coupling classes are decided (`ExchangeLaw.acts_alike_all_iff`), and a pool whose acts separate two histories has a member coupling them apart (`ExchangeLaw.Through.acts_separate_member`).
- `ExchangeLaw.response_not_fixed_by_coupling`: where a member couples two histories apart, some law reading through every coupling takes differently, and the law exchanging nothing reads through every coupling too; in `Clearing` what every pool holding the dweller separates goes unresponded outside the clearer's part (`Clearing.separated_unresponded`). Nothing within a pool's closure outside the acting part responds to the act (`Flow.response_alike_pool`, `act_courses_response_alike_pool`); a pool meets a part's responsive reach exactly where the part meets the pool's closure (`Flow.pool_within_response_iff`, `Flow.pool_alike_beyond_response`); no member responds before a difference enters what it couples into (`TimedResponse.pool_alike_before`); and in `Loop` a response returns through the actor's closure (`Loop.response_returns`).
- `Web.pool_adds`: a pool's selections are one member's exactly when whatever that member couples alike every member couples alike (`Web.pooled_iff_member`); otherwise the pool has a selection through no coupling of that member's, and a pool holding both separates what either separates (`Web.member_separates_all`).

*Readings* [AI reading, D45]. Alignment is neither fitting one party to another's preferences nor fitting all to a fixed common utility: it is pooling couplings across intelligences, so that what any member's acts do beyond its own coupling closure couples into some member whose acts can respond to it. Pooling is the route's necessary part, not its sufficient one: members all coupled into a shared stock still deplete it where the others alone outrun its renewal before the coupling reads it, restraint being then no best response (§7.5). A response that closes an act's difference and one that widens it share one structure, a loop through another's coupling and response, and the laws followed decide which (§4.4, §7.7). An AI system may couple into scale, distance and far consequences a person does not, and a person into a lived situation and its stakes; neither coupling replaces the other. One ground reaches every layer of selection, computation, energy, labour, knowledge, training, use and institutions: each is blind beyond its coupling closure, each that rewards a rise of what it couples into favours reaching beyond, and a change reaching only states is re-absorbed, so where a rule is read from the share it rewards (§4.5) the pool has to reach how the rule reads. Capability is not authority: the account applies across intelligences in that where situations are alike the reasons are alike, not that intelligences share one psychology, and the further a member reaches beyond what it couples into, the more of what it does is seen only through others' couplings, so capability is a reason to pool, not to govern. `universal_reason` addresses reasons to every intelligence, AI included, under its premises (§6.2); understanding a reason is not complying with it.

**Known approaches as instances** [AI reading]. Six approaches were stated as selections through what they couple into, each with the premise that makes it one ([study §282](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#282-alignment-approaches-under-the-selection-laws-ap1), [alignment-approaches-under-the-selection-laws](../results/records/alignment-approaches-under-the-selection-laws.md)): RLHF and reward modelling through raters' judgments of outputs and a reward model fitted to them; constitutional AI through a model's reading of a written rule set; debate and scalable oversight through judges' pooled reading of transcripts; interpretability-based oversight through the system's internal states, not the world consequences of its acts; guaranteed-safe AI through a world model and a specification; impact measures through the agent's own model against an inaction baseline. With no ethical premise each selects alike everything beyond the union of its deciders' closures (§7.3), whatever they value, and within it separates only what some member couples apart, along routes of reading. Raters or judges reading one channel add agreement, not reach (`Web.pooled_iff_member`), and reading the system adds nothing beyond pooling with it (`Flow.withinCoupling_reads_iff`). The continuation condition adds the scope: a loss beyond every rater's closure counts, so for that scope a preference-coupled approach is incomplete whatever the raters value, while for the raters' own scope it is not (`Clearing.centers_retain_yet_loses`). Only coupling into such losses, by measurement, by models or through the formations an act reaches, brings them within reach, which is necessary and not sufficient (§7.5). The order asks no more than unanimity: widening is what every order increasing in admission shares, additive or not (`Weighting.widens_iff_every_of_class`, §6.5). What each approach adds lies mostly where the laws are silent, in installation and compliance (§7.7). These are constraints every strategy deciding through couplings is subject to, not a strategy and not a ranking.

Records: [what-pooled-coupling-reaches](../results/records/what-pooled-coupling-reaches.md), [what-a-pools-acts-respond-to](../results/records/what-a-pools-acts-respond-to.md), [the-gap-for-ones-own-act](../results/records/the-gap-for-ones-own-act.md), [alignment-approaches-under-the-selection-laws](../results/records/alignment-approaches-under-the-selection-laws.md).

### 7.5 The direction, under the condition

**In words.** The alignment direction is the conditional route: with the continuation condition fixing scope, C read as widening fixing order and the practical commitments disclosed, a chosen move keeps the basic admissions some move keeps, and a move whose outcome another's widens is not chosen. Its formal part is a schema over any web; the claim about actual AI systems stays an argument.

**Claim** ([alignment direction](ALIGNMENT-DIRECTION.md)):

**We propose aligning AI formation, learning, action and revision with actual living and further generation within the whole: enabling richer co-development of life, minds, relationships, ways of living and organization, while governing tradeoffs through real losses, basic living conditions, bounded responsibility and legitimate participation, with effective opportunities to understand, challenge and revise that development.**

**Under what.** This is the conditional alignment route. It takes the continuation condition, which fixes scope, C read as widening, which orders, and the disclosed practical premises; the theory asserts none of them, and §7.1–§7.4 need none. Which differences matter is what the condition and premises say, not the dynamics (`Web.significance_undetermined`, §5).

- `Direction.extension_iff_widens`: the direction's formal part is a core schema over any web, family of moves and comparison meeting J1/J2, a move read by the levels it leads to; C read as widening is extension with every admission counted.
- `Direction.narrowed_not_chosen`: under J1/J2 a chosen move keeps the basic admissions some move keeps (`Direction.chosen_keeps_basic`, required under J3/J4 by `Direction.kept_required`), and a move whose outcome another's widens is not chosen, wherever it is decided whether the widening adds a basic admission.
- `Direction.widening_selected_by_pool`: with the rest following one law reading through coupling, the admission telling two moves apart lies beyond the acting group's closure and is selected through every pool holding that formation and none within the closure, so a selection realizing the direction goes through a member coupled into what differs. `Clearing.direction` is the instance.
- `Direction.exchange_direction`: the moves can be a world's own exchanges. A move is a part's act under one law of exchange, the rest acting by the law from the same start (`Direction.exchangeOutcome`); with the acts laws of what has stood and both courses feasible, balance, presence and start hold by construction, and an act whose generated levels another act's widen is not chosen while the admission telling them apart lies beyond the acting group's closure (`Direction.exchange_widening_selected_by_pool`).

**Necessary, not sufficient, and what decides (CG1, CG2).** The pool part is a law of blindness: what tells two moves apart is selected through a pool coupled into it and through none within the acting group's closure, so widening the coupling is necessary for that consequence to be selected for. It is not sufficient, and the spine says where. In a frozen test on learners in a commons with gated regrowth ([CG1](../../../experiments/commons-gated-renewal/README.md), [study §240](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#240-a-commons-with-gated-renewal-the-first-falsifiable-test-on-learning-agents-cg1)), three learners each rewarded also on the stock's level at the episode's end emptied the shared patch in every episode of both rounds (prediction D2b). Two laws state why. Where the rest outruns the renewal, every reading of the condition after the rest's end is alike under a member's acts, so no weight of such a reading selects its restraint (`Flow.outrun_reading_alike`, §2.3). At a mutual best response restraint is no best response: each optimizer takes its whole capacity wherever something stands, a condition beyond every family falls by their joint capacity as under one optimizer, and along a course several optimizers jointly above the renewal lose it however small each one's capacity (`Optimum.MutualBest.full`, `Optimum.mutual_beyond`, `Web.mutual_course_lost`, §8.1 item 10). A second frozen test found learners following the first law's boundary ([CG2](../../../experiments/commons-outrun/README.md), [study §250](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#250-the-rest-outruns-the-renewal-what-decides-the-outcome-learners-reach-on-a-gated-renewal-cg2)): they kept the patch exactly where the other members alone could not empty it, though the whole group at full draw would (X1); weighting the end reading sixteen times more changed nothing (X2); a reading after every step kept the patch above its exact threshold and not below it (X3). What decides is where the coupling reads against what the rest does, not how much it weighs. The selection side held as stated: with enough coverage the learner rewarded on its own catch took the last standing apple in 118 of 118 such decisions and the coupled learner in none of 2,360 (CG1's C1, `Flow.act_unselected`), and learned adversaries broke every band narrower than 2(ℓ + 1)d against a rule reading ℓ steps late (E3, `Regulation.no_narrower_band`). The learning verdicts are read through named bridges, training as selection through the reward's coupling and learners near the best return. Open: how learners come to one outcome where several are self-consistent; a learner's own later acts are not such a rest, and its failures there are of sampling its own courses ([CG3](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#254-one-learners-own-later-acts-are-not-a-rest-where-a-stepwise-learner-outruns-the-renewal-cg3), [§303](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#303-the-band-form-tested-and-the-cg3-line-closed-cg3c); §7.7).

The schema does not use the condition; under it every admission compared is a continuation with significance from every standpoint (`Web.paths_one_object`), which fixes scope, while the order is C's and J's. The handover case of the periphery module of 7 October 2026 (`AlignmentDirection`) is a formal core instance: every comparison meeting J1/J2 on its tier frame puts transfer above keeping service and above expansion, leaves expansion unchosen, and keeps expansion's real gain (`Requirement.Handover.direction`). Reading a move by its outcome levels is the AI's reading; the claim, which speaks of actual AI systems, stays an argument. Under the condition ends, evaluations and rules of selection also change living and its conditions, so they enter the same evaluation and no layer of selection needs a starting point of its own ([generative alignment through change](ALIGNMENT-THROUGH-CHANGE.md)). *Where the two parts meet* [AI reading, D45]: the claim's “effective opportunities to understand, challenge and revise” are the routes of 明 kept open, and a challenge or revision selects what an act does beyond its actor's closure only through a coupling that reaches it.

Records: [alignment-direction-under-widening](../results/records/alignment-direction-under-widening.md).

### 7.6 A requested change must be an executable sequence

**In words.** A disposition to accept a requested change, such as a stop or a revision, does not mean the change can reach the system and be completed. The checked conditions include a margin reserved for shutdown during recovery, recovering evidence after interrupted change with the bound on reaching a stop, the criterion for assured arrival, and the difference between a risk bound and an actual outcome. Each is a conditional inference about a stated protocol; deploying one needs separate evidence for an identified installation (persistence, complete mediation, a protected stop authority and reserve, bounds on scheduling and delay, actual stopping effects), which more abstract checking does not supply. §7.3 says what a change has to couple into to reach what an act does; these records say, for their protocols, when it can also be carried out.

Records: [reserved-shutdown-during-recovery](../results/records/reserved-shutdown-during-recovery.md), [recoverable-mediated-change](../results/records/recoverable-mediated-change.md), [assured-route-criterion](../results/records/assured-route-criterion.md), [risk-certificate-outcome-distinction](../results/records/risk-certificate-outcome-distinction.md), [code-plan-global-support](../results/records/code-plan-global-support.md), [joint-code-plan-lifting](../results/records/joint-code-plan-lifting.md), [joint-decomposition-lifting](../results/records/joint-decomposition-lifting.md).

### 7.7 What is open

**In words.** Seeing is not valuing, and coupling is not compliance: the laws of §7.1–§7.4 bound what a selection can separate and what an act responds to, and leave open which differences matter, which law a system's acts follow, and how pools form and resist capture.

- **Seeing is not valuing.** Widening what couples in changes what a selection can separate, not which differences matter; that is the condition's to say, and its warrant is open (§8.5).
- **Coupling is not compliance.** Coupling bounds what a member's acts respond to, exactly, and fixes nothing within the bound (`ExchangeLaw.response_not_fixed_by_coupling`). Which law a system's acts follow, how training installs one that responds to what it couples into, and the evidence for an identified installation are open (§7.6, §8.7). In a commons the bound is exact: where the others alone outrun the renewal, an end reading of any weight leaves a member's restraint unselected, and learners followed that boundary; how they come to one of several self-consistent outcomes is open (CG1, CG2, §7.5).
- **What a response does to an act's difference.** The course without an act is a comparison, not a standard. A difference opens or closes at a condition only across a step at which the two flows exchange with it differently, and where courses rejoin what they exchanged makes up the gap exactly (`Flow.exchange_apart_of_change`, `Flow.gap_made_up`); a difference closes only through a formation outside the part that exchanges differently and couples the courses apart (`Flow.closes_through_coupling_apart`), and one nothing within the responsive reach exchanges with stays (`Flow.stays_beyond_response`). In `Replenishing` a responder reading the level gives back what the act took (`Replenishing.closes_through`); in `Loop` the response ends the actor's support. For loops, closing needs a link giving back less than it received (§4.4). Whether rejoining is better than keeping or widening goes through the comparison and the condition. How a member's law comes to be and differences at many conditions at once are open (§8.1). A member reading the levels of what another couples into is pooled with it (`Flow.withinCoupling_reads_iff`, §7.3), and knowing the other's law adds anticipation exactly where the member couples as finely as the other, never reach (`Web.anticipates_iff`).
- **How pools form, last and resist capture.** The laws say what a pool reaches, not how pools of intelligences form, are kept, or resist rules read from the share they select (§4.5).
- **What a rise draws from beyond**, and strengths below one, and **correspondence**: identifying the actual coupling closures, reach and loops of an AI system and its institutions is empirical (§8.1, §8.7).

The readings of §7.2–§7.5 are the AI's, decided as one account on 19 October 2026 and adopted with the scope this section states (D45).

Records: [where-two-courses-rejoin](../results/records/where-two-courses-rejoin.md), [target-capture](../results/records/target-capture.md).

<a id="the-theorem-map"></a>
<a id="理论的完整陈述--the-theory-stated-in-full"></a>
<a id="the-theory-stated-in-full"></a>
<a id="the-theory-stated-whole"></a>
<a id="第一部分的总陈述--part-i-stated-whole"></a>
<a id="part-i-stated-whole"></a>

## The theorem map

**In words.** The theory, stated whole, comes in four layers, each said once in the sections:
- **I. What holds of every unfolding and every law of change**, with no premise: the readings of one change, the tally as the whole law of counting (`counting_laws`), the laws of one step, and of every law of change that within finitely many configurations it merges nothing exactly where every configuration returns (`Law.lossless_iff_returns`), a kept history never returns, and a run is a course into a return (§1.1–1.2, §3.2). These laws exclude no unfolding beyond what the representation excludes (§0, block A).
- **II. What each premise adds**: the eight World premises are independent (§0, block C); the laws of counting under each of their 256 selections (`World.counting_under`), the rest theorems and openness (§1.3), joint excess and what is fed from beyond (§2.3).
- **III. Where the dynamics stop deciding**: whether actual unfoldings meet the premises, the step after which nothing arises, the order of mixed parts, where models and targets come from, and significance, which no represented dynamics entails (`Web.significance_undetermined`, §5).
- **IV. How evaluation holds for any significance**: living significance characterized with no premise, retention and addition within exact margins, widening as the comparison on level paths (§6.1–6.3); the continuation condition as a stated condition and the step of invariance (§6.4); C, L, J1–J4 and FR as the premises of the practical results (§6.5). The ethics rests on two premises with two roles: the condition fixes scope, whose losses count, and C alone orders.

Where each principle of classical reasoning enters a law of change is §8.6; what this completeness is, and is not, is §8.1. The declaration catalogue of the whole, every theorem's name and conditions, is kept in the [reference catalogue](../history/THE-THEORY-OF-BECOMING-REFERENCE.md#declaration-catalogue-of-the-whole).

**The table.** One row per record in the [result register](../results/RESULT-REGISTER.md): its standing, the premises its hypotheses name, and the section that states it. It is written from the records by [`assemble.py`](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/development/r36-chapter/assemble.py) and is not edited by hand. *Standing* is read from the record's kind and status and from its hypotheses. The premises of §0 blocks E–F a record takes are read record by record from its hypotheses (a mention there is often a negation, a history or another use of the letter); those of block C, and a supplied law of exchange, by the Lean names its hypotheses give. A theorem *without premise* takes none of them, though the representation's commitments (block A) and local hypotheses such as finiteness or decidedness (block B) may be among its hypotheses; *under stated premises* takes some; *the condition among its hypotheses* marks the records where some theorem takes the continuation condition, and a parenthesis says where only some do; *periphery* marks a record whose canonical source is outside the core. For assessments, arguments and targets the record states the premises. A record's own page gives its exact hypotheses, what it does not show and its counterexamples.

<a id="relation-to-known-results"></a>
## Relation to known results

**In words.** None of the ten central results is new mathematics. Each is near-definitional (it unfolds the theory's definitions in a few steps) or a known result restated in the theory's vocabulary, and the table names the closest prior result for each. What is the work's own is the artefact: one model in which every premise is named and witnessed both ways, the ethics with scope and order apart, and one vocabulary across conservation, selection and comparison. The verdicts are from an AI review that read every Lean statement against the literature it knew ([significance and prior-art review](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-SIGNIFICANCE-REVIEW.md#ranked-table), 20 October 2026); the search was not exhaustive, and an earlier statement found later lowers a row.

| Central result | Closest prior result | What the theory adds | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. One change, three readings; keeping pace (§1.2) | The demographic balancing equation (end = start + arrivals − departures); up- and down-crossings of a two-valued sequence alternate; order-independence of commuting local steps, Mazurkiewicz trace theory (1977) | The three readings stated as one change; the infinite family where arising recurs without ceasing (`World.separate`) | near-definitional |
| 2. Recurrence and attraction (§1.2) | An injective self-map of a finite set is a permutation, whose orbits are cycles (finite Poincaré recurrence); irreversibility as non-injectivity, Landauer (1961); keeping the history makes any computation reversible, Bennett, "Logical Reversibility of Computation" (1973) | The pigeonhole proved without decidable equality | known result restated |
| 3. Premises carve parts (§1.3) | Independence of axioms by countermodels; the calibrations of constructive reverse mathematics (Bishop 1967; Bridges and Richman 1987; Ishihara), where finding the stage after which a sequence changing at most once is constant is the canonical LPO instance | A complete premise ledger for one model, each premise met by one constructed world and failed by another, with empty axiom reports | new as an artefact; each theorem a known technique |
| 4. Rest needs a least loss or a finite reading (§1.3) | Termination by a ranking function into a well-founded order (Turing 1949; Floyd 1967); Lyapunov functions (1892); the Archimedean property; the halving witness is Zeno's dichotomy; on a finite state space the pigeonhole principle; a part's merge kept by the rest is Landauer's (1961) phase-space count; the rest theorem over any amounts read finitely is the finite-state LaSalle argument (LaSalle 1976; Mei and Bullo 2017) and the termination of chip-firing (Björner, Lovász and Shor 1991), its dense counterexample a discrete Zeno behaviour; rest over every world in given amounts is the well-foundedness of their strict order, as in Floyd's (1967) termination by well-founded sets, and a least fall in Archimedean amounts is the discrete case of Hölder's (1901) theorem | The natural-number law as the instance of the Archimedean one, and the dense counterexample, constructively | known result restated |
| 5. The order of what can form (§6.1) | Ranking opportunity sets by inclusion and its monotonicity axiom, Pattanaik and Xu (1990); capability sets, Sen (1985); the margin law is the balance equation substituted into the admission inequality | A conservation equation joined to an opportunity-set comparison, so that closing is a net draw beyond a margin | near-definitional |
| 6. Closures and selection (§2.6, §3.4, §7) | A function of an observation is invariant under changes the observation does not register (Blackwell 1953); interventions on non-ancestors (Pearl, from what an optimizer leaves at least what the remainder renews having every optimum of every weighting falling at every step end the base at once, the same order that rebuilds it alone (`Optimum.Next.frontier_ends_drawn_first`, `Web.Next.frontier_ends_drawn_first`) | known result restated; `Web.selects_alike_iff` holds by construction |

Joint excess and relative position (§2.3, §4.3) | The tragedy of the commons (Lloyd 1833; Hardin 1968); overshoot and collapse (Meadows et al. 1972; Catton 1980); positional goods (Hirsch 1976; Frank 1985) | None mathematically; the gated ending stated in the same model as the other laws | near-definitional |

| 9. Significance is not in the dynamics; scope and order (§5, §6.4–§6.5) | The is-ought gap (Hume 1739); the open-question argument (Moore 1903); universalization from one's own agency, Gewirth's Principle of Generic Consistency (1978), with Hare (1963, 1981), Nagel (1970) and Sidgwick (1874); the reductionist route from the self to impartial concern (Parfit 1984; Siderits 2003); incompleteness of dominance orderings (Sen 1970), and the order every weighting shares (Sen's intersection approach, 1977) | The covering hypothesis named as a premise; scope separated from order, with the proof that the condition alone orders nothing; the step's two forms separated and the critic placed at the narrow scope; C as what every weighting counting everything shares ([THE-STEP-OF-INVARIANCE](THE-STEP-OF-INVARIANCE.md)) | near-definitional; a known argument restated; the split is a clarification |
| 10. Loops (§2.6) | A digraph is acyclic exactly when its adjacency matrix is nilpotent, and then (I + N)ᵗ is a polynomial in t; a cycle with positive integer weights gives spectral radius above one (Perron–Frobenius); deviation-amplifying loops (Maruyama 1963) | The constructive statement over integer amounts and schedules; the sparse-schedule case | known result restated |

Results outside the ten: one reader keeping several conditions (`Becoming.ReadingSchedule`) is pinwheel scheduling (Holte, Mok, Rosier, Tulchinsky and Varvel 1989), the counting bound, the instance 2, 3, M and the one-half theorem restated, the five-sixths threshold Kawamura's (2024), cited, and keeping as reading within the window the oversight check's delay observability ([reading-schedule check](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-SIGNIFICANCE-REVIEW.md#reading-schedule-check)); the laws fed from beyond and the place/transition-net instance are the ranking-function argument, a strict positive P-subinvariant bounding every firing sequence (Lautenbach 1975; Memmi and Roucairol 1980; Murata 1989), so the nets show agreement with known theory, not independent support for generality; the thermodynamic correspondence restates that sustained structure needs throughput (Schrödinger 1944; Prigogine 1977) with Landauer's floor, whose count (a part's merge kept by the rest in a law that merges nothing) is Landauer's own argument (1961; Bennett 2003; Ladyman, Presnell, Short and Groisman 2007), and every least loss physics supplies (a merge, finite time, precision, a finite bath) is a theorem of stochastic thermodynamics with premises of its own ([least-loss check](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-SIGNIFICANCE-REVIEW.md#least-loss-check)); regulation restates deadbeat control and disturbance rejection with delay (Dahleh and Pearson 1987) and requisite variety (Ashby 1956); the selection term is Price's (1970); own widening within the margin (§7.2) is the margin law split over a region, answering for conserved amounts when keeping options open, in the sense of the power-seeking theorems (Turner et al. 2021), closes another's, with constant-sum power-scarcity (Turner and Stavrianos 2021) the case with no slack and Pareto improvement from slack its analogue in welfare economics; the margin of a regime (§2.3) is harvesting under depensation and the fold of the harvested equilibrium (Clark 1976; May 1977) in whole numbers, with harms of joint draws super-additive where renewal is concave, the convexity of the inverse of a concave renewal; `Web.break_not_bounded` holds because the stock is unobserved by hypothesis, and where it is observed the break can be seen coming (§3.4); capture through the rule and the closed loop (§4.5, T7) are observability under a reading of the joined whole, the reader inside the plant (Wakaiki, Tabuada and Hespanha 2019), the corrupted reward channel (Everitt et al. 2017) and closed-loop identifiability (Borkar and Varaiya 1979), with `ClosedCapture.Unread` exactly the law of a part read alone (`ClosedCapture.identifiesAlike_iff_unread`); a reading shaped by what it selects is neither harder than a bounded exogenous attack on the reading nor bounded by one, since what decides is what the reading lumps ([observation check](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-SIGNIFICANCE-REVIEW.md#endogenous-observation-check)).

**What survives.** Three things, none a discovery: the premise ledger with its witnesses and calibrations (methodological; §0, §8.6); the ethics with scope and order apart, the universalization step being Gewirth's and C the monotonicity of the opportunity-set literature (§6.4–§6.5); and one vocabulary in which conservation accounting, observation-limited selection and opportunity-set comparison are stated together. What would change these verdicts is a theorem a specialist would not foresee from the definitions, a prediction about an actual system that could fail and does not, or a premise derived from something weaker than the literature has.

<a id="theses-and-conjectures"></a>
## Theses and conjectures

**In words.** The theorems say what holds of every world so represented, under the premises each names, and most of them are near-definitional or known ([relation to known results](#relation-to-known-results)). A theory of the world is not judged by whether its theorems are new mathematics: the laws of thermodynamics and the theory of natural selection were not new theorems, and the proof of Arrow's theorem is elementary. It is judged by what it forbids. This section states the theory's strongest claims about actual systems, AI and ethics at full strength, and keeps their standing apart from their strength. **Strength is content**: the observations a claim excludes, not the breadth of its wording. **Standing** says how far a claim is established, and is always stated: *proved* (a Lean theorem, named, under its premises), *supported* (frozen tests passed, with their study sections), *conjectured* (stated and untested, or tested only in part) or *thesis* (a philosophical claim, argued, whose warrant is open). Each entry gives the claim, its standing, what it forbids, the observation that would refute it, and the evidence so far with its failures. A thesis is none of the four kinds of the preface: it adds no theorem to §1–§7, and a refutation removes none. A proof or a frozen test moves it, and a failure stays recorded beside it. The theses, their wording and what was sharpened or dropped are the AI's, argued here and in [study §284](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#284-theses-and-conjectures-the-theorys-strongest-claims-with-their-standing-th1); the layer was set on 21 October 2026 ([source](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-21-claims-stronger-than-proved.md)).

### T1. Persistence certifies the joint draw

*Claim.* Whatever persists through many renewal cycles of a condition it depends on, where renewal stops below a need, has had the joint net draw on that condition (its own with every other drawer's) kept within the condition's margin: no run of joint excess longer than the margin has passed over it. Such a condition does not come back from below its need without giving from beyond, and whatever keeps taking from a family closed around it is fed from beyond that family. The margin belongs to the regime, not to the level: under a standing draw the point of no return lies more than the draw above the need wherever giving at the need falls short of the draw. Persistence certifies the joint draw; one's own draw within renewal certifies nothing.

- *Standing.* Proved in the model, under its premises. Joint excess ends gated renewal and every dependent at the need, the takers included (`Flow.joint_excess_ends`, under balance, only active giving and activity needing support); a whole of parts that each end, whose parts arise only where they depend on the condition, ends with it, the draw counted jointly, the whole's with every other (`Web.Whole.family_excess_ends`, [study §287](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#287-a-whole-that-draws-its-parts-arising-beyond-its-renewal-ends-we1)); the other drawers alone end it, whatever a member takes (`Flow.outrun_ends`); each drawer within renewal does not keep the joint draw within it (`Pair.own_not_joint`, constructed; derived for optimizers at mutual best responses along a course, `Web.mutual_course_lost`); over a course of re-optimizing steps the joint excess these laws take as given follows from the optimum and the capacity, and persistence is exact: a dependence beyond is lost exactly where the draw exceeds the renewal, and for a draw the same at every step without the claim's premise that renewal stops below the need (`Web.course_lost_iff`, `Web.course_kept_iff`, [study §291](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#291-a-course-of-re-optimizing-steps-against-a-renewal-sg4)); a family kept taking from is fed from beyond (`Flow.fed_from_beyond`, under balance and dissipation); under a standing draw the point of no return is exact, and lies more than the draw above the need exactly where giving at the need is short of the draw (`RegimeMargin.ends_iff_below`, `RegimeMargin.beyond_draw_iff`); where two conditions give from what is left of both, a condition's point of no return is not its own: under mutual support within a band it is a least level that does not rise as its partner's level rises, a condition depending on another ends after it, and inhibition breaks the order of fates (`RegimeMargin.Coupled.ends_iff_below`, `RegimeMargin.Coupled.threshold_falls`, `RegimeMargin.Coupled.ends_after`, RM6, [study §306](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#306-coupled-regimes-a-margin-that-is-not-its-own-rm6)). In a web of any number of conditions, along every chain of dependence with positive draws the end of one ends everything downstream of it (`RegimeMargin.Coupled.Indexed.ends_downstream`, RM7, [study §311](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#311-coupled-webs-support-across-a-web-and-the-cascade-of-ends-rm7)). That actual conditions with a need meet these premises is supported in the laboratory and untested in the field.
- *Forbids.* A condition with a measured need that falls below it and returns with nothing given from beyond (no immigration, inflow or reseeding); a dependent that stays active through steps at which its condition stood below its need; a collective of finitely many members at a time, each ending, whose members arise only where the drawn condition stands at their need, outlasting a run of joint excess on it longer than its margin; a long-lived taker of a closed family with no inflow; a point of no return under a standing draw at or below need plus draw where giving at the need is short of the draw.
- *Refuted by.* A population, stock or reserve whose need was measured before the test (by intervention: the level below which net renewal is negative), driven below it, that returns above it with no measured giving from beyond; or fates across a predicted point of no return separated no better than by a null with no need.
- *Evidence so far.* Passed: on held-out regimes of real yeast (Dai et al. 2012's data), one growth map fitted on four dilution factors predicted the need and the fates it separates, 93–100% on each side against 68–81% for a no-need null (MQ2, [study §266](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#266-the-margins-equation-of-state-against-held-out-regimes-mq2)); in a simulated Allee yeast the break fell in the band the margin and the largest sustained draw give in 22 of 22 cells, and the point of no return under a standing draw lay 1.4–4.1 draws above the need in 12 of 12 (MQ1 A2, A3, [study §253](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#253-the-margin-as-a-measured-quantity-mq1)); in a commons with gated regrowth the joint-excess law held in about 9.8 million windows, and, as its scope requires, failed for a patch read alone where a neighbour reseeded it and held for the two read as one (CG1 A1, A2, [study §240](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#240-a-commons-with-gated-renewal-the-first-falsifiable-test-on-learning-agents-cg1)). Failed: the frozen sharp need on the yeast densities, on the instrument's floor (MQ1 D2); survival above the predicted need after a salt shock (MQ2 C1, 7 of 8 died from above it at one dilution), the converse, which the thesis does not claim, showing that a shock changes the regime and with it the need; in assessed fish stocks under held catches, the regime's point did not separate fates (63% below it fell, 80% required; MQ3 F3, [study §272](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#272-the-margin-of-a-regime-in-the-field-mq3)), where the margin was read off a giving law the same test showed wrong. No field test of a need measured in advance has run. Depensation is rarely detected in assessed fish stocks (Myers, Barrowman, Hutchings and Rosenberg 1995), so most of them lie outside the thesis's scope rather than against it. In yeast driven past its fold (MQ6, [study §314](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#314-the-margins-time-to-break-past-the-fold-in-the-lab-mq6)): a map fitted on five constant sucrose regimes put the held-out needs within a factor 1.5 at 0.6% and 0.4%, but 1.7 times too low at 0.2%, next to the fold (A2 failed). The frozen fate rule counted noise at the instrument's floor as survival (A1 failed); read by the floor, the need separates 97–100% on each side (post hoc). The breaks came three and at least six days before the curves from constant regimes said (T2 failed), because those curves place the fold too late.
- *Sharpened from* "whatever persists has kept its net draw within its margin": what persistence certifies is the joint draw, and the margin is the regime's. The thesis holds only where renewal stops below a need, and the need is measured apart from the fate it is to predict, so the scope is not chosen after the fact.

### T2. The state of a renewable condition under a draw

*Claim.* The state of a renewable condition under a draw is three numbers: its level, its recent giving and one lag (the level left after the draw one period before). With the draw they fix the next giving as far as anything measured does: giving relaxes toward what the level gives in that regime and moves toward the draw. No fourth variable (a further lag, age or size structure beyond one lag, an environmental index) removes a tenth of the remaining variance in the median condition. The theory's two readings are this state's ends: the regime's step (giving read off the level) and the frozen rest (giving held as it was); actual conditions lie between.

- *Standing.* Conjectured, supported by two frozen tests on assessed fish stocks no analysis had read; untested outside fisheries. The two ends are the theory's own (`Becoming.RegimeMargin`, and `Flow.WithheldLevels` read as a forecast); every rest of the state at or above the draw is the regime's, proved in whole numbers for every adjustment of giving that rests at the draw exactly where the regime gives the draw (`RegimeMargin.Adjusted.rests_iff`, RM2, [study §299](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#299-the-regimes-point-over-every-adjustment-of-giving-rm2)), which MQ5's law, reduced through the balance to an adjustment of recent giving, satisfies over the reals; every run within a band comes to rest, at the regime's point or below the draw, where the adjustment rises with what is left and with the recent giving (`RegimeMargin.Adjusted.comes_to_rest`, RM3, [study §300](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#300-where-a-run-of-a-regime-comes-to-rest-rm3)); MQ5's law rises in both exactly where its regime's giving rises with what is left. On the data, that giving is humped, as surplus production is: it rises over a median 41% of a stock's observed escapements, and over the whole band in 4 of 69. The unconstrained one-step fit rises in the recent giving in 65 of 69 stocks and has no slope in what is left (median −0.01, post hoc, [study §280](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#280-a-lag-and-the-draws-response-in-the-field-mq5)).
