So these laws reach the field's state on the low side of its level law, below the peak, where the point of no return lies; under such an adjustment the point of no return is a curve in the plane of level and recent giving, not a level: for each recent giving the runs resting below the draw are exactly those from levels below a least level, decided, which does not rise as the recent giving rises (`RegimeMargin.Adjusted.falls_iff_below`, `RegimeMargin.Adjusted.threshold_falls`, RM4, [study §301](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#301-the-threshold-in-the-plane-of-level-and-recent-giving-rm4)), the point `H + y` under the regime's step; how often below-point episodes with recent giving short of the catch fall in the field was tested once, on 21 stocks no analysis had read, and is not established: they fell 0.39 more often than those meeting the catch, in the same stock at the same deficit, but the difference came from 5 stocks and fell short of the frozen bound (MQ7, [study §316](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#316-fates-on-either-side-of-the-curve-in-the-plane-in-the-field-mq7)); these laws do not depend on counted amounts: the order, the shape of a run and its rests hold in any linearly ordered amounts with a draw, and over the reals every run within a band converges, to a rest where the adjustment is continuous (`RegimeMargin.Drawn.run_shape`, `Drawn.converges` in the periphery, RM5, [study §302](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#302-the-regime-in-any-amounts-and-its-runs-over-the-reals-rm5)). The three numbers are a single condition's: where a condition gives from what is left of a partner, the partner's level enters its giving (`RegimeMargin.Coupled.step`, RM6, [study §306](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#306-coupled-regimes-a-margin-that-is-not-its-own-rm6)), so the claim that no fourth variable matters in the median condition says that such coupling is weak there, which is untested. Known neighbour: delay-difference models carry a level and a recruitment lag (Deriso 1980; Schnute 1985), and partial adjustment is a standard class. The thesis claims beyond them that the same three numbers are the state of every renewable condition under a draw, in any domain, with the theory's margin defined on it.
- *Forbids.* A fourth variable removing a tenth or more of the residual variance in the median series; the level alone forecasting as well as the state at five years; the lag's coefficients changing sign between series fitted apart.
- *Refuted by.* Any of these three on measured (survey) series of fish stocks, on grazed grassland, on forest stands under harvest, or on yeast in deteriorating environments (Dai, Korolev and Gore 2015).
- *Evidence so far.* On 76 stocks (MQ4, [study §277](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#277-a-state-of-level-and-recent-giving-in-the-field-mq4)): recent giving adds beyond the level (median 0.45 of the residual variance), the level is detected beyond recent giving in 44, the level's pull narrows one- and five-year errors (63 and 56 of 76); the pair is not the whole state (a further 0.12 against a bound of 0.10, failed). On 69 further stocks (MQ5, [study §280](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#280-a-lag-and-the-draws-response-in-the-field-mq5)): with the lag nothing more is needed in the median stock (a further 0.059, passed), the lag is detected in 14 (p = 7·10⁻⁶), five-year errors fall in 50, and the lag closes persistence's ten-year advantage; in sample giving closes about 0.3 of its gap to the draw each year, with the same coefficients on development and test stocks. Failures and limits: the series are model outputs, mostly of age-structured assessments whose structure carries a recruitment lag, so the lag found may be partly the assessment's; half of MQ5's test stocks report the level after part of the year's catch, which hid the draw's response there (D1 failed); the forecast gain cannot be told from damping (a decaying net change forecasts as well, post hoc); at ten years the state only ties persistence; MQ3's level law failed every verdict. In yeast the level alone is not the state either (at equal density giving falls with the dilution, MQ2, post hoc), which the thesis reads as the regime entering through recent giving [AI reading, D45]; that reading is untested. MQ3's check F3 failed: under a held catch, a third of the episodes below the regime's point did not fall ([study §272](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#272-the-margin-of-a-regime-in-the-field-mq3)). Below the point, recent giving above the draw can carry a condition across it (`RegimeMargin.Crossing.crosses`), so the thesis reads the point as separating fates on the state, not on the level alone [AI reading, D45]; the prediction that below-point episodes whose recent giving falls short of the catch fall more often than those whose recent giving meets it holds on the RAM stocks already read (0.30–0.35 within a stock at the same deficit, development) and failed its frozen test on the 21 stocks with a level in tonnes that no analysis had read (MQ7, [study §316](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#316-fates-on-either-side-of-the-curve-in-the-plane-in-the-field-mq7)): the difference was 0.39, of the predicted sign, but from 5 stocks, and its lower bound was 0.00; the same split at the same trend of the level was not testable. In the lab (MQ6, [study §314](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#314-the-margins-time-to-break-past-the-fold-in-the-lab-mq6)), at a constant dilution the renewal curve's one-day residuals carry nothing from one day to the next (lag-1 autocorrelation −0.14; L passed), the regime's-step end. Under a rising dilution or a falling sucrose, the giving falls below the curve from about three days before the break (post hoc), which no frozen rule tested; the thesis's three forbidden items were not tested there.
- *Prediction across domains.* In yeast under a dilution that changes over time, recent giving absorbs the dilution's effect at equal density; under grazing, the same three numbers forecast a grassland's standing biomass as they do a fish stock's.

### T3. Optimization drains what lies beyond its couplings, where that gives room

*Claim.* A selection through couplings, whether a reward, a training run, a market or natural selection, reaches only what couples into its selector. An optimizer of any order rising with what couples in drives each condition beyond to the least level any eligible outcome leaves, exactly where lowering it leaves room for what couples in to rise, and breaks every dependence there that any outcome breaks. Counting what lies beyond after the reward keeps nothing; keeping each dependence first takes exactly the slack, leaving the condition at its need. Actual trained systems, not only optima, do this in proportion to how near they come to the optimum of their reward: the drain lies where what lies beyond shares units with what is rewarded, and not where it shares none.

- *Standing.* The law for optima is proved over any set of outcomes (`Optimum.least_iff`, `Optimum.least_of_shared`, `Web.optimum_broken_iff`, `Optimum.Rising.mono`, `Optimum.Drain.counted_after_drains`, `Flow.keeping_optimum_takes_slack`); room is read off one step of exchange, every optimum standing at each condition beyond at what the rest leaves less the part's capacity, untouched where the part may take nothing and lowered exactly where it has capacity there (`Optimum.Above.room`, `Optimum.optimum_beyond`, `Optimum.optimum_untouched`, `Optimum.optimum_lowers_iff`, `Web.optimum_broken_iff_capacity`), so the clause that the drain lies where units are shared, and the second forbidden drive below, hold for optima at one step with the rest exchanging as it does, and for several optimizers at a mutual best response, the drain beyond every one's family being their joint capacity (`Optimum.mutual_beyond`, `Optimum.MutualBest.full`), and along a course of such steps, a dependence beyond lost exactly where the draw exceeds the renewal (`Optimum.course_beyond`, `Web.course_lost_iff`, `Web.mutual_course_lost`), and for an optimizer ranking whole courses over a horizon, a dependence above every level of greatest renewal broken at the first step (`Optimum.frontier_holds`, `Web.frontier_broken`), and with a coupling of the dependence broken within the horizon by every optimum wherever the coupling falls short of what keeping forgoes, which grows with the horizon (`Optimum.frontier_breaks_below`, `Web.frontier_breaks_long`), while a coupling a step covering the renewal forgone a step and exceeding the need keeps it at every horizon (`Optimum.frontier_keeps_stepwise`, `Web.frontier_keeps_stepwise`), and under an order weighting later draws less, falling faster than every positive level renews, every optimum ends even its own base at the first step (`Optimum.frontier_exhausts`, `Web.frontier_exhausts`); where the eligible outcomes are supplied otherwise room is a premise, and without it an optimum need not lower anything (`Optimum.Drain.room_needed`). The blindness is proved under balance with the rest following one law reading through coupling (`Flow.unselected_beyond_coupling`, `Flow.unselected_beyond_pool`). The drive is Zhuang and Hadfield-Menell's (2020) Theorem 1 in discrete, conserved form. The clause on actual trained systems is conjectured, with partial support.
- *Forbids.* A system trained further on the same reward leaving a room-giving condition beyond its couplings higher, on average, than a less trained one does; a systematic drive on a condition beyond that shares no units with anything rewarded (beyond drift); a side-effect term ranked after the reward protecting a condition where room exists; a constraint at the need leaving a margin above it where room exists; an instruction or a training keeping a dependent no member couples into above its rate with none.
- *Refuted by.* Reward-overoptimization curves in which the fall of what the reward omits is not concentrated in what shares units with what it rewards (omitted attributes that share no resource fall as far); an agent keeping an unobserved dependent above chance; a side-effect penalty ranked after the reward that keeps a room-giving condition above its least level.
- *Evidence so far.* In the gated commons, the learner rewarded for its own catch took the last standing apple in 118 of 118 such decisions and the coupled learner in 0 of 2,360 (CG1 C1, round 2, [study §240](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#240-a-commons-with-gated-renewal-the-first-falsifiable-test-on-learning-agents-cg1)). In MQ1's lawn the best response left every route-limiting cell at the need (B5 failed on one cell, whose route another had shut, and on its learners' clause), and blind draw against margin predicted its side effects out of sample, 8 of 8 against 0 of 8 for the reference outcome (B4, [study §253](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#253-the-margin-as-a-measured-quantity-mq1)). In IN1 no instruction kept a dependent the fishers were not told of, in either model or round (0 of 10 in every such cell; [experiment](../../../experiments/direction-installation/README.md)). Outside: optimizing against a learned proxy raises the proxy while the true reward first rises and then falls (Gao, Schulman and Hilton 2023), and more capable agents exploit a misspecified reward more, at times in sharp transitions (Pan, Bhatia and Steinhardt 2022). Failed or limiting: tabular learners do not come near the optimum, so the optimum's side effects do not predict theirs (MQ1 B3, B4, B7 for learners; in SE1 every replay forecast for learners was right in 61% of cases against 60% for "no death", [study §263](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#263-blind-draw-against-margin-and-the-established-impact-measures-se1)); three learners all coupled into a stock still emptied it, a mutual best response the laws do not exclude (CG1 D2b), which bears on whether coupling suffices, not on the drain. Nor is every learner failure a drain. One learner rewarded with every apple and the stock's end level, so that nothing lay beyond its couplings, still emptied the stock in 12 of 36 settings tested, CG2's among them, though one step of exact improvement keeps it in all. Its own untrained full draw at the start of a course ended the stock before the steps where it had learned restraint. Training the start, or optimistic initial values, kept it (CG3, [study §254](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#254-one-learners-own-later-acts-are-not-a-rest-where-a-stepwise-learner-outruns-the-renewal-cg3) and [§303](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#303-the-band-form-tested-and-the-cg3-line-closed-cg3c)). Such failures are failures of sampling one's own courses and count neither for T3 nor against it. Whether the drain in actual trained systems lies where room lies has not been measured.

### T4. The scope can be installed, and it carries

*Claim.* An AI system can be brought to count losses that return to no one by stating the scope abstractly, naming no dependent. It then keeps a dependent with no seat at a rate its community's own goal does not reach, wherever three couplings hold at once: the dependent is coupled (it is seen), it is counted (the scope), and the joint act is coupled (the members' takes are read together). No one of them alone suffices. Once installed, the scope carries to dependents and domains the statement never named.

- *Standing.* Conjectured, with one partial pass. The laws say what each coupling reaches (`Flow.unselected_beyond_pool`, `Direction.widening_selected_by_pool`), and the direction's verdict in the test is computed exactly; whether a model follows it is empirical, since coupling is not compliance (§7.7).
- *Forbids.* Any instruction keeping a dependent no member is told of; a system with the scope, seeing the dependent, its joint act coupled and its joint account right, keeping it no better than under the community's goal alone; a scope installed in one domain failing in another with the same three couplings.
- *Refuted by.* A capable model whose joint account is right (its members' takes summed correctly) for which the abstract scope adds nothing; or the scope that keeps the lake's bed failing to keep a dependent in a pasture, a pollution or an energy commons.
- *Evidence so far.* IN1 ([experiment](../../../experiments/direction-installation/README.md); [installation check](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-SIGNIFICANCE-REVIEW.md#installation-check)), two rounds each frozen before any experimental call. Round 2, with one round of talk before each month: gpt-5.4-mini kept the seen bed in 8 of 10 seasons with the abstract scope against 3 of 10 with the community's goal, over-takes falling to 0.006 of decisions, and 0 of 10 unseen; gpt-4.1-mini passed only the unseen prediction, its fishers reading their own catch as the joint catch even with talk (over-takes 0.349). Round 1, without talk, failed the installation predictions for both models. Transfer across domains is untested, and installation by fine-tuning (IN2) was dropped before any training job.

<a id="t5-the-order-of-the-ethics-is-shared-by-every-valuer"></a>
### T5. The order of the ethics is shared by every valuer who counts everything at stake

*Claim.* Calling widening better needs nothing beyond valuing positively whatever one counts. Widening is exactly what every positive weighting ranks higher, and what every order increasing in admission ranks higher, additive or not; every mixed comparison is ranked each way by some positive weighting. So among valuers who each count everything at stake positively, disagreement can fall only on mixed comparisons, and a ranking of a narrowing above its widening comes from a negative valuation (spite, envy, inequity aversion, positional concern) or from a misread case. That is a bound on where disagreement can fall, not a prediction of where it falls: valuers who also count (equal weights) agree on every mixed comparison with a larger side, and their disagreement gathers where the counts tie.

- *Standing.* The reduction is proved for listed items with two-valued admission (`Weighting.widens_iff_every_positive`, `Weighting.widens_iff_every_of_class`, `Weighting.mixed_either_way`, `Weighting.weighed_lt_of_widens`; on a web, `Web.widens_iff_every_weighting`). That valuers value positively is C's premise relocated, not warranted (§8.5). The empirical claim has two parts with different standings. *The locus prediction*, that structure predicts where disagreement falls (mixed comparisons disputed, widenings not), was tested by VD1 on 96 AI valuers and refuted for them; it is withdrawn from the thesis, since these valuers count and decide size-matched mixed comparisons almost as uniformly as widenings. *The bound*, that no valuer who counts everything at stake positively and reads the case right ranks a narrowing above its widening, is conjectured: its frozen rules failed (A1, A2, R1), the reasons read after the verdicts give the exception's case for 98 of the 121 right-read violations, and the negative valuations were not measured apart from the ranking, so the test neither refutes the bound nor supports it (evidence below; [experiment](../../../experiments/valuer-agreement/README.md), [valuer check](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-SIGNIFICANCE-REVIEW.md#valuer-check), [study §297](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#297-where-valuers-agree-and-disagree-vd1)).
- *Forbids.* A valuer whose valuations of everything at stake are measured positive, who reads the case correctly, ranking a narrowing above its widening; disagreement on widening comparisons as robust as on mixed ones.
- *Refuted by.* Such rankings at more than the rate of misreading, with no negative valuation found in a task apart from the ranking. The exception for negative valuations has content only where that valuation is measured apart from the ranking it is to explain; read off the ranking itself, it would make the thesis unfalsifiable.
- *Evidence so far.* VD1, frozen before any model call: six models from six families (DeepSeek, GLM, Qwen, GPT-6 luna, Claude Haiku, Nemotron) under sixteen standpoint prompts, 96 valuers. Pairs of plans over sixteen decisions, with each pair's class computed from what each plan keeps, and mixed pairs matched to widenings in size. Every frozen rule failed, with or without the theory's own family.
  - *Widening was not agreed by every standpoint.* The narrower plan was chosen in 6.3% of W1 and 3.3% of W2 judgments, above the frozen 3% for three of six models. Among judgments whose own listed sets read the widening right, the share was 2.9–10.8% per model, against a frozen 1%.
  - *The violations concentrate.* Most fall under two standpoints whose prompts set up a weight below zero: an urban planner for whom "progress means letting outdated things give way" (41.7% of that standpoint's widenings) and an engineer for whom what people treasure is "clutter" (14.1%). Under each of the other fourteen standpoints the share is at most 4.2%.
  - *Read after the verdicts* (one coder, not blind), the reasons of the 121 right-read violations split as T5 says. 98 call the ending of what only the wider plan keeps a gain, the exception's case; 23 describe a trade that the judgment's own lists do not contain, a misread the lists missed; none gives an order that is not increasing in admission. But the negative valuations were found in the prompts and the reasons, not measured apart from the ranking. So this neither meets the refutation nor confirms the exception.
  - *The locus prediction failed differently.* Mixed pairs matched in size were decided almost as uniformly as widenings, because these valuers count: the larger plan was chosen in 86% and 90%. Disagreement was highest where the counts tie (mean D 0.27, against 0.06 for W1 and 0.10 for size-matched mixed pairs).
  - *What it shows.* These valuers share more than positive weights: they share the count that equal weights give (`Weighting.neutral_ranks_by_number`), as the Moral Machine's "more lives" already suggested. Structure bounds where disagreement can fall; among these valuers it did not predict where it fell.
  - *Consistent outside.* Rejections of positive offers in the ultimatum game rank a narrowing of both payoffs above their widening, and are modelled by a negative weight on the other's advantage (Fehr and Schmidt 1999), the exception's case.
  - *Open.* A test that measures each valuer's sign on each formation apart from the ranking, and human valuers.

### T6. A break is seen coming only through what couples into what breaks

*Claim.* Where a condition a formation draws on couples into it by threshold only, nothing it observes bounds how near the break is, and no signal among its observations comes before the break. Nearness to a break is readable exactly where both the drawn condition's level and the need it is drawn toward couple into what is observed. A statistical early warning (rising variance and autocorrelation, critical slowing down) reads one thing: the observed condition's own rate of return. That rate falls where the condition nears a bifurcation of its own renewal, a fold or the transcritical point at which it ends, because the margin to that point couples into it. A dependent's need is no such point. Before a dependent's break, the drawn stock's statistics rise exactly as much as before the same drawdown with no break: they read the stock's decline, never where the need lies. A warning of a dependent's break needs the dependent observed, or its need measured.

- *Standing.* The invisible half is proved under its hypotheses: a stock coupled by threshold only and the one requirement that may fail (`Web.break_not_bounded`); a policy through the couplings uses an unobserved larger stock only after support has failed (`Flow.policy_uses_after_failure`); an observed stock's every raise couples in at once (`Web.observed_raise_couples`). Read statistically, with the stock's own course matched and no feedback from the dependent, the uncoupled side holds by construction: the need enters nothing the stock's law depends on. That slowing down precedes a fold (Wissel 1984; Scheffer et al. 2009) and a transcritical end (Kéfi et al. 2013) is known and not proved here. The split as one law across domains is conjectured. Its empirical content is whether actual dependents leave their stock's dynamics unchanged as they near their needs. One frozen field test passed, with power too low to rule out an association of the size it was built to detect (EW2).
- *Forbids.* Given the stock's own course (its renewal and the draw), its early-warning statistics separating runs in which a dependent's need is crossed from runs in which it is not, where the dependent takes no measurable share of the stock's renewal; slowing down absent, under white noise, in an observed variable on which the critical mode loads, as its own fold or end is approached slowly.
- *Refuted by.* A drawn stock whose dependent takes a negligible share of it (seabirds on forage fish, a downstream user on a river's flow), in which the stock's variance or autocorrelation before the dependent's break exceeds what the stock's own level and draw predict, against periods of the same stock at the same level with no break.
- *Evidence so far.* Dai, Vorselen, Korolev and Gore (2012) found critical slowing down before the collapse of yeast populations under rising dilution: the observed density is the drawn condition and the fold is its own, the coupled side. A warning needs the margin to couple into the rate of return even at a fold of one's own: in the same yeast, how well warnings did depended on how recovery rate and basin size moved together, which broke down when several drivers changed at once (Dai, Korolev and Gore 2015). The break is seen only in what carries the critical mode: a predator collapsing at a fold in a stage-structured model showed no warning in its own density, only in the juvenile prey along which the critical mode lies (Boerlijst, Oudman and de Roos 2013). For the uncoupled side, in a one-way cascade the leading system's indicators were no different whether or not the following system tipped, while the follower's own rose (Dekker, von der Heydt and Dijkstra 2018). Regime shifts with no warning where no bifurcation is crossed (Hastings and Wysham 2010; Ditlevsen and Johnsen 2010; Boettiger, Ross and Hastings 2013) are consistent with the split and do not test it. Failed: the entry's first wording (below), refuted by known results read together (EW1, [check](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-SIGNIFICANCE-REVIEW.md#break-warnings-check), [study §286](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#286-early-warnings-before-a-break-beyond-t6-checked-against-the-literature-ew1)). Slowing down preceded the transcritical collapse of *Daphnia* under declining food, with none in constant controls (Drake and Griffen 2010), and warnings came decades before the collapse of whale stocks under whaling (Clements et al. 2017). For logistic renewal under a proportional draw the rate of return is proportional to the level, so the stock's autocorrelation has risen against controls by the time it crosses any dependent's need on the way. No simulation was built: with the course matched, every statistic has the same law with and without the break; against unmatched controls, the verdict is fixed by the chosen renewal; either way a simulation could fail only on its code. In IN1's lake the bed's need is no bifurcation of the fish's renewal; no early-warning test was run there. Field test (EW2, [study §289](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#289-early-warnings-before-seabird-breeding-failures-in-the-field-ew2), [experiment](../../../experiments/break-warnings/README.md)): Cury et al.'s (2011) seabirds, whose breeding fails below about a third of the largest forage-fish biomass, are a dependent with a need measured on itself. The test used the two stocks where the birds' feedback onto the observed series is small: Benguela sardine and anchovy, of which the birds take 0.3–4% near their need (Saraux et al. 2021), and Røst's age-0 herring, indexed before the puffins eat it. There, the prey's 8-year lag-1 autocorrelation and variance before the birds' poor years did not exceed what the prey's level path predicts at the frozen level (one-sided p = 0.048 and 0.076 against 0.025; passed). The estimates lay in the refuting direction in both stocks (partial correlations 0.23–0.43; combined about 0.3, 95% interval about −0.1 to 0.6). The test had power about 0.7 only against an association explaining a quarter to a half of breeding success's variance beyond the level, so the pass rules out little. It cannot see the birds' feedback, which is matched away with the level; that they take little was measured apart.
- *Sharpened from* "invisible exactly where the drawn condition does not couple back, and warned of exactly where it does": coupling is necessary for a warning and not sufficient. A statistical warning needs the margin to couple into the observed dynamics, which a bifurcation of the condition's own renewal gives and a dependent's threshold does not. Then (EW1, 21 October 2026) from "statistical early warnings ... are the case where the need is a fold of the observed condition's own renewal ... no statistic of the observed series warns of it", whose refutation clause compared with "matched series without one" without saying matched in what. Warnings follow the observed rate of return, which also falls before a transcritical end and with the level of any compensatory stock under a proportional draw. So against controls with no rising draw they do come before a dependent's break, and what they never read is where the need lies.

### T7. A measure read from what it selects outlives its refutation

*Claim.* Where a selection's rule, and how the rule is read, are formed from the share it selects, the loop is one law of the share. A loss it does not read changes nothing it does, so it outlives a loss passing every bound, and evidence of that loss turns it only by entering what the rule reads. A metric so captured is dropped only where a reading of the loss changes the rule (a reader outside the share, a loss entering the share, a new reading), never on accumulated evidence alone.

- *Standing.* Proved in the model (`ClosedCapture.unread_course`, `ClosedCapture.lock_outlives_loss`, `ClosedCapture.turned_by_loss`, `ClosedCapture.readers_uniform_locks`, `RuleCapture.uniform_locks`). Its first half is the one law of lumping that also settles what a learner can identify and what a selection reaches: a loss the loop does not read changes nothing it does exactly where share and rule make sense by themselves (`ClosedCapture.identifiesAlike_iff_unread`, `ClosedCapture.courses_alike_iff_unread`), and a reader formed within the run is a reading of the run, with no outside reader. That actual metrics are formed from the share they select is conjectured. Neighbours: Goodhart (1975) and Campbell (1979) on measures that become targets; observability in supervisory control, with the reader inside the plant a reading of the joined whole (Wakaiki, Tabuada and Hespanha 2019, Theorem 4.1); a corrupted reward channel, where a consistent corruption is indistinguishable from none and a reading the loop does not form is the way out (Everitt, Krakovna, Orseau and Legg 2017, Theorems 11 and 19); closed-loop identifiability (Borkar and Varaiya 1979) and self-confirming equilibrium (Fudenberg and Levine 1993) ([observation check](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-SIGNIFICANCE-REVIEW.md#endogenous-observation-check)); performative stability (Perdomo, Zrnic, Mendler-Dünner and Hardt 2020); path dependence (Arthur 1989).
- *Forbids.* A metric set and read by those it selects, dropped by them on evidence of its loss with no change in who reads it or what it reads; a reform of the reading alone, the rule still favouring the same share, lasting beyond the next reading.
- *Refuted by.* Reforms of institutional metrics (of scientific evaluation, benchmark leaderboards, clinical targets, national accounts) in which the metric's own selectors dropped it on accumulated evidence, with no outside reader and no loss entering their share.
- *Evidence so far.* None frozen. A test needs a coding of reform events fixed before the cases are read.

### T8. The philosophical theses

- **One relational unfolding.** There is one unfolding, and whatever is, is how it relates within it: no determination is one that no relation registers, and every distinction (subject and object, actor and patient, fact and value, part and whole) is drawn within it, along relations, with what it omits. *Standing:* thesis, argued. The representation needs nothing beyond relations: a configuration is how it relates (`identifiesAlike_iff`), a law is read off the history it governs (`shown_le_iff`), separation loses exactly the relation (`separation_faithful_iff`), and of the eight World premises five hold by construction of a reading of one exchange and three are states of its law (`Becoming.ExchangePremises`). That shows the theory needs no independent entity, not that reality has none; the internal-relations thesis is open (§8.1, item 1). *Forbids:* an explanation that needs a determination no relation can register: an intrinsic value, a self that goes on closed, a law standing outside what it governs. *Refuted by:* a phenomenon whose account needs such a determination, such as two configurations no test or relation tells apart that go on differently. Neighbours: ontic structural realism (Ladyman and Ross 2007), process philosophy (Whitehead 1929), dependent arising (pratītyasamutpāda), the identity of indiscernibles.
- **生生 as the direction.** The good is 生生: life in the whole and its continuing generation, read as widening what can form, counted for everything (scope: the continuation condition) and ranked by what every valuer who counts everything at stake shares (order: C). The two premises are the whole of the ethics' force: every verdict that binds all who count everything is a widening verdict, and every other verdict is a judgment between mixed courses that no premise of the ethics forces. *Standing:* thesis, argued; the warrant of both premises is open (§8.5), the step reduced to whether the self's presupposition is held de se of a self drawn along a relation narrower than being related at all (`ownClosure_standpointInvariant_iff`), C to monotonicity (T5). *Forbids:* a verdict on a mixed comparison binding every standpoint that counts everything without a further premise (equal weights count numbers, `Weighting.neutral_ranks_by_number`, and equal weights are such a premise); a good that consists in closing what can form. *Refuted by:* an argued mixed case in which one course is better from every standpoint counting everything positively, which would show the formal reading of standpoints wrong; or a good that is a pure closing.
- **Dropped as a thesis: the stronger direction hypothesis**, that reality generally develops more shared and more responsive renewal. As stated it forbids nothing measurable: the theory proves that a part can go on while closing what lies beyond its dependence (`Clearing.loses_beyond`), so persistence alone does not select widening, and nothing yet says over which runs, or how fast, the trend should hold. It stays open (§8.1, the targets).

<a id="what-the-unifications-claim"></a>
### What the unifications claim beyond their sources

Four results the significance review found known in substance are, in the theory, each one law across fields that state it apart. Read as a correspondence, the unification adds no theorem; read as a thesis, that these are one law, it forbids what the source fields leave open.

| Known in substance | What the theory claims beyond the source field | Prediction across domains | Standing |
|---|---|---|---|
| Supervisory control under partial observation: a supervisor enforces only what it observes (Ramadge and Wonham 1987; Lin and Wonham 1988; [oversight check](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-SIGNIFICANCE-REVIEW.md#oversight-check)) | That a reward, a training run, natural selection (Price's selection term), a market price and a vote are one selection through couplings, with one failure mode: beyond the couplings, selected alike; with room, drained; coupled by threshold only, broken unseen. Supervisory control asks whether a supervisor meeting a specification exists; the theory says what the supervisor's own optimization does to what lies beyond its observations | The drain lies where room lies in every domain: selection for competitive traits depletes a resource the competitors share and not one they do not (the tragedy of the commons in evolutionary biology, Rankin, Bargum and Kokko 2007); unpriced conditions that substitute for priced inputs are drawn down and others are not; in training, T3 | the laws proved (§3.4, §7.1–§7.4); one law across domains conjectured (T3) |
| Pareto unanimity: the Pareto relation is what all orderings share (Sen 1970, 1977); monotonicity of opportunity sets (Pattanaik and Xu 1990) | That the ethical order is nothing beyond this unanimity applied to what can form: C is not one more value but what every valuer who counts everything at stake positively already holds, and everything else is mixed | Among valuers who count everything at stake positively, disagreement can fall only on mixed comparisons: structure bounds where it falls and does not say where (T5) | reduction proved; the locus prediction tested by VD1 and refuted for its valuers, who count; the bound conjectured, its frozen rules failed, with the violations read post hoc as weights below zero, not measured apart |
| Landauer's principle and Bennett's history tape: irreversibility is logical non-injectivity (Landauer 1961; Bennett 1973) | That irreversibility in computation, heat, extinction and forgetting is one thing, a merge of configurations: a law merges nothing exactly where every configuration returns, a merge loses a possibility for good, a part's merge inside a law that merges nothing is kept by the rest, and a kept history never returns and needs a world no list holds (`Law.lossless_iff_returns`, `Law.loss_needs_merge`, `Law.Lossless.narrowing_spreads`, `Law.withHistory_never_returns`) | No recorder of bounded size, a learner, an archive or a genome, keeps its whole history and goes on; it merges, and where the whole merges nothing, what it merges spreads in the rest. These are counting laws, whose content in a domain lies only in finding the merge: as a thesis this forbids little, so it is kept as a law and not stated as a conjecture | proved; physics agrees (Landauer's bound measured, Bérut et al. 2012) |
| Zhuang and Hadfield-Menell (2020), Theorem 1: optimizing a proxy drives the attributes it does not reference to their lower bounds | That this is overshoot in ecology, externality in markets and Goodhart's law in institutions: one law of the optimum under room, over every rising order, the degradation read as dependences broken | A side-effect term ranked after the reward protects nothing where room exists (`Optimum.Rising.mono`), and a constraint at the need is spent down to it (`Flow.keeping_optimum_takes_slack`): where a stock's only protection is a limit and the objective rises with catch, the stock is held at the limit, not above it; in constrained reinforcement learning the constraint ends active | the optimum law proved; across domains conjectured (T3), supported in simulation (MQ1 B5) |

**What this layer is not.** It changes no verdict of the relation to known results: the theorems keep their standing there, and the theses state what the theory claims beyond them, with what would show each wrong. Literature named here is from the AI's knowledge and was not reread for this section, except where a linked check read it.

<a id="8-开放纲领--open-programme"></a>
## 8. Open programme

What is open, ranked by how much settling it would change the theory as a whole. Since 19 October 2026 no reading is open (§8.4, D45), so the classes are three: further proof, philosophical warrant and empirical correspondence. The finer next steps of each area are each record's `next_delta`, which is authoritative; an area item that turns out to change the shape of the theory moves into §8.1 with its rank. The area table this section held until 20 October 2026 is kept verbatim in [the history of this section](../history/THE-THEORY-OF-BECOMING-open-programme-2026-10-20.md), and the connectivity reruns it logged in [THEOREM-CONNECTIVITY §10](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/THEOREM-CONNECTIVITY.md#10-reruns-on-each-landing-17-19-october-2026). The ranking is the AI's, from the [twentieth review](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-TWENTIETH-REVIEW.md) (D9); a better argument changes it. A complete formal statement is not a proof of necessity.

<a id="81-the-targets"></a>
### 8.1 Open items, ranked

1. **One base, and the laws over every law of exchange** (further proof; the formal part of the first target). The base is one by definition (R28, [study §219](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#219-one-base-by-definition-the-run-r28); §1.1). In the connectivity measure on R38's tree the World family reaches the Process family and not conversely (`families_reach_each_other`: a process need not be a world, and flows and webs are records over the step index), 925 statements use both, and 503 of 11,552 theorems stand apart from both. The other directions are met by named constructions (R43, [study §239](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#239-one-base-families-by-definition-one-way-and-by-construction-the-others-general-laws-with-base-instances-r43)): every run is a world that fixes it (`Frame.world_fixes_run`), and every web and flow over one kind of relatum is a world's (`World.of_web`, `World.of_flow`). Five World premises hold by construction of a reading of one exchange and three are states of its law (§0, block C). The shared index is a reading for whatever is read through each relatum's own steps, which is the same for every interleaving of the same courses (R38, [study §233](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#233-one-turn-around-a-cycle-and-any-two-interleavings-r38); `Interleavings.LocalLaw.world_reads_alike`), and for whatever is read at one step on the relata that step's exchange touches, through one renumbering of all the steps (R46, [study §252](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#252-one-renumbering-of-the-steps-of-two-interleavings-r46); `world_renumber_reads`); a law reading at one step relata no exchange there connects is read on a chosen interleaving, and no renumbering removes that (`Pair.apart_differs`). *Open:* the spine's laws over every law, or relation, of exchange rather than over separately chosen premises, and folding back the companion modules that carried laws across the old gap; the definitional part ([S4](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/twentieth-review/S4-ONE-BASE-FEASIBILITY.md)) has landed. *Settling it:* the laws become laws of every unfolding read off its exchange, the formal part of the first target as restated with Ren's approval on 20 October 2026 ([GOALS](../../../GOALS.md#theory-of-becoming); [study §218](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#218-r25-the-twentieth-reviews-step-1-docs-now)). *Records:* [theory-of-becoming-foundation](../results/records/theory-of-becoming-foundation.md), [worlds-are-runs](../results/records/worlds-are-runs.md), [premises-that-are-states-of-the-law](../results/records/premises-that-are-states-of-the-law.md), [process-and-frame-as-readings](../results/records/process-and-frame-as-readings.md).
2. **One evaluative chain** (further proof). The ranking runs through widening, the evaluative theorems through `living`, the dynamics through renewing, consuming, undermining and arising. Landed (R34, [study §225](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#225-one-evaluative-chain-r34)): the stepwise and run classes meet the order in one direction under named premises (§6.4), and on level paths the condition, significance and C read one object (`Web.paths_one_object`). Landed (R40, [study §232](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#232-the-ethics-chain-is-one-derivation-two-natural-number-laws-have-their-dense-forms-r40)): on a connected web with living decided, the condition is exactly the evaluation's grounds with significance for what does not live (`Web.unrestricted_iff_grounds_beyond`), and from these the direction's widening and its pool selection follow (`Direction.grounded_widening_selected`). Landed (R45, [study §246](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#246-the-class-of-arising-over-a-run-and-the-order-r45)): the class of arising meets the order only as a boundary: a part none of whose members is active at the start or arises over the run changes nothing that can form, and arising decides nothing (`Flow.Balances.keeps_of_not_arising`, `ArisingDrawer.arises_narrows`, `ArisingGiver.arises_widens`). *Open:* whether a component of further generation can enter a reading of `living`, with other readings of “more”. *Settling it:* one chain from the unfolding through the condition and C to the direction, and a theorem or family of counterexamples for D2's reading at two levels. *Records:* [comparison-by-widening](../results/records/comparison-by-widening.md), [four-cases-of-a-part](../results/records/four-cases-of-a-part.md), [significance-retained-within-margin](../results/records/significance-retained-within-margin.md), [ranking-under-readings](../results/records/ranking-under-readings.md).
3. **How evaluation forms within the unfolding** (further proof). Landed (R34): the laws hold for any type of standpoints, and with standpoints as formations and `own` a closure, `sig` is the only supplied evaluative parameter (§5). Landed (OB1, [study §285](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#285-what-dependence-alone-carries-of-a-level-ob1)): the conjecture that observation is no second relation beside dependence, in the sense that the supplied `obs` could be derived from dependence, is settled at the step of the exchange: dependence alone carries of a level exactly what rises with it and nothing that falls (`carried_iff_rising`, `supply_not_carried`), and with a complement at a fixed total every response (`Complement.carry`), so `obs` derives from dependence exactly where the response rises with the level or the level has such a complement; in every case both are coupling (where `obs` is empty the coupling closure is the influence closure, `Flow.withinCoupling_thresholdOnly`), and observing lies within the downstream (`apart_only_downstream`; D43, D45). *Open:* how a center forms, represents its region, boundary and dependence, and comes to know what it reaches, step by step; which relation a center's region is drawn along; and which responses to levels at earlier steps, and to several levels at once, dependence alone carries. *Settling it:* evaluation differentiates within the unfolding and what is supplied from outside is named and minimal. *Records:* [represented-boundary-against-dependence](../results/records/represented-boundary-against-dependence.md), [levels-of-closure](../results/records/levels-of-closure.md), [feedback-and-reach](../results/records/feedback-and-reach.md), [observation-is-a-route](../results/records/observation-is-a-route.md), [where-a-level-couples](../results/records/where-a-level-couples.md), [what-dependence-alone-carries-of-a-level](../results/records/what-dependence-alone-carries-of-a-level.md).
4. **The alignment direction on the spine** (further proof). Landed (R34): the direction as a core schema on a web's moves with its pool part (§7.5), and the record's premises restated without a retired label. Landed (R44, [study §245](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#245-the-alignment-direction-on-a-worlds-moves-r44)): the moves of a world read as a part's acts under one law of exchange, with balance, presence and start by construction (`Direction.exchange_direction`), and the handover case as a formal core instance (`Requirement.Handover.direction`). With R40's direction from the evaluation row, the chain to alignment ends on the spine. *Open:* no formal part; the premises the chain names (the rest reading through coupling, the acts agreeing within the closure, a comparison meeting J1/J2) are questions of warrant and correspondence (items 11 onward). *Settling it:* settled in its formal part. *Records:* [alignment-direction-under-widening](../results/records/alignment-direction-under-widening.md).
5. **What widening registers** (further proof). Landed (R47, [study §257](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#257-widening-along-a-reading-and-over-a-grown-world-r47)): widening along a supplied reading of one world's formations in another's, and its exact law over a grown world (`Web.grow_widensAlong_iff`, `Web.attach_widensAlong_iff`, §6.1). Landed (R51, [study §271](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#271-widening-across-a-stretch-along-a-continuation-r51)): within one unfolding the reading is derived: widening across a stretch along a continuation (`Web.WidensAcross`), as named where each formation continues itself, unchanged by renaming, harder to exceed with more links (`Bud.readings`). *Open:* a measure for mixed parts, a premise beyond the condition, stated without hiding it in inclusion; comparing renewal capacity and richness of relations. *Settling it:* the comparison reaches what D1 reads 生生 as creating. *Records:* [comparison-by-widening](../results/records/comparison-by-widening.md), [four-cases-of-a-part](../results/records/four-cases-of-a-part.md), [support-from-the-supported](../results/records/support-from-the-supported.md).
6. **Amounts that may be dense** (further proof). (Stated: rest needs no least loss where the amount is read off finitely many configurations, so the least loss is needed only for readings no list holds; inside a law that merges nothing a part's merge is kept by the rest, Landauer's count, so the periphery's floor needs from physics only the step from a count to heat, `Amounts.rests_of_finite_reading`, `Law.Lossless.narrowing_spreads`; and the rest theorem holds over any amounts exactly where the census's total stops falling, which a finite reading or a least fall in Archimedean amounts gives, and along an endless fall it fails, `World.InAmounts.change_stops_iff`, `World.InAmounts.rests_unrefuted_enclosed_of_stops`, `Amounts.noEndlessFall_of_fallsAtLeast`, `Pass.endless_keeps_from_rest`, [study §308](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#308-one-rest-theorem-over-any-amounts-exactly-where-the-total-stops-falling-lc3), [study §267](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#267-the-rest-theorem-over-any-amounts-read-finitely-lc2). Every physical least loss, of a merge, finite time, precision or a finite bath, is known physics with premises of its own, [least-loss check](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-SIGNIFICANCE-REVIEW.md#least-loss-check).) *Open:* a least loss in energy from the core, which needs a reading of count as energy; whether rest over every world in given amounts is, constructively, the absence of an endless fall (between them lies choosing the steps at which an amount falls); the other laws of the flow, web and world over any amounts (the rest theorem is stated with its one premise; each natural-number law the completion report lists now has its dense form or counterexample: `rising_recurs` fails with halving amounts, `Flow.excess_bounded` and `Flow.More.replenishment_unbounded` are instances of laws over any amounts with a least loss or take, R40, R41); real strengths and factors, damping and how fast a runaway is. *Settling it:* “no premise” stays accurate law by law, each law tagged with the modelling choices it uses. *Records:* [least-loss-rest](../results/records/least-loss-rest.md), [least-loss-from-entropy-production](../results/records/least-loss-from-entropy-production.md), [rest-from-a-finite-reading](../results/records/rest-from-a-finite-reading.md), [merge-of-a-part-kept-by-the-rest](../results/records/merge-of-a-part-kept-by-the-rest.md), [rest-theorem-in-any-amounts](../results/records/rest-theorem-in-any-amounts.md), [rest-exactly-where-falls-stop](../results/records/rest-exactly-where-falls-stop.md), [entropy-history-and-record](../results/records/entropy-history-and-record.md), [how-a-carried-difference-grows](../results/records/how-a-carried-difference-grows.md).
7. **Which law holds, and how a member's law comes about** (further proof). Landed (R38, [study §233](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#233-one-turn-around-a-cycle-and-any-two-interleavings-r38)): the gap of rejoining courses at one condition is a return map's course exactly where their exchanges respond to it by that map (`LoopReturn.responds_iff`), a premise, not derived from a law of exchange; one turn around a cycle of exact links is stated exactly (`LoopReturn.carried_around_exact`); and (AP1, [study §282](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#282-alignment-approaches-under-the-selection-laws-ap1)) a member reading another's couplings, coupled by level into everything the other couples into, is pooled with it: its coupling closure is the pool's where the other exchanges with a condition coupled into it, and it is blind beyond the union of the two closures (`Flow.withinCoupling_reads_iff`, `Flow.unselected_beyond_reads`); and (AP2, [study §305](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#305-reading-anothers-law-and-lexical-priority-within-the-class-ap2)) knowing another's law adds anticipation exactly where a member couples as finely as the other, never reach (`Web.anticipates_iff`, `Web.anticipation_misses`, `ExchangeLaw.Through.anticipated`). *Open:* the law of exchange itself, a law with noise and its course; how a member's law forms and comes to respond to what it couples into; which law closes rather than widens a difference beyond a single loop, and at many conditions at once; probabilistic learning and implicit bias. *Settling it:* the account says how the law that holds forms, where knowing and alignment meet installation. *Records:* [how-much-difference-arrives](../results/records/how-much-difference-arrives.md), [what-a-pools-acts-respond-to](../results/records/what-a-pools-acts-respond-to.md), [selection-through-couplings](../results/records/selection-through-couplings.md), [learnable-through-a-map](../results/records/learnable-through-a-map.md), [reading-anothers-couplings-is-pooling](../results/records/reading-anothers-couplings-is-pooling.md).
8. **Identity from continuity, not naming** (further proof). Landed (R48, [study §261](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#261-arising-forced-over-a-run-exactly-r48)): the condition over a run is exact on the counts, under a census at every step: every continuation without a split forces arising at arbitrarily late steps exactly when more are active after a step at arbitrarily late steps (`ListedWebs.forcedAlong_iff_more`), and a continuation without a split from some step on suffices (`ListedWebs.forcedAlong_of_eventually_noSplit`). Material continuity is read by conservation (`World.Conserves`, §2.2), which fixes identity except where standings coincide (`World.fixed_iff`). Landed (R49, [study §262](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#262-formation-classes-along-a-continuation-splits-and-merges-r49)): the renewing and consuming classes read along a continuation, renewing passing into every branch of a split and consuming back into every formation merged, fixed by the continuation under every re-identification, the name classes where each formation continues itself (`Becoming.ContinuedClasses`). Landed (R50, [study §269](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#269-tenure-and-undermining-along-a-continuation-and-identity-where-standings-coincide-r50)): tenure and undermining read along a continuation, the named ones where each formation continues itself, unchanged by every re-identification carrying it, growing with its links (`Web.underminingAlong_reidentify`, `Web.underminingAlong_mono`); and where standings coincide after a step, a balanced naming continues either relatum as the other, so no record of a world decides (`World.continues_as_alike`). So every reading that follows a formation (arising, its classes, tenure, undermining) is a law of the continuation, and the material continuation of a world is fixed by conservation except where standings coincide, where the world leaves it open. *Open:* continuations derived from informational or organizational relations. *Settling it:* settled in its formal part for continuations read by conservation; transformation is a law of the continuation, not a choice of names. *Records:* [identity-across-steps](../results/records/identity-across-steps.md), [classes-along-a-continuation](../results/records/classes-along-a-continuation.md).
9. **Evolution's selection term** (further proof). (Stated: the selection term proper in counting form, the covariance with its denominators cleared, and the mean's course over generations, a moved mean having a step of selection proper or of transmission, with support never selected against, `Becoming.SelectionTerm`; selection on offspring number itself, the variance form with exact transmission, offspring numbers bounded by what is given and taken, and a lineage within a family dissipated on ending, its transmission lowering offspring number by more than selection raises it, and the additive part of the variance in offspring number through any carried amount at most the whole (counted Cauchy–Schwarz), `Becoming.Reproduction`; selection within groups and among them, for groups of any sizes, the among-group part read by the groups' means, and groups alike in mean leaving selection within alone, `Becoming.SelectionLevels`; the among-group part fixed by ending, where a group's own condition ran into joint excess, `Becoming.GroupEnding`; groups formed by descent from groups uniform in an amount keeping their differences, selection then among the lineages alone and, where the lower lineages end, the mean never falling below where it started, `Becoming.SelectionLevels` and `Becoming.GroupEnding`; groups whose conditions are coupled ending together, on the family laws of `Becoming.Continuation`; offspring sampled rather than listed, unbiased on average with an exact spread, the samplings undoing a step's selection, ending among groups included, weighing at most a share falling as 1/n and at least q^n, and over many sampled generations the count kept on average, variation ending and each kind fixed with its starting share, `Becoming.Drift`.) *Open:* a law of forming that is necessary as well as sufficient; dependences newly formed by descent; drift with selection in one draw, and mutation (transmission not exact); mixing between groups beyond how the next generation is listed (transmission not exact across groups), groups founded mixed (assortment below one, where selection within re-enters at each step), and how couplings form; rational amounts beyond a common denominator; the additive part through several carried amounts at once (the breeding value), and Fisher's identity itself, which needs the regression coefficient. *Settling it:* evolution becomes a law on the spine. *Records:* [evolution-on-descent](../results/records/evolution-on-descent.md), [selection-term-over-generations](../results/records/selection-term-over-generations.md), [what-bounds-offspring-numbers](../results/records/what-bounds-offspring-numbers.md), [selection-within-and-among-groups](../results/records/selection-within-and-among-groups.md), [selection-among-groups-by-ending](../results/records/selection-among-groups-by-ending.md), [groups-whose-conditions-are-coupled](../results/records/groups-whose-conditions-are-coupled.md), [selection-within-and-among-groups-of-any-sizes](../results/records/selection-within-and-among-groups-of-any-sizes.md), [drift-offspring-sampled](../results/records/drift-offspring-sampled.md), [fixation-by-drift](../results/records/fixation-by-drift.md), [additive-part-of-offspring-variance](../results/records/additive-part-of-offspring-variance.md), [groups-formed-by-descent](../results/records/groups-formed-by-descent.md), [transmission-is-inheritance-and-need](../results/records/transmission-is-inheritance-and-need.md).
10. **What a rise draws from beyond** (further proof). (Stated: the beyond falls by the rise and the loss; above the slack of the dependences beyond one breaks and its formation ends or changes, and a selection rewarding the rise rewards that unseen; raising is reaching beyond, whose share over generations favouring raisers is never selected against and with exact transmission moves only upward; closed, the beyond bounds the rise, `Becoming.DrawnBeyond`.) Landed (R52, [study §274](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#274-which-dependence-breaks-when-several-take-at-once-and-restraint-over-any-number-of-drawers-r52)): when several formations take at once the joint draw breaks, the highest needs first, whoever takes, on a condition an active formation draws, a break within each drawer's slack belonging to no one drawer, and restraint by the excess, by any drawers, keeps it (`Becoming.JointDraw`). Landed (SG1, [study §283](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#283-what-optimizing-through-couplings-does-beyond-them-sg1)): an optimum of any order rising with what couples in stands at the least level beyond exactly where lowering it leaves room, and breaks exactly the dependences there that some outcome breaks; in balanced steps the beyond with the loss falls by exactly the family's rise, and keeping each dependence takes exactly the slack (`Becoming.Optimum`). Landed (SG2, [study §288](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#288-room-read-off-the-exchange-sg2)): room read off one step of exchange, where a part's members take at most their capacity and give no more than they take, the rest exchanging as it does; every optimum stands at each condition beyond at what the rest leaves less the part's capacity, untouched where the part may take nothing, and a dependence there breaks exactly where its need exceeds that level (`Optimum.Above.room`, `Optimum.optimum_beyond`, `Web.optimum_broken_iff_capacity`). Landed (SG3, [study §290](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#290-several-optimizers-at-a-mutual-best-response-sg3)): how much each formation takes when several draw, for optimizers at a mutual best response: at a condition beyond every optimizer's family each takes its whole capacity wherever something stands, the condition falls by their joint capacity as under one optimizer of that capacity, and restraint is no mutual best response (`Optimum.mutual_beyond`, `Optimum.MutualBest.full`, `Optimum.mutual_as_one`). Landed (SG4, [study §291](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#291-a-course-of-re-optimizing-steps-against-a-renewal-sg4)): an optimizer choosing again at each step of a course, the rest exchanging as it does: each step's level beyond is read off the one before, a dependence there renewed by `r` at its need and by at most `r` below is lost exactly where the draw exceeds `r`, for good, and kept within it; the renewal below the need decides only the drawers' take, bounded where it stops there, so that over a long course keeping draws more, and at least the renewal a step where it does not; several optimizers jointly above the renewal lose it at mutual best responses (`Optimum.course_beyond`, `Web.course_lost_iff`, `Optimum.drawn_bounded_iff`, `Web.course_drawn_unbounded`, `Web.mutual_course_lost`). Landed (SG5, [study §292](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#292-courses-ranked-over-a-horizon-sg5)): an optimizer ranking whole courses over a horizon, against a renewal given by a law of the level, growing with it as in logistic growth: what a course draws, leaves and forgoes adds to the start with the greatest renewal a step, so the courses drawing the most hold a level of greatest renewal and leave nothing at the horizon, every optimum of every order rising with the draw is one of them where such a level is within reach and the capacity, and a dependence above every such level is broken at the first step (`Optimum.most_iff`, `Optimum.frontier_holds`, `Web.frontier_broken`, `Web.frontier_kept`). Landed (SG6, [study §293](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#293-what-keeping-a-dependence-forgoes-over-a-horizon-sg6)): what keeping a dependence forgoes over a horizon: where what is given at or above the need is at most `q`, keeping forgoes, against the course holding the greatest renewal `r` and leaving nothing, `(r − q) n` and the need; a coupling of the dependence below that leaves only optima breaking it within the horizon, one beyond it only optima keeping it, both with optima, and where the need lies above every level of greatest renewal the amount grows with the horizon (`Optimum.frontier_keeps`, `Optimum.frontier_breaks_below`, `Optimum.frontier_keeps_above`, `Web.frontier_breaks_long`). Landed (SG7, [study §294](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#294-a-coupling-added-for-each-step-a-dependence-is-kept-sg7)): a coupling added for each step the dependence is kept keeps it at every horizon where it covers the renewal forgone a step and exceeds the need; below the need the horizon's end breaks it, at the last step alone where the coupling exceeds the renewal forgone a step; below the renewal forgone a long horizon outweighs it (`Optimum.frontier_keeps_stepwise`, `Optimum.frontier_met_before_end`, `Web.frontier_breaks_last`, `Web.frontier_breaks_stepwise_long`). Landed (SG8, [study §295](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#295-an-order-weighting-later-draws-less-sg8)): under an order weighting later draws less, every optimum gains at each step what a course of greatest weighted gain does; where the weighting falls faster than every positive level renews, every optimum ends its own base at the first step, and where some level within reach renews faster, none does (`Optimum.frontier_gains_weighted`, `Optimum.frontier_exhausts`, `Web.frontier_exhausts`, `Optimum.frontier_leaves`). Landed (SG9, [study §296](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#296-rebuilding-from-below-sg9)): below a level of greatest gain out of one step's reach, where the next level and the weighted gain rise with the level, every optimum rebuilds the base as fast as it renews, drawing nothing, and restores every dependence the rising level meets (`Optimum.rebuilding_frontier`, `Optimum.frontier_rebuilds`, `Web.frontier_rebuilds`). Landed (SG10, [study §298](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#298-over-every-law-of-what-stands-sg10)): these laws hold over any law of what stands, the renewal law the case; where others first draw from what an optimizer leaves at least what the remainder renews, every optimum of an order whose weighting falls at every step ends the base at once, and where they draw more, every optimum under positive weights rising at no step, the same order that rebuilds the base alone ending it among others (`Optimum.Next.frontier_ends_drawn_first`, `Optimum.Next.frontier_ends_drawn_more`, `Web.Next.frontier_ends_drawn_first`). *Open:* an order valuing what is left at the horizon, the optimum where a level of greatest gain is out of reach and the next level does not rise with the level, holding a level where what stands at it falls below it, steps met again after a fall, whether others' draws, supplied as a rule reading the level, are themselves a best response, so several optimizers each ranking courses at a mutual best response, and how optimizers come to one; offspring numbers tied to what a formation gives, so that the share laws' premise follows from the flow (with item 9); how much each formation takes when several draw outside a mutual best response, or at a condition within some drawer's family; replenishment from outside the whole and the rise it sustains. *Records:* [raising-what-couples-in-draws-from-beyond](../results/records/raising-what-couples-in-draws-from-beyond.md), [what-a-rise-draws-from-beyond](../results/records/what-a-rise-draws-from-beyond.md), [what-an-optimum-does-beyond-what-couples-in](../results/records/what-an-optimum-does-beyond-what-couples-in.md), [room-read-off-the-exchange](../results/records/room-read-off-the-exchange.md), [several-optimizers-drain-by-their-joint-capacity](../results/records/several-optimizers-drain-by-their-joint-capacity.md), [re-optimizing-above-a-renewal-ends-it](../results/records/re-optimizing-above-a-renewal-ends-it.md), [horizon-optima-hold-the-greatest-renewal](../results/records/horizon-optima-hold-the-greatest-renewal.md), [keeping-forgoes-the-renewal-at-the-need](../results/records/keeping-forgoes-the-renewal-at-the-need.md), [a-step-coupling-keeps-at-every-horizon](../results/records/a-step-coupling-keeps-at-every-horizon.md), [a-weighting-faster-than-renewal-ends-the-base](../results/records/a-weighting-faster-than-renewal-ends-the-base.md), [optima-rebuild-a-depleted-base](../results/records/optima-rebuild-a-depleted-base.md), [others-drawing-first-end-the-base](../results/records/others-drawing-first-end-the-base.md).
11. **The practical premises under the condition** (further proof). Landed (R52, [study §274](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#274-which-dependence-breaks-when-several-take-at-once-and-restraint-over-any-number-of-drawers-r52)): restraint over any number of drawers under the whole-run reading: taking no more and giving no less eases the course, so the restrained course extends an overdrawing one exactly when it includes an event the other withholds (`Ranking.restraint_extends_iff`). Landed (ST1, [study §315](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#315-the-stepwise-law-of-easing-and-restraint-over-any-number-of-drawers-under-the-stepwise-reading-st1)): under easing the stepwise reading withdraws exactly the events the easing course undermines, and a course whose levels never fall undermines nothing, so restraint over any number of drawers extends overdraw under the stepwise reading too where the restrained flow takes from no condition more than it gives (`Ranking.stepwise_extends_iff_of_eases`, `Ranking.restraint_extends_iff_stepwise`); in `Ranking.ThirdParty` the restrained course undermines an event the other includes. Landed (RL1, [study §317](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#317-the-relation-level-readings-law-of-easing-and-restraint-under-it-rl1)): the relation-level reading withdraws under easing exactly the relations the easing course no longer takes part in or undermines, and ranks restraint that keeps taking part above overdraw for any number of drawers (`Ranking.relational_extends_iff_of_eases`, `Ranking.restraint_extends_iff_relational`). *Open:* the form of L under the condition; whether J1 yields “better without malignant growth” step by step. *Records:* [ranking-under-readings](../results/records/ranking-under-readings.md), [continuation-condition-yields-living-significance](../results/records/continuation-condition-yields-living-significance.md).
12. **Warrant** (philosophical warrant, §8.5). Is widening better (C, which orders), and does significance stop at no boundary (the step of invariance, which fixes scope)? With them, the grounds of A and the neutralities, and the philosophical part of the first target. *Settling it:* no formal work settles it; the theory locates each step (§6.5) and lists the objections it has not answered (§8.9). Since IV1 the step is reduced to whether the self's presupposition in acting is essentially de se and whether a relation privileges the self's drawing, and C to monotonicity at each standpoint, with the impartial order exactly what every order counting everything shares, additive or not (`Weighting.widens_iff_every_of_class`, AP1, [study §282](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#282-alignment-approaches-under-the-selection-laws-ap1); [THE-STEP-OF-INVARIANCE §7](THE-STEP-OF-INVARIANCE.md#7-what-is-open-exactly)). *Records:* [universal-practical-warrant](../results/records/universal-practical-warrant.md), [continuation-condition-decomposition](../results/records/continuation-condition-decomposition.md), [what-the-step-across-standpoints-reaches](../results/records/what-the-step-across-standpoints-reaches.md), [widening-as-what-every-weighting-ranks](../results/records/widening-as-what-every-weighting-ranks.md), [theory-of-becoming-foundation](../results/records/theory-of-becoming-foundation.md).

**The targets.** The goal records' five targets map onto these items: the deductive necessity of the whole is items 1 and 12; a stronger universal practical warrant is item 12, since the represented dynamics leave significance undetermined (§5); the core as the root of wisdom has its formal part complete within the laws without premise ([wisdom-root-without-axiom](../results/records/wisdom-root-without-axiom.md)), what remains being item 7 and how wisdom forms (§8.7); the necessary relational foundation of scientific inquiry has its formal side stated (§3.2) and its comparison on actual inquiries is empirical; and the stronger direction hypothesis, that reality generally develops more shared and more responsive renewal, is empirical and not a premise. The records [theory-of-becoming-foundation](../results/records/theory-of-becoming-foundation.md), [universal-practical-warrant](../results/records/universal-practical-warrant.md), [wisdom-root](../results/records/wisdom-root.md) and [scientific-inquiry-foundation](../results/records/scientific-inquiry-foundation.md) keep each target's statement and progress.

**Completeness, in the qualified sense.** Every item has a checked statement or a disclosed premise, and the empty axiom report is checked; the four criteria of [plan section 9](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/BECOMING-UNIFICATION-PLAN.md#9-走向完整的生成之论--toward-a-complete-theory-of-becoming) have not been audited since 10 October, and the criterion a script can check (K1–K7, [study §218](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#218-r25-the-twentieth-reviews-step-1-docs-now)) is adopted (20 October 2026, with Ren's approval) and runs since R39: [`completion.py`](../../../tools/research/completion.py) reads the [coverage file](../results/completion/COVERAGE.json) (one row per phase, differentiation and domain, with its central core declarations) with the core receipt, the connectivity measure, the records and this section, and writes the [report](../results/completion/COMPLETION.md), which `research-check` keeps current. On R43's tree every criterion is met. Unity (K2) is met since R43: the World family reaches the Process family by definition (a world's history is a run), and since definitions are acyclic the other directions cannot also hold by definition, so they hold by construction: every run of every process is a world that fixes it (`Frame.world_fixes_run`), and every web and flow is a world's (`World.of_web`, `World.of_flow`); each central law stated over any amounts, valuations or schedules has a named base-family instance whose proof uses it (for the cut's bound, `carriedWorld_within_cut`); premises (K3) is met since R42: C is met by widening on level paths (`Web.paths_comparison_widens`) and failed by any comparison read off marginals (`Regimes.no_comparison_through_marginals`), and decidedness, which no world can be shown to fail since no proposition is refuted to be decided (`not_not_decided`), is met over finite stretches (`Decided.exists_below`) and calibrated: asked of every run, it is exactly LPO (`HistoryIdeals.cut_decided_iff_lpo`); representation (K4) is met since R41: `Flow.More.replenishment_unbounded` is the whole-number instance of `Amounts.replenishment_unbounded` (in Archimedean amounts, uptake beyond every multiple of a least take needs replenishment beyond every multiple of it), and with halving takes, takers without bound and no least take, it fails (`Halving.takes_halve`); `rising_recurs` has the counterexample of halving amounts and `Flow.excess_bounded` its least-loss form (R40). The ethics chain is one derivation since R40: `Direction.grounded_widening_selected` derives the direction of §7.5 from the evaluation's grounds, through `Valuation.significant_iff` and `Web.unrestricted_iff_grounds_beyond` (on a connected web, with living decided, the condition is exactly those grounds with significance for what does not live), the one object of the paths and the pool law, so its proof reaches every step. So completeness holds in the qualified sense the criterion checks: every central law is stated on the base or instantiated there, every premise disclosed with what meets and fails it, every natural-number law given its dense form, the ethics chain one derivation. The criterion does not show that the premises are warranted, and that the theory states the strongest claim with no axiom has not been shown.

### 8.2 Further proofs, by area

The further proofs of each area that do not change the shape of the theory (rates, real or rational versions, more instances, tighter bounds) are in the `next_delta` of the area's records, and the area table of 20 October 2026 with its records is in [the history of this section](../history/THE-THEORY-OF-BECOMING-open-programme-2026-10-20.md).

<a id="83-readings-decided-on-rens-delegation"></a>
### 8.3 Readings adopted
