Each reading is an interpretation of checked content, adopted with its argument, numbered D1–D57 in the [decision register](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/DELEGATED-CHOICES.md), which says what in the work would change it. The readings stand as one account in five links (one unfolding, generation, continuation and its extent, knowing, alignment), each decided by its place in it; where two did not fit together the account decided between them (D45). They change no theorem and add no premise; who said a word is provenance and does not settle a reading. The readings are stated where they apply: §1 (D23, D25, D30, D32, D33, D43, D47–D55), §2 (D3, D4, D6, D10, D17, D20, D25, D39, D43, D45), §3 (D25, D26, D33, D34, D40–D42, D52), §4 (D39, D44, D48, D56, D57), §6 (D1, D2, D9, D11–D21, D43), §7 (D20, D25, D26, D45). D36 adopts the AI's working names where the statement has their exact meaning, with 生起／止息 for arising and ceasing. The three senses of 生成 are kept apart in the Chinese edition: 展开 for the one unfolding, 形成 for a formation and its forming, 生成 for generation; becoming is 生成／变化. The review's question whether 生生 may be glossed by `living` is answered by D1's revision: it may not. Of the finite name, the reading adopted says only what the theorem shows: within a bound, kinds have finite names, and 名可名 is an analogy only. The likeness of `Novelty`'s quantification to `BoundaryInvariant` is an analogy only.

<a id="84-what-stays-rens"></a>
<a id="84-open-readings"></a>
### 8.4 No reading open; texts that change by proposal

No reading is open: a reading added later is decided the same way, by argument and by how it fits the rest.

**Texts that change by proposal.** OPENING's body is Ren's text and the generativity chain is his curated argument; they change only through proposals. His terms keep his wording where quoted, with their source, and are read like any reading. The Chinese term for coming to rest is 停息, with 停息定理 for the rest theorem: the theorems say that events stop recurring, which is 停, not what holds at once (静), and 息 is what 生生不息 negates. “Closure bounds change” stays Ren's phrase, and the law keeps its name, enclosure bounds change. The chain's learning and wisdom links took `Selection`, `Observation` and `Parting` on 18 October 2026 through its maintenance record ([chain](../results/GENERATIVITY-CHAIN.md), [maintenance record](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/GENERATIVITY-CHAIN-INDEX-RECORD.md#learning-and-wisdom-links-18-october-2026)). Whether a reading is Ren's own is a question of provenance; whether the step of invariance holds is a question of warrant (D21).

**Other questions decided in the one account** (D45). The eighteenth review counts as the review its records asked for, its kind stated wherever cited (AI, read-only, no Lean built). `IdealWarrant` is joined with `CompletionRole` and `HistoryIdeals` as `Becoming.Ideals` and `Becoming.HistoryIdeals`, the originals kept as instances ([IdealsCorrespondence](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/completion/formal/IdealsCorrespondence.lean)). No integrator is named: landers take the queue in order of readiness. The representation's commitments (§0, block A) stay, the theorems at finer grains showing what refining them changes ([brief, Part C](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/READINGS-PENDING-REN.md#c-the-register-itself-d-f-review-item-50)).

### 8.5 Philosophical warrant

- **The deductive necessity of the whole**, with no added substantive premise: that its determinations are internally related moments of one unfolding, not a collection of instances ([foundation §9](BECOMING-FOUNDATION.md#9-the-content-of-the-whole-deduction)).
- **A stronger universal practical warrant**: grounding reasons without an externally supplied end.
- **The necessary relational foundation of scientific inquiry**: the core's indispensability from independently stated achievements.
- **The grounds of living significance, A and the neutralities**, none independently reviewed. On held lives what living significance adds beyond self-significance lies in standpoint neutrality, on a connected whole where every standpoint holds a living claim in relational neutrality instead; under the condition living significance is an instantiation, so its ground is the condition's; route (b) claims no warrant.
- **What the support for living significance contributes.** Four kinds of support count comparatively, none as proof: explanatory unification (care, repair, creation, learning, renewal, rest, finite fulfilment and constructive ending from one ground), discriminating content (renewing and consuming formation separated by descriptive content where a one-step count ranks them the other way), discrimination against rivals, and fruitfulness for alignment. The formal checks contribute inference, premise footprint, dynamical distinctions and independence; none verifies the starting commitment, and the dynamical theorems are sign-neutral ([history](../history/G0-WARRANT.md#2-支持的四种方式--four-kinds-of-support)).
- **Calling the generative space good**: is widening better (the warrant of C, which orders; C's no-loss extension is widening by definition, so the definition carries no warrant), and does significance stop at no boundary (the warrant of the step of invariance, which fixes scope and orders nothing)? The step is Gewirth's across standpoints and Parfit's and Siderits's across drawings of a self; the universalist and the critic part only at the scope of "however the self is drawn", so the step holds exactly if the self's presupposition is not essentially de se and no relation privileges the self's drawing. C is what every standpoint that counts everything shares if each standpoint's order is monotone in what it counts, which is C's premise relocated, not warranted ([THE-STEP-OF-INVARIANCE](THE-STEP-OF-INVARIANCE.md); [literature check](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-SIGNIFICANCE-REVIEW.md#invariance-check)).
- **The scope of representation.** Can every becoming be represented with discrete steps and natural-number amounts? The shared index is read off own courses for local exchanges (§1.4), so what remains is whether every becoming has such a local law with finite courses; where it cannot be so represented, the spine is extended (items 1 and 6).

### 8.6 The logical commitment

The core assumes no principle of classical logic (§0, block A). Where a law needs one, the principle is its own hypothesis, or the law is proved equivalent to it; each calibration below is a result only where it says something about the unfolding, and otherwise a lemma of the law that needs it.

- **Decidedness is local** (`Becoming.Decidedness`): a count exists exactly where what it counts is decided (`counts_iff_decided`). The counting laws (`World.enclosure_bounds_change`, `World.counting_under`) take decided activity for every relatum; over counted censuses (`Becoming.CensusCounts`, `Becoming.CensusBinding`) the laws under the premises take none. The [decidability inventory](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/DECIDABILITY-INVENTORY.md) lists every theorem still conditioned on it.
- **Excluded middle**, asked of every case, is exactly each of: the finite-world law that arising recurs exactly where ceasing does, without decidability (`World.law_iff_em`, `World.law_decides`; with EM as hypothesis, `World.law_of_em`, `Carries.chain_ends_of_em`), a lemma of Prop-valued activity, the witness `World.undecided` putting an arbitrary proposition at one step; a fixed number of relata up to indiscernibility (`World.numbers_indiscernible_iff_em`); deciding likeness of finite frames (`alike_decided_iff_em`); branching tests fixing likeness without decided steps (`alike_of_sameBranchingTests_iff_em`); matching parts by joint tests without stability (`matchesParts_of_compact_iff_em`); every recipe or program as one test (`Recipe.holds_after_iff_em`, `diamond_correct_iff_em`); the robust comparison under every classification (`classification_robust_iff_iff_em`); forming and holding making inheritance (`split_iff_em`); every undominated member having a property wherever each lacking it is bettered (`undominated_has_iff_em`); the disjunctive remainder's density (`orNotAdheres_dense_iff_em`).
- **Weak excluded middle** is an undominated member of every nonempty listed menu under every irreflexive transitive relation (`exists_undominated_iff_weakly`), on frames the choice at every step (`FrontierFrames.choice_exists_iff_weakly`): where whether one move betters another is open, a step need leave no move known to be chosen. It is the one classical step of the practical commitments as first proved (`finite_frontier`).
- **Double-negation elimination**, asked of every process, is the origin law with a proposition as the gate (`Gated.origin_of_propositions_iff`), a refutable reading holding where adhered to (`refutable_holds_iff`), and passing either way between refutable and closed classes (`Stretches.refutable_closed_iff`, `Stretches.closed_refutable_iff`; for every order, `Ideals.refutable_closed_iff`, `Ideals.closed_refutable_iff`); and the step from verifiable to refutable complement (`Ideals.verifiable_compl_refutable_iff`).
- **LPO** is given by a never-supported formation with a carried reserve having a last active step (`Web.lpo_of_ending`), a list of every member that ever holds (`Listed.lpo_of_few`), naming the step from which a finite world rests (`World.lpo_of_finite_ends`), finding the juncture of every loss (`lpo_of_junctures_found`), every run of every finite-world process settling (`LastChange.lpo_iff_settles`, `Settling.OneWay.lpo_of_settles`), locating where a run stopped (`Limit.Sequences.eventuallyFalse_lpo`), and reaching within one step in the search witness (`Lasting.Search.reaches_iff_lpo`); it is exactly telling whether a history ends or goes on (`Stretches.cut_decided_iff_lpo`, `HistoryIdeals.cut_decided_iff_lpo`): telling whether a history ends is deciding whether it changes again. So the core states the weaker forms, “not active at arbitrarily late steps” and rest that cannot be refuted (`World.rests_unrefuted`, `World.comes_to_rest_unrefuted`, `World.rests_unrefuted_enclosed`, `Flow.change_stops_unrefuted`, `Amounts.rests_unrefuted`). At or above it: deciding vanishing gives LPO (`lpo_of_vanish_decided`) and as a rule is exactly the LPO rule (`vanishDecision_iff_lpoRule`, `LPO.of_rule`); every schedule settling is exactly `RecursOrStops` (`settled_iff_recursOrStops`), which gives LPO (`RecursOrStops.lpo`, `lpo_of_settled`) and is excluded middle at level Σ⁰₂ (Akama, Berardi, Hayashi and Kohlenbach 2004), strictly stronger.
- **WLPO** (`WLPO`, `WLPO.of_lpo`) is given by a law of what has stood differing under histories agreeing on all it is sensitive to, with numbered conditions (`wlpo_of_not_through_sensitive`), and by a law of the whole and no part (`wlpo_of_insensitive_differs`); as a rule, a counterexample exists exactly where a rule decides whether a sequence is false throughout (`not_through_sensitive_iff_rule`, `changedByNoCondition_iff_rule`, `WLPO.of_rule`). So the core states the least coupling only for laws reading finitely many conditions under each history.
- **Markov's principle** (`Markov`): asked of every process, the reading of tests is refutable exactly under it (`refutable_passes_iff_markov`); on runs, from verifiable to refutable complement (`verifiable_compl_refutable_markov`), and no method refutes settling in the limit under it (§3.2).
- **LLPO** (`LLPO`, `LLPO.of_lpo`): `EitherThroughout` and `EitherNever` are each exactly LLPO for decided families (`eitherThroughout_iff_llpoForDecided`, `eitherNever_iff_llpoForDecided`, `LLPOForDecided`); lifting where branching is finite takes `EitherThroughout` and a choice of an unbounded successor (`Lasting.lifting`).
- **Choice.** A run from every member of every family that keeps moving, asked of every process, is exactly dependent choice (`runs_iff_dependentChoice`, `DependentChoice`); within finitely many configurations a run is a course into a return with no choice. Where values have choice into plans a class is kept exactly where some plan keeps it (`keptFor_iff_plan_of_choice`; with listed values, no premise, `keptFor_iff_plan`).

The periphery may use Lean's standard axioms, each result through its own receipt (§9).

### 8.7 Empirical correspondence

- The actual realization of common conditions and of each mechanism, with tests that discriminate between accounts; whether actual unfoldings meet the premises, including reserve and transmission (`Flow.TransmitsWithin`).
- Dissipation as stated is stronger than the statistical second law, and `Passing` is a finite, enclosed, lossless world that never rests. Read in available work, the second law gives nothing from nothing; dissipation needs, in addition, every taking exchange irreversible in whole units, or a floor of destroyed exergy at least the unit (Landauer's kT ln 2 per merged bit, which survives refinement); destroying at least a (q + 1)th of what is taken gives loss in proportion; and dissipation only in expectation still ends taking from a closed family on almost every run (`taking_not_recurring_ae`). Over dense amounts rest needs a least loss, or amounts read off finitely many configurations, and approach to rest only Archimedean amounts (§1.3); a part's merge inside a law that merges nothing is kept by the rest in count (`Law.Lossless.narrowing_spreads`), so Landauer's floor needs from physics only the step from a count to heat. Whether a given system meets these is empirical.
- The margin measured (MQ1–MQ7, [experiments/margin-measured](../../../experiments/margin-measured/README.md); every test frozen before its run or before any held-out value was read): the margin law's frozen-rest accounting is an identity on measured data, so its test lies in the margin of a regime (§2.3). In the laboratory the margin predicts thresholds. Measured by intervention in a simulated Allee yeast, it bounds the time to break whatever the history, with the largest sustained draw. On real yeast (Dai et al. 2012), one growth map fitted on four dilution factors predicted the need of held-out regimes and the fates it separates (93–100% on each side, against 68–81% for a null with no need). At equal density giving falls with the dilution (post hoc), so the need is the regime's. In yeast driven past its fold by a rising dilution or a falling sucrose (Dai, Korolev and Gore 2015; MQ6), the level is the state at a constant draw and carries across experiments within 8%, and the regime margin forecasts the level away from the break better than the frozen rest. But the populations broke three and at least six days before the curves measured in constant regimes said, because those curves place the fold too late. In assessed fish stocks (RAM Legacy, 258 stocks over three tests), the level is not the state. Giving read off the level forecasts worse than the frozen rest. The state is the level, the recent giving and one lag. The regime's step and the frozen rest are the two ends of one law, and the stocks lie near the second. The frozen-rest margin over the net draw ranks the risk of a break but does not time it, because the giving moves toward the draw and the draw follows the stock. Below the regime's point, whether the recent giving meets the catch separates three-year fates in the stocks already read; on the 21 stocks with a level in tonnes that no analysis had read (NOAA Stock SMART and ICES SAG, MQ7) the difference, 0.39 of the predicted sign, came from 5 stocks and missed its frozen bound. Draws on a shared condition add and their harms do not. Blind draw against margin predicts side effects for an exact optimizer only. These bear on theses [T1](#t1-persistence-certifies-the-joint-draw) and [T2](#t2-the-state-of-a-renewable-condition-under-a-draw); studies [§253](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#253-the-margin-as-a-measured-quantity-mq1), [§266](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#266-the-margins-equation-of-state-against-held-out-regimes-mq2), [§272](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#272-the-margin-of-a-regime-in-the-field-mq3), [§277](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#277-a-state-of-level-and-recent-giving-in-the-field-mq4), [§280](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#280-a-lag-and-the-draws-response-in-the-field-mq5), [§314](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#314-the-margins-time-to-break-past-the-fold-in-the-lab-mq6), [§316](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#316-fates-on-either-side-of-the-curve-in-the-plane-in-the-field-mq7).
- One standard formalism defined outside the theory meets the premises by its own definitions: in a place/transition net (a Petri net, or a reaction network with whole-number counts) with finitely many places, balance is the firing rule (`Net.balances_iff`), dissipation is a strict P-subinvariant (`Net.dissipative_iff`), rest is unrefuted (`Net.rests_unrefuted`), an emptied place every producer of which consumes it stays empty (`Net.siphon_empty`), and a conservative cycle never rests (`Net.cycle_never_rests`). Which systems are such nets, with which weights, remains.
- How wisdom forms in actual humans and AI; which lumping an actual network's architecture, starting points and updates impose, and how it corresponds to the world; evidence for an identified installation of each conditional mechanism of §7.
- The stronger direction hypothesis, that reality generally develops more shared and more responsive renewal: open and not a premise.
- Claims about actual systems at full strength, each with the observation that would refute it and the frozen tests so far: [theses and conjectures](#theses-and-conjectures).

Records: [least-loss-from-entropy-production](../results/records/least-loss-from-entropy-production.md), [place-transition-nets-on-the-spine](../results/records/place-transition-nets-on-the-spine.md), [generalization-through-lumping](../results/records/generalization-through-lumping.md), [situated-recovery-empirical-correspondence](../results/records/situated-recovery-empirical-correspondence.md).

<a id="89-objections-not-yet-answered"></a>
### 8.9 Objections not yet answered

Each stands against the account as stated, with what would answer it. Where one is answered, answered in part or sharpened into an exact question, the entry says which and links the argument; none is shown to defeat the step, and the step is not shown.

- **The step across standpoints (Gewirth's critics).** From "I must value my continuation" nothing follows about another's; universalizing yields only that each standpoint holds its own to matter. It hits the step across standpoints, which from each standpoint's own presupposition forces exactly the centers (`forced_iff_center`), and not the step across drawings (`boundaryInvariant_iff_all`); the half aimed at rights has no target, since the theory makes no claim of right or duty. *Sharpened* ([THE-STEP-OF-INVARIANCE §6.1](THE-STEP-OF-INVARIANCE.md#61-gewirths-critics-from-my-agency-to-others), [§4.1](THE-STEP-OF-INVARIANCE.md#4-the-strongest-case-against-the-step)). The egoist reply is exactly the de se reading of the presupposition. Held de se, as a property each standpoint ascribes to itself, the presupposition gives each center its own closure, invariant only where the centers' closures agree (`ownClosure_standpointInvariant_iff`). Held de dicto, the step forces the union of closures (`forced_iff_within_some`). The two readings agree exactly where the closures agree (`selfAscribed_iff_withinSome`), and along being related at all in a connected unfolding the de se reading is the condition with no step (`ownClosure_unrestricted_of_whole`). So the de se objection and the privileged-relation objection are one, and the exact open question is this: is the self's presupposition held de se of a self drawn along a privileged relation narrower than being related at all? *Would answer it:* an argument either way on that question.
- **Agent-relative reasons and partiality.** Williams argued that agent-relative reasons and partial attachments are not defects awaiting universalization (“one thought too many”). *Answered for two-valued significance* ([§6.2 there](THE-STEP-OF-INVARIANCE.md#62-agent-relative-reasons-and-partiality-williams)): the step fixes scope only; partial choices (one's wife or a stranger) are mixed comparisons, which C does not rank and every weighting counting everything may decide either way (`Weighting.mixed_either_way`), so there is no impartial verdict for partiality to be licensed against; partiality cannot rank a widened course above its widening (`Weighting.weighed_lt_of_widens`) or count a loss as nothing (`Weighting.weighed_eq_of_unweighted`). The price is that C also leaves the grossest partiality unranked where numbers differ (Taurek's position). *Answered for graded significance too* (IV2): the step gives standing (whether a formation counts), not status (how much). Invariant grades give invariant standing and rank alike from every standpoint (`Weighting.ranks_alike_of_gradeInvariant`), but invariant standing does not give invariant grades (`Partial.ranks_differ`). Numbers count (`Weighting.neutral_ranks_by_number`) only under the further premise that status is invariant and equal, which the theory does not assert. *What remains:* an argument for or against that premise.
- **Why “anything” has significance**, a hurricane or a tumour included. *Sharpened* ([§6.3 there](THE-STEP-OF-INVARIANCE.md#63-why-anything-has-significance-a-hurricane-a-tumour)). The narrower scope, what wants its continuation read as the centers of standpoints [AI reading, D59], is checked: retaining under it is admitting every center (`Web.retains_atCenters_iff`), and it parts from the condition exactly at a formation that is no center (`Clearing.centers_retain_yet_loses`). The tumour does not tell the scopes apart (on the conatus reading of wanting it is a center, and ending it is mixed on both); the hurricane does only on a reading of wanting stronger than conatus. “Anything” is not idle: it decides which losses C counts. *Would answer it:* an argument for which formations hold standpoints, and for covering, every formation within some standpoint's closure along a relation the unfolding reads; covering by feeding is the defensible part.
- **The externalism of A.** Internalists deny a reason where no motive or sound deliberation connects to it. *Sharpened* ([§6.4 there](THE-STEP-OF-INVARIANCE.md#64-the-externalism-of-a)): restated internally, from a standpoint's presupposition along the relations its concern carries, a reason reaches exactly the closure (`forced_iff_within`), and everything route (b) proves survives (§6.3, `Flow.Balances.presupposed_reach_comparison`); A's external part is exactly the step. *Would answer it:* a sound deliberative route from the presupposition to invariance, or an argument that there is none.
- **Graded status, priority and constraints.** *Answered* for graded status, priority, sufficiency and constraints against closing ([§6.5 there](THE-STEP-OF-INVARIANCE.md#65-graded-status-priority-and-constraints)).
  - Graded status is a positive weighting. It agrees with C wherever C ranks and decides only mixed comparisons (`Weighting.weighed_lt_of_widens`, `Weighting.mixed_either_way`). Status zero is a boundary (`Weighting.weighed_eq_of_unweighted`).
  - Priority on levels of continuation (steps admitted, `Weighting.level`), IV2: a strictly increasing transform agrees with C wherever C ranks (`Weighting.prior_lt_of_widens`, Pareto). A transform strictly concave up to a bound decides one-step transfers that C leaves mixed (`Weighting.prior_transfer`, Pigou–Dalton).
  - Sufficiency is a scope denial above its threshold (`Weighting.sufficient_ties`).
  - Lexical priority is no additive weighting.
  - A constraint against closing never conflicts with C (`Web.not_widens_of_closes`).

  *Sharpened* for constraints on acts rather than outcomes (doing and allowing beyond closing, promising). *Would answer it:* whether such a constraint reads as a de se ranking of outcomes (Howard 2022), and so decides only mixed comparisons. Well-being beyond the length of continuation is not modelled.
- **Which function is learned.** The laws of §7.1–§7.4 hold for every selection through the couplings and say nothing about which function training installs, inductive bias, goal misgeneralization or deception. *Would answer it:* results on which law a trained system's acts come to follow, joined to the learning laws, and evidence for identified installations.
- **The internal-relations thesis is open** (item 1). *Would answer it:* the passage the first target describes.
- **Every review so far is by AI.** *Would answer it:* independent human review of the Lean statements against the prose and of the philosophical arguments, beginning with §6 and §7.

<a id="9-形式脊柱--the-formal-spine"></a>
## 9. The formal spine

All new formal results are stated on one spine; where it cannot express a result it is extended rather than another model built, and existing modules connect by instance or equivalence theorems. Since the F3 sweep (19 October 2026) the core states the content of every periphery module that was in the checker's run, with the excluded middle, choice, extensionality, stability or sections their proofs used taken as hypotheses (72 theorems). One package and one checker are not yet one web of dependence: the [theorem-connectivity measure](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/THEOREM-CONNECTIVITY.md#11-r32-the-measure-stops-rewarding-restatement), rerun on each landing, finds on R37c's tree 503 of the 11,552 spine theorems apart from both base families; each of the 356 roots among them stands apart for a stated reason (one constructed model 210, a support lemma 81, a general structure 44, a restatement 9, logical strength alone 10, a calibration 2), and 180 statements reach the base only through a law of their own module. Since R32 the measure counts no named one-line restatement as a connection; the earlier reruns are logged in [its §10](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/THEOREM-CONNECTIVITY.md#10-reruns-on-each-landing-17-19-october-2026).

**Rings.** The checker's lists fix them, and the [package README](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/README.md) describes them.
- *Core*: the foundation modules `GenerativeCore`, `InquirySufficiency` and `RelationalRenewal`, with the base and its layers: 285 modules counting the root `Becoming`.
- *Instances and applications* (R35b, 20 October 2026): the instances read general laws of the core on one named carrier, the Changes (`Becoming.Hexagrams`, `HexagramPlaces`, `HexagramCommuting`, `HexagramLaws`, `HexagramSites`, `HexagramSequences`) and `Becoming.PlaceTransitionNets`, 7 modules; the applications are leaves no core module imports (`Becoming.Dispatch`, `GuardHorizon`, `ResponseLearning`, `Coverage`, `SeedReach`, `ScopedReach`), 6 modules. They are held to the core's conditions (the closure of the core and the ring is 282 modules with the foundation, compiled from the prelude, every axiom report empty) and lie outside the core by direction: no core module but the root imports one of them, and no instance imports an application (`INSTANCES`, `APPLICATIONS` and `ring_direction` in the checker). The split of `Becoming.Commuting`'s instance section into `Becoming.HexagramCommuting` made the Changes' chain importer-free; no file moved.
- *No bridge* since 19 October 2026: the bridge fold (F1) moved into the core what reached no periphery constant, the port (F3) stated in the core the content of the 75 periphery modules in the run, graded theorem by theorem ([port program](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/development/bridge-fold/PORT-PROGRAM.md)), and the sweep registered those modules as superseded (bytes kept, out of the run) and moved every bridge and join file to [`lean/history/becoming/`](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/history/becoming/), where the receipts that checked them still name them ([fold study](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/BECOMING-BRIDGE-FOLD.md)).
- *Periphery*: everything else, including the Mathlib leaves and their bridges [CorrectionInquiry](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/universal-ethics/generative-capacity/CorrectionInquiry.lean) and [LearningCorrespondence](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/universal-ethics/evolution-connectionism/LearningCorrespondence.lean), the periphery's instance modules and the Python experiments; it may use any tool, and its dependence runs towards the core. [`Becoming.FallibleInquiry`](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/FallibleInquiry.lean) sits in the package directory but is periphery, with its own receipt.

**Three layers on one base.** `import Becoming` brings in the whole core. The import tree starts at the foundation and `Becoming.Prelude`, which imports only `Init` and states once each lemma on numbers, truth values, lists and options; `Becoming.World` (the records of the unfolding with no premise among them) and `Becoming.Laws` (the notions every law uses, stated once) import the frame; modules stating laws that need no record of the unfolding import nothing or the prelude only. The layers are computed from each module's source and imports by [`layers.py`](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/development/r31-support/layers.py): L0, the base (`Becoming.World`, `Becoming.Frame`, `Becoming.Prelude`), 3 modules; L2, the 18 modules whose source names the condition or a valuation (`Unrestricted`, `Valuation`), from `Becoming.Metaethics` and `Becoming.Significance` on; L1, the other 263 of the core, and the 13 modules of the instance and applications ring, all L1. No module imports one of a higher layer, which the script checks; sixteen core modules reach `Significance` or `Metaethics` through imports (138 before R31, [study §222](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#222-layers-and-one-prelude-r31); [§223](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#223-forwarders-copies-and-reading-modules-r32); [§225](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#225-one-evaluative-chain-r34)). The module table, every core module with its computed layer and what it states, is in the [package README](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/README.md#three-layers-on-one-base), which `layers.py` writes; the [reference catalogue's table](../history/THE-THEORY-OF-BECOMING-REFERENCE.md#module-table) is a frozen record of the four layers before R31.

**The checker.** The core's requirement (§0, block A) is enforced on every run by the [consolidated checker](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/check_becoming.py): the import closure is exactly the foundation and the core; every source declaration has an empty axiom report; the core compiles from the prelude alone, the foundation's `import Std` stripped; generated constants are audited too, the only ones on an axiom being the injectivity lemmas of constructors with fields (`injEq`, on `propext`), used by no source declaration; every inherited module's bytes match a passed receipt by sha256; and no module of the superseded register (`SUPERSEDED`, 75 modules since F3) is in the run, each keeping its bytes. Living significance, C, D and the positive ground of assurance are stated once in the core; the periphery modules that stated them first are superseded, their port files grading each theorem against its core statement.

**Why the core excludes `propext`, and why the periphery may use it** ([`Becoming.PropositionIdentity`](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/PropositionIdentity.lean), no axiom; [record](../results/records/proposition-identity.md)). From the relational start a proposition is read through its relations of derivation, what derives it and what it derives, and `propext` (`(a ↔ b) → a = b`) says that a proposition is nothing beyond them. The module states this exactly. `propext`, as a proposition, holds exactly where every reading of propositions is lumped by interderivability (`Learning.Lumped`, read through the two ends of an interderivation; `extensional_iff_lumped`), and exactly where the reading that reads a proposition by name, the identity, is (`byName_lumped_iff`); a reading built from relations (atoms, the connectives, quantifiers over any type) respects interderivability already, with `↔` rather than `=` (`Formula.eval_congr`). The identification forgets no witness: any two witnesses of `a ↔ b` are equal (`iff_witness_unique`), while `Bool` has two different self-inverse maps (`two_equivalences`), so identifying types along an equivalence would lose which one. So `propext` is an exact lumping of outcomes. What it does not keep is the course: transport along any equation of propositions with a constant motive keeps the value (`transport_const`), but a value transported along `propext` does not reduce. `#reduce` stops at the transport, and neither `rfl` nor `decide` proves the value it provably has; this is a kernel observation with Lean 4.29.1, kept with the record, not a theorem. The core's runs, censuses and witnesses are checked by computation (`rfl` and `decide` on them), so the core states `↔` wherever a result would need `=` between propositions and keeps its empty report; the periphery, whose results concern outcomes and carry their own receipts, may use `propext` as the exact lumping it is. That every reading of a proposition is relational is `propext` itself: here it is a hypothesis (`Extensional`), never a conclusion.

**The current receipt** is run [`20261021T141520.308357Z`](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/becoming-checks/20261021T141520.308357Z/verification.json) (the [current pointer](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/becoming-verification.json)), checked with Lean 4.29.1 on main 8e10d7ff1 with RL1 in the working tree ([study §317](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#317-the-relation-level-readings-law-of-easing-and-restraint-under-it-rl1)). It passed, the source unchanged during the check and the core with the ring compiled from the prelude.

| Spine (the core and the ring; no bridge since F3) | 12,915 theorems |
| Audited spine declarations | 17,711 |
| Inherited modules bound to passed receipts by hash | 3 (the foundation) |
| Superseded periphery modules (out of the run, bytes kept) | 75 |
| Modules in the compile order | 301 |
| Generated `injEq` lemmas on `propext` | 201 in the closure |

Unnamed instances in 11 modules (Change, Population, Quantum, Resolution, Counting, Identity, Entropy, SelectionReach, Optimum, Gluing, Dispatch) are covered by the audit of the whole core environment, not by name. Three declaration names carry market words, `MergeClass.spent_consumed` (spent), `Flow.Balances.rise_paid_from_beyond` (paid) and `Coverage.charge` (charge). The run reused 1,171 cached Lean steps from `~/.cache/becoming-lean` and ran 33; the last fresh run, [`20261016T130738.412710Z`](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/becoming-checks/20261016T130738.412710Z/verification.json) on d21b311a, ran all 703 steps of that core and passed ([I5 report](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/development/i5-checker-reproducible/REPORT.md)).

**The periphery's own checks.** CorrectionInquiry connects the Mathlib leaves to the core, checked with Lean 4.30.0-rc2 and Mathlib (receipt `20261020T161757.201244Z`; the core compiles there too, and the bridge's 7 theorems have empty axiom reports). [MachineInstances](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/universal-ethics/generative-capacity/MachineInstances.lean) connects older peripheral machines as instances of the general laws (103 theorems, standard axioms only). Eighteen further instance modules connect in the same way, each with its own checker and standard axioms only: copy machines, source histories, markets, the correction and shutdown protocols, versioned tasks, the shared stock, continuation plans, transmission of learned procedures, material preparation and fragmentation, judging a risk certificate, renewing and consuming formation, binding-preserving substitution, organizational formation, the origin of an organization's pools, the prefix order of infinite traces, finite-branching lifting, selection through a model of the options, and the completion package's ideals, completion and histories. `LearningCorrespondence` (15 theorems) is checked likewise, and `FallibleInquiry` has its own receipt, `fallible-inquiry-verification.json` (run `20261020T161546.707477Z`: 31 theorems, 37 declarations, 17 on `propext` or `Quot.sound`). [RegimeReals](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/regime/RegimeReals.lean) reads the regime over the non-negative reals, with its own checker (receipt `20261021T102713.696033Z`: 101 theorems, 135 declarations, standard axioms only; RM5). All 23 periphery receipts were renewed on 20 October 2026 against the core of main 6b07c9edf ([study §275](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#275-every-periphery-receipt-renewed-on-the-current-core-pr1)). Seven modules no longer compiled after the core's renames and moves of 19 and 20 October and now name the core's current declarations: MachineInstances, ProtocolInstances, VersionedTaskInstances, IdealsCorrespondence and EntropyProduction renamed definitions, LearningCorrespondence and TransmissionCorrespondence the modules that now hold what they use. Every theorem and declaration kept its name and axiom report, and the twelve theorems whose printed statements changed name the same definitions by their new names. Read again at the new receipts, every periphery use of `propext` is classed as before: none computes a value through an identification of propositions.

<!-- zh-table:begin -->
**Module statements from the Chinese edition's table.** The 20 October Chinese edition kept a table of the core's modules in this section; seven of its rows state laws the English named nowhere, carried here in translation (the frozen table is in [the Chinese edition of 20 October](../history/THE-THEORY-OF-BECOMING-2026-10-20.zh.md#9-形式脊柱--the-formal-spine)).
- [Becoming.Prelude](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Prelude.lean) (L0, no premise, imports only `Init`): the lemmas on numbers, truth values, lists and options the layers use, each stated once: cancellation, subtraction and products of natural numbers (`add_right_cancel`, `Naturals.sub_sub`, `mul_assoc_nat`), what `all`, `any`, `contains`, `filter`, `find?`, `bind` and `map` test (`ListTests.all_true_iff`, `ListTests.find_none_iff`), membership in appended and mapped lists (`mem_append_cases`, `preimage_of_mem_map`), proved by recursion where Lean's own versions use `propext`.
- [Becoming.ClassifiedBeliefProcesses](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/ClassifiedBeliefProcesses.lean) (no premise): a narrowed belief whose inhabitation is decided is empty or inhabited (`after_empty_or_inhabited`); where every narrowed belief's inhabitation is decided, every belief is classified at every depth, and bounded sufficiency is the implication form (`classified_of_decidable`, `sufficiency_iff_implication_of_decidable`).
- [Becoming.MoveOutcomeProcesses](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/MoveOutcomeProcesses.lean) (no premise): one process covers another at a configuration exactly where every target the first completes in one step the second also completes in one step (`covers_iff`); covering is a preorder kept along revision (`covers_along`); covering fails exactly where some target is not carried (`not_covers_iff`), and with double-negation elimination as a hypothesis the lost target can be named. Four processes on two configurations witness that more possible outcomes need not cover, that covering can remove a possibility, and that a summary can hide a loss.
- [Becoming.RenewalFlow](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/RenewalFlow.lean) (no premise): on webs, neither a formation's trace nor the focal formation's own trace decides the class of a run: formations each within renewal consume jointly, upgrading consumes, and the seasonal web neither renews nor consumes (`SupportGatedWebs.formation_does_not_decide` and five more).
- [Becoming.EdgeClosure](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/EdgeClosure.lean) (no premise): what a single-label process stepping along edges reaches from a seed contains the seed, is closed under the edges and lies within every property kept along them (`Closure.base`, `Closure.step`, `Closure.induction`); the core's closure of a relation is this closure from a seed (`withinClosure_iff_closure`), derived from a process's reach being the closure of its steps (`reach_iff_withinClosure`, `Becoming.Frame`).
- [Becoming.SeedReach](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/SeedReach.lean) (no premise): reach from a seed is the least invariant holding at the seed; a configuration is separated from the seed by some invariant exactly where it is not reached; "every process reaches every configuration not so separated" is exactly double-negation elimination (`fromSeed_of_not_separated_iff_dne`), so asserting reach needs a path or a decision of reach.
- [LivingOrganizationCorrespondence](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/universal-ethics/LivingOrganizationCorrespondence.lean) (periphery instance, Lean 4.30.0-rc2, its own checker, standard axioms only): with the recipe entering the pool as the gate (`entryGate`) and the other steps reflecting the pool's presence (`presence_reflected`), `trace_pool_origin` is given by the core's `Gated.origin` without deciding the pool (`trace_pool_origin_by_core`), the pool is present after the entry step (`entry_then_present`), and an empty pool stays empty where every admitted recipe closes (`empty_pool_preserved_by_core`).
<!-- zh-table:end -->
